###############################################################################
# Disease events: who is to blame for an epidemic, the dancing plague, the
# Great Plague news, symptoms and diseases, treatment, court physicians,
# hospitals and seclusion
#
# Part 1: Blame for the epidemic (namespace blame)
# Faction: Generic
# Serves: any ruler of a settled people whose capital the sickness has
# emptied; not greenskins, Skaven, ogres, Lizardmen or the dead.
#
# Also in this file (each part keeps its own header and namespace):
#   Part 2: The dancing plague (namespace dancing)
#   Part 3: The Great Plague (namespace plague)
#   Part 4: Symptoms and diseases (namespace symptom)
#   Part 5: Treatment and recovery (namespace treatment)
#   Part 6: Finding a court physician (namespace physician)
#   Part 7: Hospitals (namespace hospital)
#   Part 8: Seclusion (namespace seclusion)
#
# When an epidemic has emptied a ruler's capital, the people look for someone
# to blame. One of four chains runs, once in a ruler's life:
#   blame.1          Starter (yearly random list blame_epidemic)
#   blame.10-20      The cats
#   blame.30-35      The Strigany
#   blame.40-58      A witch hunt, led by a court witch hunter, a witch
#                    hunter of the court or the court chaplain
#   blame.60-68      The impious ruler (restored; vanilla's was Hindu only
#                    and commented out)
#
# Was events/rip_epidemic_events.txt (RIP.13000-13508). blame.N for the
# cats is old RIP.130NN + 9, the Strigany are RIP.13400-13405, the witch
# hunt RIP.13198-13235 and the impious ruler RIP.13500-13508, in order.
###############################################################################

namespace = blame

# blame.1 The people look for someone to blame
# A ruler whose capital the epidemic has depopulated faces one of four blame
# chains: the cats, the Strigany (only with Strigany in the realm), a witch
# hunt (needs a chaplain or a witch hunter at court) or the ruler's own
# impiety (temple faiths with no temple in the capital). Once in a life.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired from on_yearly_pulse, random list
# blame_epidemic.
character_event = {
	id = blame.1
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_pulse (blame_epidemic)

	only_playable = yes
	only_independent = yes

	trigger = {
		host = { character = ROOT }
		NOT = { has_character_flag = epidemic_blame_game }
		blame_epidemic_ruler_trigger = yes
		capital_scope = {
			OR = {
				has_province_modifier = depopulated_1
				has_province_modifier = depopulated_2
				has_province_modifier = depopulated_3
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = epidemic_blame_game
		random_list = {
			10 = { # The cats
				character_event = { id = blame.10 }
			}
			10 = { # The Strigany
				modifier = { factor = 0 blame_strigany_present_trigger = no }
				character_event = { id = blame.30 }
			}
			10 = { # A witch hunt
				modifier = { factor = 0 blame_witch_hunt_possible_trigger = no }
				modifier = { factor = 2 any_courtier = { whunter_chapter_member_trigger = yes } }
				character_event = { id = blame.40 }
			}
			10 = { # The impious ruler
				modifier = { factor = 0 blame_impious_ruler_possible_trigger = no }
				character_event = { id = blame.60 }
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################################
# The cats (blame.10-20)
###############################################################################

# blame.10 The cats are blamed
# Since the epidemic came the town is full of cats, and the people are sure
# they spread it. The ruler can pay to have some examined.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = blame.10
	desc = EVTDESCblame10
	picture = GFX_evt_depopulated_town
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		capital_scope = { save_event_target_as = capital }
	}

	option = { # Gather some cats
		name = EVTOPTAblame10
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAblame10_TT }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion } scaled_wealth = -0.4 }
		else = { scaled_wealth = -0.1 }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			character_event = { id = blame.11 days = 20 random = 5 }
			character_event = { id = blame.12 days = 90 random = 30 }
		}
	}
	option = { # The cats are not to blame
		name = EVTOPTBblame10
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = blame.12 days = 90 random = 30 } }
	}
}

# blame.11 The cats seem healthy
# The examiner finds the cats calm and mostly healthy. Keeping some to watch
# may win the ruler a pet.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = blame.11
	desc = EVTDESCblame11
	picture = GFX_evt_cat
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = cats_examined
		if = {
			limit = { any_courtier_or_vassal = { has_minor_title = title_court_physician } }
			random_courtier_or_vassal = {
				limit = { has_minor_title = title_court_physician }
				save_event_target_as = cat_examiner
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { job_marshal = { always = yes } }
			job_marshal = { save_event_target_as = cat_examiner }
		}
		else = { random_courtier = { save_event_target_as = cat_examiner } }
	}

	option = { # Let them go
		name = EVTOPTAblame11
	}
	option = { # Keep some for observation
		name = EVTOPTBblame11
		scaled_wealth = -0.2
		set_character_flag = cats_observed
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				70 = { character_event = { id = blame.15 days = 30 random = 10 } }
				30 = { }
			}
		}
	}
}

# blame.12 A mob against the cats
# The complaints will not end: a mob says the cats bring bad luck. The ruler
# can make an example of one, talk the mob down, explain, kill every cat or
# send them home.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = blame.12
	desc = EVTDESCblame12
	picture = GFX_evt_peasants
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Make an example of the "source"
		name = EVTOPTAblame12
		tooltip_info = intrigue
		trigger = { has_character_flag = cats_observed intrigue = 15 }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAblame12_TT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				20 = { character_event = { id = blame.20 days = 50 random = 20 } }
				80 = { character_event = { id = blame.19 days = 50 random = 20 } }
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # The cats are not the cause
		name = EVTOPTBblame12
		tooltip_info = diplomacy
		trigger = { diplomacy = 15 has_character_flag = cats_examined }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBblame12_TT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				20 = { character_event = { id = blame.20 days = 50 random = 20 } }
				80 = { character_event = { id = blame.19 days = 50 random = 20 } }
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # The cats seem mostly healthy
		name = EVTOPTCblame12
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = cats_examined
			NOR = { diplomacy = 15 AND = { intrigue = 15 has_character_flag = cats_observed } }
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTCblame12_TT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = { character_event = { id = blame.20 days = 50 random = 20 } }
				50 = { character_event = { id = blame.19 days = 50 random = 20 } }
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # Get rid of all cats
		name = EVTOPTDblame12
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = pet_cat } remove_character_modifier = pet_cat }
		capital_scope = { add_province_modifier = { name = no_more_cats years = 10 } }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				30 = { }
				70 = {
					random_courtier = {
						limit = {
							is_adult = yes
							NOR = { is_rival = ROOT is_friend = ROOT owes_favor_to = ROOT }
						}
						character_event = { id = blame.16 days = 4 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # This is absurd, go home
		name = EVTOPTEblame12
		capital_scope = { add_province_modifier = { name = epidemic_blame_1 years = 10 } }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				20 = { character_event = { id = blame.19 days = 50 random = 20 } }
				80 = { character_event = { id = blame.20 days = 50 random = 20 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

# blame.13 A courtier wants to keep their cats
# A courtier who loves their cats asks for an exception to the cull.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM the courtier (worried_courtier).
character_event = {
	id = blame.13
	desc = EVTDESCblame13
	picture = GFX_evt_suspicious_noble
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Even your cats
		name = EVTOPTAblame13
		event_target:worried_courtier = {
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_hate years = 5 }
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = blame.17 } }
		}
	}
	option = { # Keep it secret, but you owe me
		name = EVTOPTBblame13
		add_favor = event_target:worried_courtier
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:worried_courtier = { character_event = { id = blame.18 } }
			random_list = {
				50 = { character_event = { id = blame.19 days = 20 random = 20 } } # The talk dies down
				50 = { character_event = { id = blame.14 days = 20 random = 20 } } # The people find out
			}
		}
	}
}

	}
}

# blame.15 A new pet
# One of the examined cats takes a liking to the ruler.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = blame.15
	desc = EVTDESCblame15
	picture = GFX_evt_cat
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			10 = { set_character_flag = cat_name_1 }
			10 = { set_character_flag = cat_name_2 }
			10 = { set_character_flag = cat_name_3 }
		}
	}

	option = { # Keep her
		name = { text = EVTOPTAblame15 trigger = { has_character_flag = cat_name_1 } }
		name = { text = EVTOPTBblame15 trigger = { has_character_flag = cat_name_2 } }
		name = { text = EVTOPTCblame15 trigger = { has_character_flag = cat_name_3 } }
		clr_character_flag = cat_name_1
		clr_character_flag = cat_name_2
		clr_character_flag = cat_name_3
		add_cat_effect = yes
	}
	option = { # The people would not approve
		name = EVTOPTDblame15
		clr_character_flag = cat_name_1
		clr_character_flag = cat_name_2
		clr_character_flag = cat_name_3
	}
}

# blame.16 The liege wants the cats dead
# A courtier who loves cats hears of the cull and may plead for them.
# Scope: ROOT is the courtier, FROM the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = blame.16
	desc = EVTDESCblame16
	picture = GFX_evt_quarrel
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = worried_courtier
	}

	option = { # Ask for an exception
		name = EVTOPTAblame16
		trigger = { NOT = { owes_favor_to = FROM } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = EVTOPTAblame16_TT
			hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = blame.13 } } }
		}
	}
	option = { # I cannot argue with my liege
		name = EVTOPTBblame16
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
		prestige = -200
	}
}

# blame.20 The mob turns angry
# The people want every cat gone and their taxes back.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = blame.20
	desc = EVTDESCblame20
	picture = GFX_evt_peasants
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Meet their demands
		name = EVTOPTAblame20
		scaled_wealth = -1
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = pet_cat } remove_character_modifier = pet_cat }
		capital_scope = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_province_modifier = no_more_cats } }
				add_province_modifier = { name = no_more_cats years = 10 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_province_modifier = epidemic_blame_1 }
				remove_province_modifier = epidemic_blame_1
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_province_modifier = epidemic_blame_2 }
				remove_province_modifier = epidemic_blame_2
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # You have no power here
		name = EVTOPTBblame20
		capital_scope = {
			add_province_modifier = { name = epidemic_blame_2 years = 10 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_province_modifier = epidemic_blame_1 }
				remove_province_modifier = epidemic_blame_1
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################################
# The Strigany (blame.30-35)
# The people blame the Strigany wanderers. The head of the faith may weigh
# in. Expelling them sends the caravans and Strigany courtiers away, as the
# expel_strigany decision does; protecting them may mean sending troops to
# stop a purge.
###############################################################################

# blame.30 The Strigany are blamed
# The people say the Strigany wanderers brought the sickness.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = blame.30
	desc = EVTDESCblame30
	picture = GFX_evt_wandering_strigany
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Something must be done
		name = EVTOPTAblame30
		any_realm_province = {
			limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
			add_province_modifier = { name = epidemic_blame_1 duration = 3650 }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { rightful_religious_head_scope = { NOT = { character = ROOT } } }
				rightful_religious_head_scope = { character_event = { id = blame.31 days = 30 } }
			}
		}
		character_event = { id = blame.32 days = 60 }
	}
	option = { # I don't really care
		name = EVTOPTBblame30
		any_realm_province = {
			limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
			add_province_modifier = { name = epidemic_blame_2 duration = 3650 }
		}
	}
}

# blame.31 The head of the faith weighs in
# The head of the faith decides whether the Strigany are to blame.
# Scope: ROOT is the head of the faith, FROM the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = blame.31
	desc = EVTDESCblame31
	picture = GFX_evt_end_of_days
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Leave the Strigany be
		name = EVTOPTAblame31
		FROM = { letter_event = { id = blame.34 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 OR = { trait = zealous trait = cynical } }
			modifier = { factor = 2 is_virtous = yes }
		}
	}
	option = { # The Strigany did it
		name = EVTOPTBblame31
		FROM = { letter_event = { id = blame.35 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 modifier = { factor = 2 is_wicked = yes } }
	}
}

# blame.32 The ruler decides
# Expelling the Strigany pays, but costs prestige and makes the ruler
# arbitrary; sparing them may lead to a purge (blame.33).
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = blame.32
	picture = GFX_evt_suspicious_noble
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	desc = { text = EVTDESCblame32 trigger = { has_character_flag = religion_head_says_stop } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCblame32b trigger = { has_character_flag = religion_head_says_go } }
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCblame32c
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				has_character_flag = religion_head_says_stop
				has_character_flag = religion_head_says_go
			}
		}
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # The Strigany are not to blame
		name = EVTOPTAblame32
		clr_character_flag = religion_head_says_stop
		clr_character_flag = religion_head_says_go
		character_event = { id = blame.33 days = 30 }
	}
	option = { # Expel them
		name = EVTOPTBblame32
		clr_character_flag = religion_head_says_stop
		clr_character_flag = religion_head_says_go
		any_realm_province = {
			limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
			remove_province_modifier = epidemic_blame_1
		}
		prestige = -100
		scaled_wealth = 2
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = arbitrary } } add_trait = arbitrary }
		add_character_modifier = { name = expelled_strigany duration = 3650 inherit = yes }
		custom_tooltip = { text = blame_strigany_expelled_tt }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			expel_strigany_effect = yes
			character_event = { id = SoA.105 } # Strigany Expelled notice
		}
		any_friend = { limit = { culture_group = strigany_group } remove_friend = ROOT }
	}
}

# blame.33 A purge is coming
# Despite the ruler's word, a mob means to purge the Strigany in one of the
# ruler's cities.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = blame.33
	picture = GFX_evt_impalement
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	desc = { text = EVTDESCblame33 trigger = { has_character_flag = pogrom_target_selected } }
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCblame33b
		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = pogrom_target_selected } }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_demesne_province = {
					NOT = { has_province_flag = pogrom_target }
					any_province_holding = { holding_type = city }
				}
			}
			random_demesne_province = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_province_flag = pogrom_target }
					any_province_holding = { holding_type = city }
				}
				random_province_holding = {
					limit = { holding_type = city }
					save_event_target_as = pogrom_city_target
				}
				set_province_flag = pogrom_target
				save_event_target_as = pogrom_province_target
			}
			set_character_flag = pogrom_target_selected
		}
	}

	option = { # Send troops to stop them
		name = EVTOPTAblame33
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = pogrom_target_selected }
			event_target:pogrom_province_target = {
				remove_province_modifier = epidemic_blame_1
				add_province_modifier = { name = epidemic_blame_2 duration = 3650 }
			}
		}
		clr_character_flag = pogrom_target_selected
		clear_event_target = pogrom_city_target
	}
	option = { # Let them do it
		name = EVTOPTBblame33
		prestige = -100
		clr_character_flag = pogrom_target_selected
		clear_event_target = pogrom_city_target
	}
}

###############################################################################
# The witch hunt (blame.40-58)
# A court witch hunter, any witch hunter at court, or else the court
# chaplain tells the ruler that servants of the Dark Gods brought the
# sickness. For three hundred days the court points fingers; then the
# accused are judged. Real Chaos cultists, mutants and unlicensed wizards
# are the first suspects, and unmasking one is a true success.
###############################################################################

# blame.40 Who leads the witch hunt
# Picks the investigator and has them bring the news.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = blame.40
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_courtier_or_vassal = { whunter_court_hunter_title_trigger = yes prisoner = no }
			}
			random_courtier_or_vassal = {
				limit = { whunter_court_hunter_title_trigger = yes prisoner = no }
				character_event = { id = blame.41 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_courtier = { whunter_chapter_member_trigger = yes prisoner = no is_adult = yes }
			}
			random_courtier = {
				limit = { whunter_chapter_member_trigger = yes prisoner = no is_adult = yes }
				character_event = { id = blame.41 }
			}
		}
		else = { job_spiritual = { character_event = { id = blame.41 } } }
	}
}

# blame.41 Relay to the ruler
# Scope: ROOT is the investigator, FROM the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = blame.41
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { character_event = { id = blame.42 } }
	}
}

# blame.42 Servants of the Dark Gods
# The investigator says worshippers of the Dark Gods are behind the
# epidemic. Starting the hunt angers the peasants until it ends; refusing
# angers them more.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM the investigator.
character_event = {
	id = blame.42
	picture = GFX_evt_witch_hunters
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCblame42
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				OR = {
					whunter_court_hunter_title_trigger = yes
					whunter_chapter_member_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCblame42b
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				NOR = {
					whunter_court_hunter_title_trigger = yes
					whunter_chapter_member_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { save_event_target_as = bg_witch_hunter }
	}

	option = { # Investigate
		name = EVTOPTAblame42
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAblame42_TT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			character_event = { id = blame.43 days = 30 }
			character_event = { id = blame.52 days = 300 } # The judgement
			set_character_flag = bg_witch_hunt
			set_variable = { which = bg_imprisoned_witches value = 0 }
			any_realm_province = {
				limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
				add_province_modifier = { name = peasant_unrest duration = 3650 }
			}
		}
		reverse_opinion = { modifier = opinion_took_my_advice who = FROM }
	}
	option = { # There are no such things
		name = EVTOPTBblame42
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBblame42_TT }
		reverse_opinion = { modifier = opinion_did_not_take_my_advice who = FROM }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_realm_province = {
				limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
				add_province_modifier = { name = peasant_crisis duration = 3650 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# blame.43 Witnesses come forward
# The ruler can set the investigator on the most likely witch, ask the
# court to watch, or accuse someone they dislike.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = blame.43
	desc = EVTDESCblame43
	picture = GFX_evt_shadowy_cabal
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = blame.51 } }
	}

	option = { # Find the most likely witch
		name = EVTOPTAblame43
		trigger = { event_target:bg_witch_hunter = { is_alive = yes prisoner = no } }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAblame43_TT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:bg_witch_hunter = {
				character_event = { id = blame.44 days = 100 random = 40 }
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # Warn the court
		name = EVTOPTBblame43
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBblame43_TT }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = blame.48 days = 80 random = 40 } }
	}
	option = { # I already have my suspicions
		name = EVTOPTCblame43
		trigger = {
			any_courtier = {
				OR = { is_rival = ROOT NOT = { reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 0 } } }
			}
			NOT = { trait = honest }
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTCblame43_TT }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = blame.50 days = 50 random = 30 } }
	}
	option = { # Stop wasting my time
		name = EVTOPTDblame43
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTDblame43_TT }
	}
}

# blame.44 The most likely witch
# The accuser (the investigator or a courtier from blame.51) picks a
# suspect: a real Chaos cultist first, then a mutant or an unlicensed
# wizard, a rival of the ruler, a madman, someone of another faith, a wicked
# courtier, or anyone at all.
# Scope: ROOT is the accuser, FROM the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = blame.44
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		prisoner = no
		event_target:bg_witch_hunter = { has_character_flag = bg_witch_hunt }
	}

	immediate = {
		# The investigator gives someone else a chance to accuse too
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { character = event_target:bg_witch_hunter } }
			event_target:bg_witch_hunter = { character_event = { id = blame.51 } }
		}
		FROM = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_courtier = {
						blame_witch_suspect_base_trigger = yes
						is_cultist_trigger = yes
					}
				}
				random_courtier = {
					limit = { blame_witch_suspect_base_trigger = yes is_cultist_trigger = yes }
					save_event_target_as = bg_likely_witch
					set_character_flag = bg_accused_witch
					set_character_flag = bg_cultist
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					any_courtier = {
						blame_witch_suspect_base_trigger = yes
						OR = { is_mutated_trigger = yes whunter_mage_suspect_trigger = yes }
					}
				}
				random_courtier = {
					limit = {
						blame_witch_suspect_base_trigger = yes
						OR = { is_mutated_trigger = yes whunter_mage_suspect_trigger = yes }
					}
					save_event_target_as = bg_likely_witch
					set_character_flag = bg_accused_witch
					set_character_flag = bg_tainted
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					any_courtier = { blame_witch_suspect_base_trigger = yes is_rival = ROOT }
				}
				random_courtier = {
					limit = { blame_witch_suspect_base_trigger = yes is_rival = ROOT }
					save_event_target_as = bg_likely_witch
					set_character_flag = bg_accused_witch
					random_list = {
						10 = { set_character_flag = bg_crazy }
						10 = { set_character_flag = bg_sinful }
						10 = { set_character_flag = bg_infidel }
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					any_courtier = {
						blame_witch_suspect_base_trigger = yes
						OR = { trait = possessed is_lunatic_trigger = yes }
					}
				}
				random_courtier = {
					limit = {
						blame_witch_suspect_base_trigger = yes
						OR = { trait = possessed is_lunatic_trigger = yes }
					}
					save_event_target_as = bg_likely_witch
					set_character_flag = bg_accused_witch
					set_character_flag = bg_crazy
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					any_courtier = {
						blame_witch_suspect_base_trigger = yes
						OR = { NOT = { religion = FROM } trait = mystic }
					}
				}
				random_courtier = {
					limit = {
						blame_witch_suspect_base_trigger = yes
						OR = { NOT = { religion = FROM } trait = mystic }
					}
					save_event_target_as = bg_likely_witch
					set_character_flag = bg_accused_witch
					set_character_flag = bg_infidel
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					any_courtier = { blame_witch_suspect_base_trigger = yes is_wicked = yes }
				}
				random_courtier = {
					limit = { blame_witch_suspect_base_trigger = yes is_wicked = yes }
					save_event_target_as = bg_likely_witch
					set_character_flag = bg_accused_witch
					set_character_flag = bg_sinful
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { any_courtier = { blame_witch_suspect_base_trigger = yes } }
				random_courtier = {
					limit = { blame_witch_suspect_base_trigger = yes }
					save_event_target_as = bg_likely_witch
					set_character_flag = bg_accused_witch
					random_list = {
						10 = { set_character_flag = bg_crazy }
						10 = { set_character_flag = bg_sinful }
						10 = { set_character_flag = bg_infidel }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:bg_likely_witch = { always = yes } }
			FROM = { character_event = { id = blame.45 } }
		}
	}
}

# blame.45 The accusation
# The accuser names the most likely witch and the ruler may imprison them.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM the accuser.
character_event = {
	id = blame.45
	picture = GFX_evt_suspicious_noble
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCblame45_cultist
		trigger = { event_target:bg_likely_witch = { has_character_flag = bg_cultist } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCblame45_tainted
		trigger = { event_target:bg_likely_witch = { has_character_flag = bg_tainted } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCblame45_crazy_hunter
		trigger = {
			FROM = { character = event_target:bg_witch_hunter }
			event_target:bg_likely_witch = {
				has_character_flag = bg_crazy
				NOR = { has_character_flag = bg_cultist has_character_flag = bg_tainted }
			}
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCblame45_sinful_hunter
		trigger = {
			FROM = { character = event_target:bg_witch_hunter }
			event_target:bg_likely_witch = {
				has_character_flag = bg_sinful
				NOR = { has_character_flag = bg_cultist has_character_flag = bg_tainted }
			}
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCblame45_infidel_hunter
		trigger = {
			FROM = { character = event_target:bg_witch_hunter }
			event_target:bg_likely_witch = {
				has_character_flag = bg_infidel
				NOR = { has_character_flag = bg_cultist has_character_flag = bg_tainted }
			}
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCblame45_crazy_courtier
		trigger = {
			FROM = { NOT = { character = event_target:bg_witch_hunter } }
			event_target:bg_likely_witch = {
				has_character_flag = bg_crazy
				NOR = { has_character_flag = bg_cultist has_character_flag = bg_tainted }
			}
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCblame45_sinful_courtier
		trigger = {
			FROM = { NOT = { character = event_target:bg_witch_hunter } }
			event_target:bg_likely_witch = {
				has_character_flag = bg_sinful
				NOR = { has_character_flag = bg_cultist has_character_flag = bg_tainted }
			}
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCblame45_infidel_courtier
		trigger = {
			FROM = { NOT = { character = event_target:bg_witch_hunter } }
			event_target:bg_likely_witch = {
				has_character_flag = bg_infidel
				NOR = { has_character_flag = bg_cultist has_character_flag = bg_tainted }
			}
		}
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		event_target:bg_witch_hunter = { has_character_flag = bg_witch_hunt }
		FROM = { prisoner = no }
		event_target:bg_likely_witch = { prisoner = no }
	}

	option = { # Imprison the likely witch
		name = EVTOPTAblame45
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAblame45_TT }
		reverse_opinion = { modifier = opinion_took_my_advice who = FROM }
		event_target:bg_likely_witch = {
			imprison = ROOT
			set_character_flag = bg_imprisoned_as_witch
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_realm_province = {
				limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
				add_province_modifier = { name = peasant_pleased_witch_hunt duration = 60 }
			}
			change_variable = { which = bg_imprisoned_witches value = 1 }
		}
	}
	option = { # Preposterous
		name = EVTOPTBblame45
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBblame45_TT }
		reverse_opinion = { modifier = opinion_did_not_take_my_advice who = FROM }
	}
}

# blame.46 Two rivals accuse each other
# Two rivals each say the other is a witch; the ruler picks one, both or neither.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = blame.46
	desc = EVTDESCblame46
	picture = GFX_evt_suspicious_noble
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		event_target:bg_rival_1 = { prisoner = no }
		event_target:bg_rival_2 = { prisoner = no }
	}

	option = { # Imprison the first
		name = EVTOPTAblame46
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAblame46_TT }
		event_target:bg_rival_1 = { imprison = ROOT set_character_flag = bg_imprisoned_as_witch }
		event_target:bg_rival_2 = { opinion = { modifier = opinion_took_my_advice who = ROOT } }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_realm_province = {
				limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
				add_province_modifier = { name = peasant_pleased_witch_hunt duration = 60 }
			}
			change_variable = { which = bg_imprisoned_witches value = 1 }
		}
	}
	option = { # Imprison the second
		name = EVTOPTBblame46
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBblame46_TT }
		event_target:bg_rival_2 = { imprison = ROOT set_character_flag = bg_imprisoned_as_witch }
		event_target:bg_rival_1 = { opinion = { modifier = opinion_took_my_advice who = ROOT } }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_realm_province = {
				limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
				add_province_modifier = { name = peasant_pleased_witch_hunt duration = 60 }
			}
			change_variable = { which = bg_imprisoned_witches value = 1 }
		}
	}
	option = { # Imprison both
		name = EVTOPTCblame46
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTCblame46_TT }
		event_target:bg_rival_1 = { imprison = ROOT set_character_flag = bg_imprisoned_as_witch }
		event_target:bg_rival_2 = { imprison = ROOT set_character_flag = bg_imprisoned_as_witch }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_realm_province = {
				limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
				add_province_modifier = { name = peasant_pleased_witch_hunt duration = 60 }
			}
			change_variable = { which = bg_imprisoned_witches value = 2 }
		}
	}
	option = { # Get out of my sight
		name = EVTOPTDblame46
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTDblame46_TT }
		event_target:bg_rival_1 = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_did_not_take_my_advice who = ROOT }
		}
		event_target:bg_rival_2 = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_did_not_take_my_advice who = ROOT }
		}
	}
}

# blame.47 Relay for the jealous lover
# Scope: ROOT is the jealous lover, FROM the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = blame.47
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { character_event = { id = blame.49 } }
	}
}

# blame.48 The court points fingers
# Either two courtiers accuse each other, or a married courtier's new lover
# accuses the courtier's spouse.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = blame.48
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			50 = { # Two rivals accuse each other
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					NOT = {
						any_courtier = {
							count = 2
							is_adult = yes
							prisoner = no
							ai = yes
							NOT = { has_character_flag = bg_accused_witch }
						}
					}
				}
				random_courtier = {
					limit = {
						is_adult = yes
						prisoner = no
						ai = yes
						NOT = { has_character_flag = bg_accused_witch }
					}
					save_event_target_as = bg_rival_1
					set_character_flag = bg_accused_witch
				}
				random_courtier = {
					limit = {
						is_adult = yes
						prisoner = no
						ai = yes
						NOT = { character = event_target:bg_rival_1 }
						NOT = { has_character_flag = bg_accused_witch }
					}
					save_event_target_as = bg_rival_2
					set_character_flag = bg_accused_witch
					add_rival = event_target:bg_rival_1
				}
				character_event = { id = blame.46 }
			}
			50 = { # A jealous lover
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					NOT = {
						any_courtier = {
							has_lover = no
							is_married = yes
							is_adult = yes
							any_spouse = {
								NOT = { character = ROOT }
								ai = yes
								prisoner = no
								NOT = { has_character_flag = bg_accused_witch }
							}
						}
					}
				}
				random_courtier = {
					limit = {
						has_lover = no
						is_married = yes
						is_adult = yes
						any_spouse = {
							NOT = { character = ROOT }
							ai = yes
							prisoner = no
							NOT = { has_character_flag = bg_accused_witch }
						}
					}
					save_event_target_as = bg_lover
					random_spouse = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { character = ROOT }
							ai = yes
							prisoner = no
							NOT = { has_character_flag = bg_accused_witch }
						}
						save_event_target_as = bg_spouse
						set_character_flag = bg_accused_witch
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { event_target:bg_lover = { prefers_men_trigger = yes } }
					create_character = {
						female = no
						random_traits = yes
						dynasty = random
						culture = event_target:bg_lover
						religion = event_target:bg_lover
					}
				}
				else = {
					create_character = {
						female = yes
						random_traits = yes
						dynasty = random
						culture = event_target:bg_lover
						religion = event_target:bg_lover
					}
				}
				new_character = {
					character_event = { id = aiclean.31 } # Racial trait
					remove_trait = chaste
					remove_trait = kind
					add_trait = lustful
					add_trait = envious
					save_event_target_as = bg_jealous_lover
					if = { limit = { is_no_females_race = no event_target:bg_lover = { is_no_females_race = no } } add_lover = event_target:bg_lover }
					character_event = { id = blame.47 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# blame.50 Whom to accuse
# The ruler names up to three courtiers they dislike.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = blame.50
	desc = EVTDESCblame50
	picture = GFX_evt_suspicious_noble
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = { blame_disliked_courtier_trigger = yes }
			save_event_target_as = bg_dislike_1
		}
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				blame_disliked_courtier_trigger = yes
				NOT = { character = event_target:bg_dislike_1 }
			}
			save_event_target_as = bg_dislike_2
		}
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				blame_disliked_courtier_trigger = yes
				NOT = { character = event_target:bg_dislike_1 }
				NOT = { character = event_target:bg_dislike_2 }
			}
			save_event_target_as = bg_dislike_3
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAblame50
		trigger = { event_target:bg_dislike_1 = { always = yes } }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAblame50_TT }
		event_target:bg_dislike_1 = {
			imprison = ROOT
			set_character_flag = bg_imprisoned_as_witch
			set_character_flag = bg_accused_witch
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_realm_province = {
				limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
				add_province_modifier = { name = peasant_pleased_witch_hunt duration = 60 }
			}
			change_variable = { which = bg_imprisoned_witches value = 1 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBblame50
		trigger = { event_target:bg_dislike_2 = { always = yes } }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBblame50_TT }
		event_target:bg_dislike_2 = {
			imprison = ROOT
			set_character_flag = bg_imprisoned_as_witch
			set_character_flag = bg_accused_witch
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_realm_province = {
				limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
				add_province_modifier = { name = peasant_pleased_witch_hunt duration = 60 }
			}
			change_variable = { which = bg_imprisoned_witches value = 1 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCblame50
		trigger = { event_target:bg_dislike_3 = { always = yes } }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTCblame50_TT }
		event_target:bg_dislike_3 = {
			imprison = ROOT
			set_character_flag = bg_imprisoned_as_witch
			set_character_flag = bg_accused_witch
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_realm_province = {
				limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
				add_province_modifier = { name = peasant_pleased_witch_hunt duration = 60 }
			}
			change_variable = { which = bg_imprisoned_witches value = 1 }
		}
	}
	option = { # Accuse no one
		name = EVTOPTDblame50
	}
}

# blame.51 A courtier may point out someone
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = blame.51
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = { prisoner = no age = 12 NOT = { character = event_target:bg_witch_hunter } }
			character_event = { id = blame.44 days = 50 random = 60 }
		}
	}
}

# blame.52 The hunt ends
# After three hundred days the hunt is over: the accused are judged if
# there are any, else the ruler admits no witch was found.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = blame.52
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		clr_character_flag = bg_witch_hunt
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = bg_imprisoned_witches value = 1 } }
			character_event = { id = blame.53 }
		}
		else = { character_event = { id = blame.56 } }
		any_courtier = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = bg_accused_witch }
			clr_character_flag = bg_accused_witch
		}
	}
}

# blame.53 The judgement
# The ruler burns the accused, lets them rot, or hands them to the priests.
# Burning a real cultist pleases the faithful; Old World zealots may come to
# serve (once in a life, 10%).
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = blame.53
	desc = EVTDESCblame53
	picture = GFX_evt_witch_hunter_pyre
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			10 = { # A relative is killed
				modifier = {
					factor = 2
					check_variable = { which = bg_imprisoned_witches value = 2 }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 2
					check_variable = { which = bg_imprisoned_witches value = 4 }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 2
					check_variable = { which = bg_imprisoned_witches value = 6 }
				}
				modifier = { factor = 0 blame_relative_at_court_trigger = no }
				character_event = { id = blame.54 days = 20 random = 30 }
			}
			60 = { character_event = { id = blame.55 days = 20 random = 30 } }
		}
	}

	option = { # Light the pyres
		name = EVTOPTAblame53
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAblame53_TT }
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_courtier = {
					has_character_flag = bg_imprisoned_as_witch
					prisoner = yes
					has_character_flag = bg_cultist
				}
			}
			piety = 100
			custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAblame53_cultist_TT }
			if = {
				limit = {
					religion_group = old_world_group
					is_landed = yes
					NOT = { has_character_flag = blame_pyre_sworn_raised }
				}
				random = {
					chance = 10
					set_character_flag = blame_pyre_sworn_raised
					blame_pyre_sworn_effect = yes
				}
			}
		}
		any_courtier = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = bg_imprisoned_as_witch prisoner = yes }
			death = { death_reason = death_execution_burning killer = ROOT }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_realm_province = {
				limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
				remove_province_modifier = peasant_unrest
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # Let them rot
		name = EVTOPTBblame53
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBblame53_TT }
		any_courtier = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = bg_imprisoned_as_witch }
			clr_character_flag = bg_imprisoned_as_witch
			clr_character_flag = bg_cultist
			clr_character_flag = bg_tainted
		}
	}
	option = { # Let the priests deal with them
		name = EVTOPTCblame53
		any_courtier = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = bg_imprisoned_as_witch prisoner = yes }
			prisoner = no
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_grateful who = ROOT years = 10 }
			add_character_modifier = { name = renewed_faith duration = 120 }
			clr_character_flag = bg_imprisoned_as_witch
			clr_character_flag = bg_cultist
			clr_character_flag = bg_tainted
		}
	}
}

# blame.54 A relative is chosen
# The fear the hunt has stirred up claims one of the ruler's family.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = blame.54
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { any_spouse = { at_location = ROOT } }
			random_spouse = {
				limit = { at_location = ROOT }
				save_event_target_as = bg_relative_witch
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { any_child = { at_location = ROOT age = 4 } }
			random_child = {
				limit = { at_location = ROOT age = 4 }
				save_event_target_as = bg_relative_witch
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { mother = { at_location = ROOT } }
			mother = { save_event_target_as = bg_relative_witch }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { any_sibling = { at_location = ROOT } }
			random_sibling = {
				limit = { at_location = ROOT }
				save_event_target_as = bg_relative_witch
			}
		}
		event_target:bg_relative_witch = { character_event = { id = blame.57 } }
	}
}

		name = EVTOPTAblame55
	}
}

# blame.56 No witch was found
# A relative may still die at the hands of a frightened court.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = blame.56
	desc = EVTDESCblame56
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAblame56
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random = {
				chance = 30
				modifier = { factor = 0 blame_relative_at_court_trigger = no }
				character_event = { id = blame.54 days = 20 random = 30 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# blame.57 Relay for the murdered relative
# Scope: ROOT is the relative, FROM the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = blame.57
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { character_event = { id = blame.58 } }
	}
}

# blame.58 A relative found dead
# A relative of the ruler is murdered as a witch.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM the relative.
character_event = {
	id = blame.58
	picture = GFX_evt_death
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCblame58
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = bg_imprisoned_witches value = 1 } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCblame58b
		trigger = { NOT = { check_variable = { which = bg_imprisoned_witches value = 1 } } }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAblame58
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAblame58_TT }
		event_target:bg_relative_witch = { death = { death_reason = death_murder } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = depressed } } add_trait = depressed }
	}
}

###############################################################################
# The impious ruler (blame.60-68)
# The priests say the gods sent the sickness because the ruler is never seen
# at the temple. The ruler can go, send a double, raise a temple, or ignore
# them. Restored from vanilla's Hindu chain, which was commented out; it now
# serves the temple faiths of the Old World, Araby, Cathay, the Dawi, the
# elves and the halflings.
###############################################################################

# blame.60 The ruler is to blame
# The priests say the sickness is the ruler's fault for never going to the temple.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = blame.60
	desc = EVTDESCblame60
	picture = GFX_evt_cathedral
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		job_spymaster = { save_event_target_as = blame_spymaster }
		any_realm_province = {
			limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
			add_province_modifier = { name = epidemic_blame_1 duration = 3650 }
		}
	}

	option = { # They're right
		name = EVTOPTAblame60
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAblame60_TT }
		if = { limit = { trait = proud } remove_trait = proud }
		else_if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = humble } } add_trait = humble }
		piety = 20
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion }
			remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = blame.61 days = 15 } }
	}
	option = { # Send a double
		name = EVTOPTBblame60
		tooltip_info = intrigue
		trigger = {
			intrigue = 12
			NOT = { trait = deceitful }
			event_target:blame_spymaster = { always = yes }
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBblame60_TT }
		scaled_wealth = -0.3
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = blame.62 days = 15 } }
	}
	option = { # Send a double (deceitful)
		name = EVTOPTBblame60
		tooltip_info = deceitful
		trigger = { trait = deceitful event_target:blame_spymaster = { always = yes } }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBblame60_TT }
		scaled_wealth = -0.3
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = blame.62 days = 15 } }
	}
	option = { # Build a temple
		name = EVTOPTCblame60
		trigger = {
			capital_scope = { num_of_empty_holdings = 2 }
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion }
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTCblame60_TT }
		scaled_wealth = -0.8
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = blame.66 days = 15 } }
	}
	option = { # I do as I please
		name = EVTOPTDblame60
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTDblame60_TT }
	}
}

# blame.61 Back at the temple
# The ruler worships in the faith's own way and feels closer to their patron.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = blame.61
	picture = GFX_evt_priest_blessing
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	desc = { text = EVTDESCblame61_sigmar trigger = { has_character_modifier = patron_god_sigmar } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCblame61_ulric trigger = { has_character_modifier = patron_god_ulric } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCblame61_taal trigger = { has_character_modifier = patron_god_taal } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCblame61_morr trigger = { has_character_modifier = patron_god_morr } }
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCblame61_shallya
		trigger = { has_character_modifier = patron_god_shallya }
	}
	desc = { text = EVTDESCblame61_verena trigger = { has_character_modifier = patron_god_verena } }
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCblame61_myrmidia
		trigger = { has_character_modifier = patron_god_myrmidia }
	}
	desc = { text = EVTDESCblame61_manann trigger = { has_character_modifier = patron_god_manann } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCblame61_ranald trigger = { has_character_modifier = patron_god_ranald } }
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCblame61
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				has_character_modifier = patron_god_sigmar
				has_character_modifier = patron_god_ulric
				has_character_modifier = patron_god_taal
				has_character_modifier = patron_god_morr
				has_character_modifier = patron_god_shallya
				has_character_modifier = patron_god_verena
				has_character_modifier = patron_god_myrmidia
				has_character_modifier = patron_god_manann
				has_character_modifier = patron_god_ranald
			}
		}
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_from = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAblame61
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = devoted_worshiper } }
			add_character_modifier = { name = devoted_worshiper duration = 365 }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = blame.63 days = 65 random = 10 } }
	}
}

# blame.62 The double at the temple
# The spymaster's double calms the people for now. The ruse may hold or be
# found out, depending on the ruler's and the spymaster's intrigue.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = blame.62
	desc = EVTDESCblame62
	picture = GFX_evt_stone_church
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_from = yes

	option = { # Keep up the charade
		name = EVTOPTAblame62
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAblame62_TT }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = fake_worshiper } }
			add_character_modifier = { name = fake_worshiper duration = 365 }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = { # The people are fooled
					modifier = { factor = 1.1 intrigue = 10 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.1 intrigue = 12 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.1 intrigue = 15 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.1 intrigue = 17 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.1 intrigue = 20 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.1 event_target:blame_spymaster = { intrigue = 10 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.1 event_target:blame_spymaster = { intrigue = 12 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.1 event_target:blame_spymaster = { intrigue = 15 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.1 event_target:blame_spymaster = { intrigue = 17 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.1 event_target:blame_spymaster = { intrigue = 20 } }
					character_event = { id = blame.65 days = 50 }
				}
				50 = { # The people see through it
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 NOT = { intrigue = 10 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.1 event_target:blame_spymaster = { trait = deceitful } }
					modifier = {
						factor = 2
						event_target:blame_spymaster = {
							trait = deceitful
							NOT = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = 25 } }
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 2
						event_target:blame_spymaster = { is_dumb_trigger = yes }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 2
						event_target:blame_spymaster = { NOT = { intrigue = 6 } }
					}
					character_event = { id = blame.64 days = 70 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # Stop while I can
		name = EVTOPTBblame62
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBblame62_TT }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = fake_worshiper_felt_guilty } }
			add_character_modifier = { name = fake_worshiper_felt_guilty duration = 365 }
		}
	}
}

# blame.63 A child for the temple
# A grateful family offers their zealous child as the ruler's servant.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = blame.63
	desc = EVTDESCblame63
	picture = GFX_evt_educate_child
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_from = yes
	hide_new = yes

	trigger = {
		is_wicked = no
	}

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				create_character = {
					female = yes
					age = 12
					random_traits = yes
					dynasty = NONE
					culture = ROOT
					religion = ROOT
				}
			}
			50 = {
				create_character = {
					female = no
					age = 12
					random_traits = yes
					dynasty = NONE
					culture = ROOT
					religion = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		new_character = {
			character_event = { id = aiclean.31 } # Racial trait
			remove_trait = slothful
			remove_trait = idolizer
			remove_trait = dwarf
			remove_trait = harelip
			remove_trait = hunchback
			remove_trait = imbecile
			remove_trait = slow
			remove_trait = stutter
			remove_trait = cruel
			add_trait = quick
			add_trait = zealous
			save_event_target_as = zealous_child
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_loyal_servant who = ROOT years = 100 }
		}
	}

	option = { # Take the child in
		name = EVTOPTAblame63
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAblame63_TT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_realm_province = {
				limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
				remove_province_modifier = epidemic_blame_1
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # Send them to the temple school
		name = EVTOPTBblame63
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBblame63_TT }
		treasury = -20
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_nickname = nick_the_wise } } give_nickname = nick_the_wise }
		if = { limit = { trait = cruel } remove_trait = cruel }
		else_if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = kind } } add_trait = kind }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_realm_province = {
				limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
				remove_province_modifier = epidemic_blame_1
			}
			event_target:zealous_child = { leave_court_effect = yes }
		}
	}
}

# blame.64 The ruse is found out
# The people find out about the double and burn the temple.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = blame.64
	desc = EVTDESCblame64
	picture = GFX_evt_end_of_days
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Filthy peasants
		name = EVTOPTAblame64
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAblame64_TT }
		add_character_modifier = { name = failed_temple_ruse duration = 3650 }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_realm_province = {
				limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
				remove_province_modifier = epidemic_blame_1
				add_province_modifier = { name = epidemic_blame_2 duration = 3650 }
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # Blame the spymaster
		name = EVTOPTBblame64
		trigger = {
			event_target:blame_spymaster = { has_job_title = job_spymaster is_alive = yes }
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBblame64_TT }
		event_target:blame_spymaster = {
			remove_title = job_spymaster
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_fired_from_council who = ROOT years = 5 }
		}
	}
}

# blame.65 The people are fooled

		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_realm_province = {
				limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
				remove_province_modifier = epidemic_blame_1
			}
		}

		name = EVTOPTBblame65
		trigger = {
			event_target:blame_spymaster = { has_job_title = job_spymaster is_alive = yes }
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAblame65_TT }
		change_diplomacy = 1
		event_target:blame_spymaster = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_was_praised who = ROOT years = 5 }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_realm_province = {
				limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
				remove_province_modifier = epidemic_blame_1
			}
		}
	}
}

# blame.66 Vandals at the building site
# Two starving children are caught sabotaging the new temple. Mercy angers
# the workers; harshness hardens the ruler.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = blame.66
	desc = EVTDESCblame66
	picture = GFX_evt_child_bully
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_new = yes
	hide_from = yes

	immediate = {
		create_character = {
			female = yes
			age = 9
			random_traits = yes
			dynasty = NONE
			culture = ROOT
			religion = ROOT
		}
		new_character = {
			character_event = { id = aiclean.31 } # Racial trait
			remove_trait = slothful
			remove_trait = idolizer
			remove_trait = dwarf
			remove_trait = harelip
			remove_trait = hunchback
			remove_trait = imbecile
			remove_trait = slow
			remove_trait = ugly
			remove_trait = weak
			add_trait = twin
			add_trait = genius
			save_event_target_as = vagrant_child_1
		}
		create_character = {
			female = no
			age = 9
			random_traits = yes
			dynasty = NONE
			culture = ROOT
			religion = ROOT
		}
		new_character = {
			character_event = { id = aiclean.31 } # Racial trait
			remove_trait = slothful
			remove_trait = idolizer
			remove_trait = dwarf
			remove_trait = harelip
			remove_trait = hunchback
			remove_trait = imbecile
			remove_trait = slow
			remove_trait = ugly
			remove_trait = weak
			add_trait = twin
			add_trait = strong
			save_event_target_as = vagrant_child_2
		}
	}

	option = { # Off with their heads
		name = EVTOPTAblame66
		trigger = { NOT = { religion = shallyan } }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAblame66_TT }
		event_target:vagrant_child_1 = {
			death = { death_reason = death_execution_beheading killer = ROOT }
		}
		event_target:vagrant_child_2 = {
			death = { death_reason = death_execution_beheading killer = ROOT }
		}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = cruel } } add_trait = cruel }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = zealous } } add_trait = zealous }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_realm_province = {
				limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
				remove_province_modifier = epidemic_blame_1
			}
			random_list = {
				60 = { character_event = { id = blame.67 days = 75 } }
				40 = { character_event = { id = blame.68 days = 30 } }
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # Shelter, in prison
		name = EVTOPTBblame66
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBblame66_TT }
		event_target:vagrant_child_1 = { imprison = ROOT }
		event_target:vagrant_child_2 = { imprison = ROOT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = { character_event = { id = blame.67 days = 90 } }
				50 = { character_event = { id = blame.68 days = 30 } }
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # They are children
		name = EVTOPTCblame66
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTCblame66_TT }
		event_target:vagrant_child_1 = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_relieved who = ROOT years = 5 }
		}
		event_target:vagrant_child_2 = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_relieved who = ROOT years = 5 }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			set_character_flag = spared_urchins
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion }
				set_character_flag = exposed_to_outside
				event_target:vagrant_child_1 = {
					add_character_modifier = { name = in_seclusion duration = -1 }
				}
				event_target:vagrant_child_2 = {
					add_character_modifier = { name = in_seclusion duration = -1 }
				}
			}
			random_list = {
				40 = { character_event = { id = blame.67 days = 100 } }
				60 = { character_event = { id = blame.68 days = 30 } }
			}
			any_realm_province = {
				limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
				remove_province_modifier = epidemic_blame_1
				add_province_modifier = { name = epidemic_blame_2 duration = 3650 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# blame.67 The temple is finished
# The temple is built if the capital still has room; else the builders
# took the money for another site and the treasurer gets some of it back.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = blame.67
	picture = GFX_evt_cathedral
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	desc = { text = EVTDESCblame67 trigger = { capital_scope = { num_of_empty_holdings = 2 } } }
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCblame67b
		trigger = { NOT = { capital_scope = { num_of_empty_holdings = 2 } } }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_from = yes

	option = { # A temple was built
		name = EVTOPTAblame67
		trigger = { capital_scope = { num_of_empty_holdings = 2 } }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAblame67_TT }
		prestige = 100
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = devoted_worshiper } }
			add_character_modifier = { name = devoted_worshiper duration = 365 }
		}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { lifestyle_traits = 1 } } add_trait = architect }
		capital_scope = { build_holding = { type = temple } }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_realm_province = {
				limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
				remove_province_modifier = epidemic_blame_2
			}
			remove_character_modifier = fake_worshiper
			clr_character_flag = spared_urchins
		}
	}
	option = { # The temple could not be built
		name = EVTOPTBblame67
		trigger = { NOT = { capital_scope = { num_of_empty_holdings = 2 } } }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBblame67_TT }
		treasury = 200
		clr_character_flag = spared_urchins
	}
}

# blame.68 The building is cursed
# An accident at the site; the workers walk off. If the ruler spared the
# children, one of them is hurt.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = blame.68
	picture = GFX_evt_burning_house
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	desc = { text = EVTDESCblame68 trigger = { has_character_flag = spared_urchins } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCblame68b trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = spared_urchins } } }

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_from = yes

	option = { # That fool of a child
		name = EVTOPTAblame68
		trigger = { has_character_flag = spared_urchins }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAblame68_TT }
		event_target:vagrant_child_1 = { add_maimed_trait_effect = yes }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_realm_province = {
				limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
				remove_province_modifier = epidemic_blame_1
				add_province_modifier = { name = epidemic_blame_2 duration = 3650 }
			}
			clr_character_flag = spared_urchins
		}
	}
	option = { # What about my investment
		name = EVTOPTBblame68
		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = spared_urchins } }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAblame68_TT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_realm_province = {
				limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
				remove_province_modifier = epidemic_blame_1
				add_province_modifier = { name = epidemic_blame_2 duration = 3650 }
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Part 2: The dancing plague (namespace dancing)
# Faction: Generic
# Serves: anyone living where the dancing plague breaks out; followers of
# Slaanesh hear the Dark Prince in the music.
#
# A town starts to dance and cannot stop. The ruler may join in: a short
# dance wins the people's love, a long one ends in exhaustion or a broken
# leg. The disease itself (common/disease/00_disease.txt) picks who catches
# it and fires dancing.3.
#   dancing.1   Starter (on_outbreak)
#   dancing.2   The news
#   dancing.3   The music calls (from the disease)
#   dancing.4   The court physician says rest
#   dancing.5   Lord or Lady of the Dance
#   dancing.6   Caught by the dance
#   dancing.7   The dance ends
#   dancing.8   The dance gets worse
#   dancing.9   A broken leg
#
# Was events/rip_dancing_plague_events.txt (RIP.4000-4008, in order).
###############################################################################

namespace = dancing

# dancing.1 The dancing plague breaks out
# Tells the province's owner and every liege above them.
# Scope: ROOT is the province. Fired from on_outbreak.
province_event = {
	id = dancing.1
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_outbreak

	trigger = {
		disease = dancing_plague
	}

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = dancing_plague_outbreak_location
		owner = {
			narrative_event = { id = dancing.2 }
			any_liege = {
				limit = { NOT = { character = PREV } }
				narrative_event = { id = dancing.2 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# dancing.2 The dancing plague
# News of the dancing plague for the province's owner and lieges.
# Scope: ROOT is the owner or a liege.
narrative_event = {
	id = dancing.2
	title = EVTNAMEdancing2
	picture = GFX_evt_dancing
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_religion

	desc = EVTDESCdancing2

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAdancing2
	}
}

# dancing.3 The music calls
# The ruler hears the music from the window. Joining in sends them to the
# street (or to the court physician first); refusing may not be enough if
# they have already caught it. Followers of Slaanesh hear the Dark Prince
# in it and may dance for him.
# Scope: ROOT is the character. Fired by the dancing_plague disease, which
# sets got_dancing_plague if they caught it.
character_event = {
	id = dancing.3
	picture = GFX_evt_dancing
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	desc = { text = EVTDESCdancing3_slaanesh trigger = { follower_of_slaanesh = yes } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCdancing3 trigger = { follower_of_slaanesh = no } }

	is_triggered_only = yes # dancing_plague on_character_infection

	option = { # Join the dance
		name = EVTOPTAdancing3
		custom_tooltip = { text = join_in_dance }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_courtier_or_vassal = {
						liege = { character = ROOT }
						has_minor_title = title_court_physician
					}
				}
				character_event = { id = dancing.4 days = 5 random = 10 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = got_dancing_plague }
				character_event = { id = dancing.6 days = 5 random = 10 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { is_landed = yes }
				character_event = { id = dancing.5 days = 5 random = 10 }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 2 OR = { trait = hedonist trait = gluttonous } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.3 trait = temperate }
		}
	}
	option = { # Dance for the Prince of Excess
		name = EVTOPTCdancing3
		trigger = { follower_of_slaanesh = yes }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTCdancing3_TT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			set_character_flag = dancing_for_slaanesh
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = got_dancing_plague }
				character_event = { id = dancing.6 days = 5 random = 10 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { is_landed = yes }
				character_event = { id = dancing.5 days = 5 random = 10 }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 3 }
	}
	option = { # Rest instead
		name = EVTOPTBdancing3
		custom_tooltip = { text = refuse_to_join_in_dance }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = { limit = { has_character_flag = got_dancing_plague } dancing_resist_effect = yes }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
}

# dancing.4 The court physician says rest
# The physician warns the ruler they will drop from exhaustion. Listening
# may be enough; ignoring them sends the ruler to the street.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = dancing.4
	desc = EVTDESCdancing4
	picture = GFX_evt_poke_king_in_chest
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_courtier_or_vassal = {
			limit = { liege = { character = ROOT } has_minor_title = title_court_physician }
			save_event_target_as = target_physician
		}
	}

	option = { # As you say
		name = EVTOPTAdancing4
		custom_tooltip = { text = refuse_to_join_in_dance }
		event_target:target_physician = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_glad who = ROOT years = 5 }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = { limit = { has_character_flag = got_dancing_plague } dancing_resist_effect = yes }
		}
	}
	option = { # I must dance
		name = EVTOPTBdancing4
		custom_tooltip = { text = join_in_dance }
		event_target:target_physician = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_irritated who = ROOT years = 5 }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = got_dancing_plague }
				character_event = { id = dancing.6 days = 5 random = 10 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { is_landed = yes }
				character_event = { id = dancing.5 days = 5 random = 10 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# dancing.5 Lord or Lady of the Dance
# The ruler dances with the people for a few hours and is loved for it. A
# follower of Slaanesh who danced for the Dark Prince wins his favour too.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = dancing.5
	picture = GFX_evt_dancing
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCdancing5_slaanesh
		trigger = { has_character_flag = dancing_for_slaanesh }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCdancing5
		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = dancing_for_slaanesh } }
	}

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAdancing5
		prestige = 50
		if = { limit = { trait = stressed } remove_trait = stressed }
		if = { limit = { trait = depressed } remove_trait = depressed }
		if = {
			limit = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
			add_character_modifier = { modifier = dancing_queen years = 5 }
		}
		else = { add_character_modifier = { modifier = dancing_king years = 5 } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = dancing_for_slaanesh }
			clr_character_flag = dancing_for_slaanesh
			daemon_favour_slaanesh_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

# dancing.6 Caught by the dance
# The character cannot stop. They may recover after a few months, get
# worse, or dance until a leg breaks.
# Scope: ROOT is the character.
character_event = {
	id = dancing.6
	picture = GFX_evt_dancing
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCdancing6
		trigger = {
			NOR = { has_character_flag = forced_to_dance has_character_flag = dancing_for_slaanesh }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCdancing6b
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = forced_to_dance
			NOT = { has_character_flag = dancing_for_slaanesh }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCdancing6_slaanesh
		trigger = { has_character_flag = dancing_for_slaanesh }
	}

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAdancing6
		add_trait = dancing_plague
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = dancing_for_slaanesh }
			clr_character_flag = dancing_for_slaanesh
			daemon_favour_slaanesh_effect = yes
		}
		clr_character_flag = forced_to_dance
		nurgle_blessing_check_effect = yes
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				45 = { # Recover
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 is_strong_trigger = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = infirm }
					character_event = { id = dancing.7 days = 90 random = 20 }
				}
				40 = { # Worse
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = diligent }
					modifier = { factor = 50 is_unimportant_trigger = yes }
					character_event = { id = dancing.8 days = 10 random = 10 }
				}
				15 = { # A broken leg
					modifier = { factor = 0 trait = one_legged }
					modifier = { factor = 3 is_weak_trigger = yes }
					character_event = { id = dancing.9 days = 30 random = 10 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# dancing.7 The dance ends
# The character stops dancing and will not catch it again.
# Scope: ROOT is the character.
character_event = {
	id = dancing.7
	desc = EVTDESCdancing7
	picture = GFX_evt_dancing
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAdancing7
		dancing_end_effect = yes
	}
}

# dancing.8 The dance gets worse
# The character can no longer eat or sleep. It ends after a few months.
# Scope: ROOT is the character.
character_event = {
	id = dancing.8
	desc = EVTDESCdancing8
	picture = GFX_evt_dancing
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAdancing8
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = cant_stop_dancing duration = -1 }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = dancing.7 days = 90 random = 20 } }
	}
}

# dancing.9 A broken leg
# The character dances until a leg breaks and loses it.
# Scope: ROOT is the character.
character_event = {
	id = dancing.9
	desc = EVTDESCdancing9
	picture = GFX_evt_dancing
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAdancing9
		dancing_end_effect = yes
		add_trait = one_legged
		add_trait = severely_injured
		hidden_tooltip = {
			character_event = { id = maimed.3 } # Notify one-legged
			resolve_severely_injured_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Part 3: The Great Plague (namespace plague)
# Faction: Generic
# Serves: player rulers anywhere, who hear of a Great Plague (Nurgle's Rot
# or the Creeping Buboes) as it spreads; followers of Nurgle and the Skaven
# get their own reactions. Also the recovery of depopulated provinces.
#
# Restored from vanilla's Black Death news chain, which was commented out
# because the mod has no bubonic plague. It now follows the mod's two great
# plagues.
#   plague.1      Starter (on_outbreak)
#   plague.2      Word to the stricken realm
#   plague.3      Word to rulers far away
#   plague.4      Word to rulers nearby
#   plague.5      Word to the lieges of the stricken realm
#   plague.6      The plague reaches a border (disease on_province_infection)
#   plague.7      The plague approaches
#   plague.10     A depopulated province recovers (on_province_major_modifier)
#   plague.20     Yearly check for ravaged regions (on_yearly_pulse)
#   plague.21-32  A region is ravaged
#   plague.40     The plague is over (on_decade_pulse)
#
# Was events/rip_outbreak_events.txt: RIP.10000-10006 are plague.1-7 (the
# old RIP.10001 also picked the receivers, now plague.1 does), RIP.10030 is
# plague.10, RIP.10012 is plague.20, RIP.10015-10024 are plague.21-32 (the
# real-world regions became the mod's) and RIP.10031 is plague.40.
###############################################################################

namespace = plague

# plague.1 A Great Plague breaks out
# The first outbreak of Nurgle's Rot or the Creeping Buboes since the last
# one ended is told to the owner, every player ruler near and far, and the
# owner's lieges.
# Scope: ROOT is the province. Fired from on_outbreak.
province_event = {
	id = plague.1
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_outbreak

	trigger = {
		OR = { disease = nurgles_rot disease = creeping_buboes }
		NOT = { has_global_flag = great_plague_broadcasted }
	}

	immediate = {
		set_global_flag = great_plague_broadcasted
		if = {
			limit = { disease = nurgles_rot }
			set_global_flag = great_plague_rot
			clr_global_flag = great_plague_buboes
		}
		else = { set_global_flag = great_plague_buboes clr_global_flag = great_plague_rot }
		save_event_target_as = plague_outbreak_location
		owner = { save_event_target_as = plague_outbreak_ruler narrative_event = { id = plague.2 } }
		any_playable_ruler = {
			limit = { ai = no NOT = { character = event_target:plague_outbreak_ruler } }
			if = {
				limit = { is_liege_or_above = event_target:plague_outbreak_ruler }
				narrative_event = { id = plague.5 days = 2 random = 2 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					distance = { where = event_target:plague_outbreak_location value = 1000 }
				}
				narrative_event = { id = plague.3 days = 6 random = 3 }
			}
			else = { narrative_event = { id = plague.4 days = 3 random = 3 } }
		}
	}
}

# plague.2 A horrible affliction
# The owner of the first stricken province hears what the plague does.
# Scope: ROOT is the owner of the stricken province.
narrative_event = {
	id = plague.2
	title = EVTNAMEplague2
	picture = GFX_evt_plague_doctor
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_religion

	desc = { text = EVTDESCplague2_rot trigger = { has_global_flag = great_plague_rot } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCplague2 trigger = { NOT = { has_global_flag = great_plague_rot } } }

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = great_plague_close
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAplague2
		trigger = { plague_news_dark_trigger = no }
	}
	option = { # Nurgle's followers rejoice
		name = EVTOPTNURGLEplague
		trigger = { follower_of_nurgle = yes }
	}
	option = { # The Skaven see the work of the Clans Pestilens
		name = EVTOPTSKAVENplague
		trigger = { religion_group = horned_rat_god_group follower_of_nurgle = no }
	}
}

# plague.3 Word of a plague far away
# A distant player ruler hears rumours of a Great Plague.
# Scope: ROOT is a player ruler far from the outbreak.
narrative_event = {
	id = plague.3
	title = EVTNAMEplague3
	picture = GFX_evt_danse_macabre
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_religion

	desc = EVTDESCplague3

	is_triggered_only = yes
	only_playable = yes
	ai = no

	immediate = {
		chronicle = { entry = CHRONICLE_DISTANT_PLAGUE picture = GFX_evt_plague_spreading }
	}

	option = { # All those poor people
		name = EVTOPTAplague3
		trigger = { plague_news_dark_trigger = no }
	}
	option = { # We must prepare
		name = EVTOPTBplague3
		trigger = {
			plague_news_dark_trigger = no
			OR = { trait = erudite trait = shrewd trait = genius trait = quick trait = scholar }
		}
	}
	option = { # Let the heretics suffer
		name = EVTOPTCplague3
		trigger = { plague_news_dark_trigger = no OR = { trait = zealous trait = cruel } }
	}
	option = { # If I can help, I will
		name = EVTOPTDplague3
		trigger = { plague_news_dark_trigger = no OR = { trait = kind trait = charitable } }
	}
	option = { # Plague only takes the weak
		name = EVTOPTEplague3
		trigger = {
			plague_news_dark_trigger = no
			OR = { trait = strong trait = robust trait = brave }
			OR = { trait = stubborn trait = proud }
		}
	}
	option = { # I knew this would happen
		name = EVTOPTFplague3
		trigger = { plague_news_dark_trigger = no trait = paranoid }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTNURGLEplague
		trigger = { follower_of_nurgle = yes }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTSKAVENplague
		trigger = { religion_group = horned_rat_god_group follower_of_nurgle = no }
	}
}

# plague.4 Word of a plague nearby
# Refugees bring the tale of what the plague does.
# Scope: ROOT is a player ruler near the outbreak.
narrative_event = {
	id = plague.4

	picture = GFX_evt_bring_out_your_dead
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_religion

	desc = { text = EVTDESCplague4_rot trigger = { has_global_flag = great_plague_rot } }
