	is_triggered_only = yes
	only_playable = yes
	ai = no

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAplague4
		trigger = { plague_news_dark_trigger = no }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTNURGLEplague
		trigger = { follower_of_nurgle = yes }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTSKAVENplague
		trigger = { religion_group = horned_rat_god_group follower_of_nurgle = no }
	}
}

# plague.5 The plague in a vassal's lands
# A player liege hears the plague is in a vassal's lands.
# Scope: ROOT is a player liege of the stricken province's owner.
narrative_event = {
	id = plague.5
	title = EVTNAMEplague2
	picture = GFX_evt_bring_out_your_dead
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_religion

	desc = { text = EVTDESCplague2_rot trigger = { has_global_flag = great_plague_rot } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCplague2 trigger = { NOT = { has_global_flag = great_plague_rot } } }

	is_triggered_only = yes
	only_playable = yes
	ai = no

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = great_plague_close
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAplague2
		trigger = { plague_news_dark_trigger = no }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTNURGLEplague
		trigger = { follower_of_nurgle = yes }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTSKAVENplague
		trigger = { religion_group = horned_rat_god_group follower_of_nurgle = no }
	}
}

# plague.6 The plague reaches a border
# When the Great Plague infects a province next to a player's lands, the
# player (and their player lieges) are warned once.
# Scope: ROOT is the newly infected province. Fired from the
# on_province_infection of nurgles_rot and creeping_buboes.
province_event = {
	id = plague.6
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes # nurgles_rot / creeping_buboes on_province_infection

	has_global_flag = great_plague_broadcasted

	trigger = {
		any_neighbor_province = { plague_warning_owner_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = infected_province
		any_neighbor_province = {
			limit = { plague_warning_owner_trigger = yes }
			save_event_target_as = bordering_province
			owner = {
				if = {
					limit = { ai = no NOT = { has_character_flag = great_plague_close } }
					narrative_event = { id = plague.7 days = 10 }
				}
				any_liege = {
					limit = { ai = no NOT = { has_character_flag = great_plague_close } }
					narrative_event = { id = plague.7 days = 15 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# plague.7 The plague approaches
# Refugees flood across the border ahead of the plague.
# Scope: ROOT is the player ruler.
narrative_event = {
	id = plague.7
	title = EVTNAMEplague7
	picture = GFX_evt_bring_out_your_dead
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_religion

	desc = EVTDESCplague7

	is_triggered_only = yes
	ai = no

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_character_flag = great_plague_close }
	}

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = great_plague_close
		chronicle = { entry = CHRONICLE_BORDERING_PLAGUE picture = GFX_evt_plague_spreading }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAplague7
		trigger = { plague_news_dark_trigger = no }
	}
	option = { # Hospitals will be needed
		name = EVTOPTBplague7
		trigger = {
			plague_news_dark_trigger = no
			OR = { trait = kind trait = charitable trait = scholar trait = mystic }
		}
	}
	option = { # Heretics brought it
		name = EVTOPTCplague7
		trigger = { plague_news_dark_trigger = no OR = { trait = zealous trait = paranoid } }
	}
	option = { # Let it come
		name = EVTOPTDplague7
		trigger = {
			plague_news_dark_trigger = no
			OR = { trait = brave trait = strong trait = robust }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTNURGLEplague
		trigger = { follower_of_nurgle = yes }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTSKAVENplague
		trigger = { religion_group = horned_rat_god_group follower_of_nurgle = no }
	}
}

# plague.10 A depopulated province recovers
# A province with no sickness slowly fills again. Capitals, ports, trade
# posts, good stewards, many holdings and hospitals help; war, sieges,
# winter and deep depopulation hold it back.
# Scope: ROOT is the province. Fired from on_province_major_modifier.
province_event = {
	id = plague.10
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_province_major_modifier

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			has_province_modifier = depopulated_1
			has_province_modifier = depopulated_2
			has_province_modifier = depopulated_3
		}
		NOT = { has_disease = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			30 = { # Recovers a step
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 is_capital = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 1.3 port = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 1.3 has_trade_post = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 1.3 holder_scope = { stewardship = 20 } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 holder_scope = { stewardship = 15 } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.1 holder_scope = { stewardship = 10 } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.1 num_of_settlements = 2 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.1 num_of_settlements = 3 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.1 num_of_settlements = 4 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.1 num_of_settlements = 5 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.1 num_of_settlements = 6 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.1 num_of_settlements = 7 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.1 hospital_level = 1 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.1 hospital_level = 2 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.1 hospital_level = 3 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.1 hospital_level = 4 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.1 hospital_level = 5 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.1 hospital_level = 6 }
				decrease_depopulation_effect = yes
			}
			70 = { # Nothing happens
				modifier = {
					factor = 3
					any_province_holding = { OR = { is_occupied = yes has_siege = yes } }
				}
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 is_winter = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_province_modifier = depopulated_2 }
				modifier = { factor = 2 has_province_modifier = depopulated_3 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# plague.20 Has the plague ravaged a region?
# Once half a region is depopulated during a Great Plague, every player
# ruler hears of it, once per region per plague.
# Scope: ROOT is a player ruler. Fired from on_yearly_pulse.
character_event = {
	id = plague.20
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_pulse

	ai = no
	has_global_flag = great_plague_broadcasted

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_global_flag = great_plague_world_old_world_empire }

					region = world_old_world_empire
					percentage = 0.5
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = depopulated_1
						has_province_modifier = depopulated_2
						has_province_modifier = depopulated_3
					}
				}
			}
			set_global_flag = great_plague_world_old_world_empire
			any_playable_ruler = {
				limit = { ai = no }
				narrative_event = { id = plague.21 days = 10 random = 250 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_global_flag = great_plague_world_old_world_bretonnia }

					percentage = 0.5

# plague.40 The Great Plague is over
# Once no province has Nurgle's Rot or the Creeping Buboes and no region
# is still half depopulated, the plague's flags are cleared so the next
# one is told again. Also clears the player's "plague is close" flag,
# which vanilla never cleared.
# Scope: ROOT is a player ruler. Fired from on_decade_pulse.
character_event = {
	id = plague.40
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_decade_pulse

	ai = no
	has_global_flag = great_plague_broadcasted

	trigger = {
		NOT = { any_province = { OR = { disease = nurgles_rot disease = creeping_buboes } } }
		NOR = {
			regional_percentage = {
				region = world_old_world_empire
				percentage = 0.5
				OR = {
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_1
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_2
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_3
				}
			}
			regional_percentage = {
				region = world_old_world_bretonnia
				percentage = 0.5
				OR = {
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_1
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_2
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_3
				}
			}
			regional_percentage = {
				region = world_old_world_kislev
				percentage = 0.5
				OR = {
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_1
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_2
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_3
				}
			}
			regional_percentage = {
				region = world_old_world_tilea
				percentage = 0.5
				OR = {
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_1
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_2
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_3
				}
			}
			regional_percentage = {
				region = world_old_world_estalia
				percentage = 0.5
				OR = {
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_1
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_2
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_3
				}
			}
			regional_percentage = {
				region = world_old_world_border_princes
				percentage = 0.5
				OR = {
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_1
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_2
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_3
				}
			}
			regional_percentage = {
				region = world_greater_araby
				percentage = 0.5
				OR = {
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_1
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_2
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_3
				}
			}
			regional_percentage = {
				region = world_norsca
				percentage = 0.5
				OR = {
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_1
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_2
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_3
				}
			}
			regional_percentage = {
				region = world_karaz_ankor
				percentage = 0.5
				OR = {
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_1
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_2
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_3
				}
			}
			regional_percentage = {
				region = world_albion
				percentage = 0.5
				OR = {
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_1
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_2
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_3
				}
			}
			regional_percentage = {
				region = world_ulthuan
				percentage = 0.5
				OR = {
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_1
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_2
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_3
				}
			}
			regional_percentage = {
				region = world_new_world_lustria
				percentage = 0.5
				OR = {
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_1
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_2
					has_province_modifier = depopulated_3
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		clr_global_flag = great_plague_broadcasted
		clr_global_flag = great_plague_rot
		clr_global_flag = great_plague_buboes
		clr_global_flag = great_plague_world_old_world_empire
		clr_global_flag = great_plague_world_old_world_bretonnia
		clr_global_flag = great_plague_world_old_world_kislev
		clr_global_flag = great_plague_world_old_world_tilea
		clr_global_flag = great_plague_world_old_world_estalia
		clr_global_flag = great_plague_world_old_world_border_princes
		clr_global_flag = great_plague_world_greater_araby
		clr_global_flag = great_plague_world_norsca
		clr_global_flag = great_plague_world_karaz_ankor
		clr_global_flag = great_plague_world_albion
		clr_global_flag = great_plague_world_ulthuan
		clr_global_flag = great_plague_world_new_world_lustria
		any_playable_ruler = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = great_plague_close }
			clr_character_flag = great_plague_close
		}
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Part 4: Symptoms and diseases (namespace symptom)
# Faction: Generic
# Serves: every living character who can fall sick; Daemons, the dead and
# other disease-immune races are left out by the symptom effects.
#
# The Reaper's Due sickness machine. An infection or a bad turn adds a
# symptom; each symptom may lead on to a disease, which grows as more
# symptoms pile up until it is diagnosed and the trait is added.
#   symptom.1-11    Symptoms: cough, fever, diarrhoea, vomiting, headache,
#                   chest pain, cramps, rash, abdominal pain, fatigue, malaise
#   symptom.12-26   Hidden: a disease develops (one per disease)
#   symptom.27-41   Diagnosis, one per disease: the Rattlelung, the Kruts, the
#                   Weevil Cough, the Ochre Pox, the Red Pox, the Black
#                   Plague, the Green Pox, the Ghoulpox, the Lustrian Fever,
#                   flu, the Bloody Flux, gout, food poisoning, rabies, cancer
#   symptom.42      A leper becomes disfigured
#   symptom.51-57   Scurvy at sea
#   symptom.61-62   Close family hear of it
#
# Diseases started by an epidemic (common/disease/00_disease.txt and
# wh_disease.txt) jump straight to the develop events. Symptom effects:
# add_<symptom>_effect in common/scripted_effects/00_scripted_effects.txt.
#
# Was events/rip_symptom_events.txt (RIP.5000-5061): symptom.N is RIP.N-4999.
###############################################################################

namespace = symptom

# symptom.1 Cough
# Adds the cough symptom. Can lead on to the Rattlelung, the Weevil Cough, the
# Green Pox, the flu or a cancer.
# Scope: ROOT is the character. Fired by a symptom effect in
# 00_scripted_effects (add_<disease>_symptom_effect) or by another disease
# event.
character_event = {
	id = symptom.1
	desc = EVTDESCsymptom1
	picture = GFX_evt_illness
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		is_disease_immune_race = no
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTsymptom_alas
		add_trait = cough

		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_playable = no }
				host = {
					random_courtier_or_vassal = { #Markus doing
						limit = {
							liege = { character = PREVPREV }
							has_minor_title = title_court_physician
						}
						character_event = { id = treatment.32 days = 1 }
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_playable = yes }
				random_courtier_or_vassal = { #Markus doing
					limit = { has_minor_title = title_court_physician }
					character_event = { id = treatment.32 days = 1 }
				}
			}
			random_list = {
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						OR = {
							AND = {
								NOT = { has_character_flag = got_pneumonia }
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
							}
							has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
						}
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 age = 6 NOT = { age = 45 } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 health = 5 }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = got_pneumonia
						set_character_flag = developing_illness
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_character_flag = got_pneumonia }
						character_event = { id = symptom.12 days = 14 random = 14 }
					}
				}
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						OR = {
							AND = {
								NOT = { has_character_flag = got_tuberculosis }
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
							}
							has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.01 #rare when not an epidemic
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_flag = immune_tuberculosis }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = got_tuberculosis
						set_character_flag = developing_illness
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_character_flag = got_tuberculosis }
						character_event = { id = symptom.16 days = 14 random = 14 }
					}
				}
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						OR = {
							AND = {
								NOT = { has_character_flag = got_measles }
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
							}
							has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.01 #rare when not an epidemic
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_flag = immune_measles }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = got_measles
						set_character_flag = developing_illness
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_character_flag = got_measles }
						character_event = { id = symptom.19 days = 14 random = 14 }
					}
				}
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						OR = {
							AND = {
								NOT = { has_character_flag = got_flu }
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
							}
							has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
						}
					}

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = got_flu
						set_character_flag = developing_illness
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_character_flag = got_flu }
						character_event = { id = symptom.21 days = 14 random = 14 }
					}
				}
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						OR = {
							AND = {
								NOT = { has_character_flag = got_cancer }
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
							}
							has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							trait = creature_orc
							trait = creature_goblin
							trait = creature_snotling
						}
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0.1 NOT = { age = 16 } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.15 NOT = { age = 45 } }
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.05
						OR = { trait = immortal has_artifact = elixir_of_eternal_youth }
					}
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 age = 45 is_long_lived_character = no }
					modifier = { factor = 0.1 NOT = { age = 500 } is_long_lived_character = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 0.05 NOT = { age = 300 } is_long_lived_character = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { age = 100 } is_long_lived_character = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 age = 100 is_member_of_bretonnia_trigger = yes }
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.1
						age = 150
						OR = { trait = creature_dwarf trait = creature_chaos_dwarf }
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = got_cancer
						set_character_flag = developing_illness
						if = {
							limit = { trait = creature_elf age = 300 }
							set_character_flag = immortal_old_age_flag
						}
						else_if = {
							limit = { trait = creature_dwarf age = 150 }
							set_character_flag = immortal_old_age_flag
						}
						else = { set_character_flag = generic_cancer_flag }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_character_flag = got_cancer }
						character_event = { id = symptom.26 days = 14 random = 14 }
					}
				}
				100 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_flag = developing_illness }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = disease_not_serious
						random_list = {
							100 = { character_event = { id = treatment.3 days = 10 random = 30 } }
							100 = { character_event = { id = treatment.3 days = 90 random = 30 } }
							100 = { character_event = { id = treatment.3 days = 700 random = 100 } }
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# symptom.2 Fever
# Adds the fever symptom. Can lead on to almost any disease.
# Scope: ROOT is the character. Fired by a symptom effect in
# 00_scripted_effects (add_<disease>_symptom_effect) or by another disease
# event.
character_event = {
	id = symptom.2
	desc = EVTDESCsymptom2
	picture = GFX_evt_illness
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		is_disease_immune_race = no
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTsymptom_alas
		add_trait = fever

		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_playable = no }

						limit = {
							liege = { character = PREVPREV }
							has_minor_title = title_court_physician
						}
						character_event = { id = treatment.32 days = 1 }
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_playable = yes }
				random_courtier_or_vassal = { #Markus doing
					limit = { has_minor_title = title_court_physician }
					character_event = { id = treatment.32 days = 1 }
				}
			}
			random_list = {
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						OR = {
							AND = {
								NOT = { has_character_flag = got_pneumonia }
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
							}
							has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
						}

						factor = 0

							AND = {
								NOT = { has_character_flag = got_syphilis }
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
							}
							has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
						}
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { NOT = { age = 16 } trait = celibate } }

					modifier = { factor = 0.01 trait = chaste }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = got_syphilis
						set_character_flag = developing_illness

							has_character_flag = disease_not_serious

					modifier = { factor = 2 age = 30 trait = creature_human }
					modifier = { factor = 2 trait = gluttonous }
					modifier = { factor = 2 trait = drunkard }
					modifier = { factor = 2 trait = hedonist }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_focus = focus_carousing }

					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = gluttonous }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = hedonist }

					modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_flag = developing_illness }
					if = {

							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness

							}
						}
						set_character_flag = disease_not_serious
						character_event = { id = treatment.4 days = 10 random = 30 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# symptom.3 Diarrhoea
# Adds the diarrhoea symptom. Can lead on to the Ochre Pox, the Red Pox, the
# flu, the Bloody Flux, food poisoning or a cancer.
# Scope: ROOT is the character. Fired by a symptom effect in
# 00_scripted_effects (add_<disease>_symptom_effect) or by another disease
# event.
character_event = {
	id = symptom.3
	desc = EVTDESCsymptom3
	picture = GFX_evt_illness
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		is_disease_immune_race = no
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTsymptom_alas
		add_trait = diarrhea

		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_playable = no }
				host = {
					random_courtier_or_vassal = { #Markus doing
						limit = {
							liege = { character = PREVPREV }
							has_minor_title = title_court_physician
						}
						character_event = { id = treatment.32 days = 1 }
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_playable = yes }
				random_courtier_or_vassal = { #Markus doing
					limit = { has_minor_title = title_court_physician }
					character_event = { id = treatment.32 days = 1 }
				}
			}
			random_list = {
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						OR = {
							AND = {
								NOT = { has_character_flag = got_typhoid_fever }
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
							}
							has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.01 #rare when not an epidemic
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_flag = immune_typhoid_fever }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = got_typhoid_fever
						set_character_flag = developing_illness
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_character_flag = got_typhoid_fever }
						character_event = { id = symptom.17 days = 14 random = 14 }
					}
				}
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						OR = {
							AND = {
								NOT = { has_character_flag = got_typhus }
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
							}
							has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.01 #rare when not an epidemic
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_flag = immune_typhus }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = got_typhus
						set_character_flag = developing_illness
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_character_flag = got_typhus }
						character_event = { id = symptom.18 days = 14 random = 14 }
					}
				}
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						OR = {
							AND = {
								NOT = { has_character_flag = got_flu }
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
							}
							has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
						}
					}
					modifier = { factor = 2 NOT = { age = 13 } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 age = 13 }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = got_flu
						set_character_flag = developing_illness
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_character_flag = got_flu }
						character_event = { id = symptom.21 days = 14 random = 14 }
					}
				}
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						OR = {
							AND = {
								NOT = { has_character_flag = got_dysentery }
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
							}
							has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
						}
					}

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = got_dysentery
						set_character_flag = developing_illness
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_character_flag = got_dysentery }
						character_event = { id = symptom.22 days = 14 random = 14 }
					}
				}
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						OR = {
							AND = {
								NOT = { has_character_flag = got_food_poisoning }
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
							}
							has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
						}
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { age = 4 } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.1 NOT = { age = 6 } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 NOT = { age = 13 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = gluttonous }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = hedonist }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_focus = focus_carousing }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = temperate }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = got_food_poisoning
						set_character_flag = developing_illness
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_character_flag = got_food_poisoning }
						character_event = { id = symptom.24 days = 6 random = 6 }
					}
				}
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						OR = {
							AND = {
								NOT = { has_character_flag = got_cancer }
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
							}
							has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							trait = creature_orc
							trait = creature_goblin
							trait = creature_snotling
						}
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0.1 NOT = { age = 16 } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.15 NOT = { age = 45 } }
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.05
						OR = { trait = immortal has_artifact = elixir_of_eternal_youth }
					}
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 age = 45 is_long_lived_character = no }
					modifier = { factor = 0.1 NOT = { age = 500 } is_long_lived_character = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 0.05 NOT = { age = 300 } is_long_lived_character = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { age = 100 } is_long_lived_character = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 age = 100 is_member_of_bretonnia_trigger = yes }
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.1
						age = 150
						OR = { trait = creature_dwarf trait = creature_chaos_dwarf }
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = got_cancer
						set_character_flag = developing_illness
						if = {
							limit = { trait = creature_elf age = 300 }
							set_character_flag = immortal_old_age_flag
						}
						else_if = {
							limit = { trait = creature_dwarf age = 150 }
							set_character_flag = immortal_old_age_flag
						}
						else = { set_character_flag = generic_cancer_flag }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_character_flag = got_cancer }
						character_event = { id = symptom.26 days = 14 random = 14 }
					}
				}
				100 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_flag = developing_illness }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = disease_not_serious
						character_event = { id = treatment.5 days = 10 random = 30 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# symptom.4 Vomiting
# Adds the vomiting symptom. Can lead on to the flu, food poisoning or a
# cancer.
# Scope: ROOT is the character. Fired by a symptom effect in
# 00_scripted_effects (add_<disease>_symptom_effect) or by another disease
# event.
character_event = {
	id = symptom.4
	desc = EVTDESCsymptom4
	picture = GFX_evt_illness
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		is_disease_immune_race = no
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTsymptom_alas
		add_trait = vomiting

		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_playable = no }
				host = {
					random_courtier_or_vassal = { #Markus doing
						limit = {
							liege = { character = PREVPREV }
							has_minor_title = title_court_physician
						}
						character_event = { id = treatment.32 days = 1 }
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_playable = yes }
				random_courtier_or_vassal = { #Markus doing
					limit = { has_minor_title = title_court_physician }
					character_event = { id = treatment.32 days = 1 }
				}
			}
			random_list = {
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						OR = {
							AND = {
								NOT = { has_character_flag = got_flu }
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
							}
							has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
						}
					}
					modifier = { factor = 2 NOT = { age = 13 } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 age = 13 }

								NOT = { has_character_flag = got_food_poisoning }
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
							}
							has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
						}
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { age = 4 } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.1 NOT = { age = 6 } }

					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = gluttonous }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = hedonist }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_focus = focus_carousing }

								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = got_food_poisoning
						set_character_flag = developing_illness
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_character_flag = got_food_poisoning }
						character_event = { id = symptom.24 days = 6 random = 6 }
					}
				}
				100 = {

								NOT = { has_character_flag = got_cancer }
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
							}
							has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							trait = creature_orc
							trait = creature_goblin
							trait = creature_snotling

# symptom.5 Headache
# Adds the headache symptom. Can lead on to the Kruts, the Red Pox, the
# Ghoulpox, the Lustrian Fever, the flu or a cancer.
# Scope: ROOT is the character. Fired by a symptom effect in
# 00_scripted_effects (add_<disease>_symptom_effect) or by another disease
# event.
character_event = {
	id = symptom.5
	desc = EVTDESCsymptom5
	picture = GFX_evt_illness
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		is_disease_immune_race = no
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTsymptom_alas
		add_trait = headache

		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_playable = no }
				host = {
					random_courtier_or_vassal = { #Markus doing
						limit = {
							liege = { character = PREVPREV }
							has_minor_title = title_court_physician
						}
						character_event = { id = treatment.32 days = 1 }
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_playable = yes }
				random_courtier_or_vassal = { #Markus doing
					limit = { has_minor_title = title_court_physician }
					character_event = { id = treatment.32 days = 1 }
				}
			}
			random_list = {
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						OR = {
							AND = {
								NOT = { has_character_flag = got_syphilis }
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
							}
							has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
						}
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { NOT = { age = 16 } trait = celibate } }

					modifier = { factor = 0.01 trait = chaste }

					modifier = { factor = 0.75 NOT = { trait = lustful } is_female = yes }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = got_syphilis
						set_character_flag = developing_illness
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_character_flag = got_syphilis }
						character_event = { id = symptom.15 days = 14 random = 14 }
					}
				}
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						OR = {
							AND = {
								NOT = { has_character_flag = got_typhus }
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
							}
							has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.01 #rare when not an epidemic
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_flag = immune_typhus }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = got_typhus
						set_character_flag = developing_illness
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_character_flag = got_typhus }
						character_event = { id = symptom.18 days = 14 random = 14 }
					}
				}
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						OR = {
							AND = {
								NOT = { has_character_flag = got_smallpox }
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
							}
							has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.01 #rare when not an epidemic
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_flag = immune_smallpox }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = got_smallpox
						set_character_flag = developing_illness
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_character_flag = got_smallpox }
						character_event = { id = symptom.20 days = 14 random = 14 }
					}
				}
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						OR = {
							AND = {
								NOT = { has_character_flag = got_aztec_disease }
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
							}
							has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
						}
					}
					modifier = { # the Lustrian Fever is caught in the jungles of Lustria
						factor = 0
						NOT = { location = { region = world_new_world_lustria } }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.05 # rare, but the jungle is unkind to strangers
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_flag = immune_aztec_disease }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = got_aztec_disease
						set_character_flag = developing_illness
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_character_flag = got_aztec_disease }
						character_event = { id = symptom.20 days = 14 random = 14 }
					}
				}
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						OR = {
							AND = {
								NOT = { has_character_flag = got_flu }
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
							}
							has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
						}
					}

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = got_flu
						set_character_flag = developing_illness
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_character_flag = got_flu }
						character_event = { id = symptom.21 days = 14 random = 14 }
					}
				}
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						OR = {
							AND = {
								NOT = { has_character_flag = got_cancer }
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
							}
							has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							trait = creature_orc
							trait = creature_goblin
							trait = creature_snotling
						}
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0.1 NOT = { age = 16 } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.15 NOT = { age = 45 } }
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.05
						OR = { trait = immortal has_artifact = elixir_of_eternal_youth }
					}
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 age = 45 is_long_lived_character = no }
					modifier = { factor = 0.1 NOT = { age = 500 } is_long_lived_character = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 0.05 NOT = { age = 300 } is_long_lived_character = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { age = 100 } is_long_lived_character = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 age = 100 is_member_of_bretonnia_trigger = yes }
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.1
						age = 150
						OR = { trait = creature_dwarf trait = creature_chaos_dwarf }
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = got_cancer
						set_character_flag = developing_illness
						if = {
							limit = { trait = creature_elf age = 300 }
							set_character_flag = immortal_old_age_flag
						}
						else_if = {
							limit = { trait = creature_dwarf age = 150 }
							set_character_flag = immortal_old_age_flag
						}
						else = { set_character_flag = generic_cancer_flag }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_character_flag = got_cancer }
						character_event = { id = symptom.26 days = 14 random = 14 }
					}
				}
				100 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_flag = developing_illness }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = disease_not_serious
						random_list = {
							100 = { character_event = { id = treatment.10 days = 10 random = 30 } }
							100 = { character_event = { id = treatment.10 days = 90 random = 45 } }
							100 = {
								character_event = { id = treatment.10 days = 700 random = 100 }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

					modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_flag = immune_tuberculosis }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = got_tuberculosis
						set_character_flag = developing_illness
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_character_flag = got_tuberculosis }
						character_event = { id = symptom.16 days = 14 random = 14 }
					}
				}
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						OR = {
							AND = {
								NOT = { has_character_flag = got_cancer }
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
							}
							has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							trait = creature_orc
							trait = creature_goblin
							trait = creature_snotling
						}
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0.1 NOT = { age = 16 } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.15 NOT = { age = 45 } }
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.05
						OR = { trait = immortal has_artifact = elixir_of_eternal_youth }
					}
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 age = 45 is_long_lived_character = no }
					modifier = { factor = 0.1 NOT = { age = 500 } is_long_lived_character = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 0.05 NOT = { age = 300 } is_long_lived_character = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { age = 100 } is_long_lived_character = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 age = 100 is_member_of_bretonnia_trigger = yes }
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.1
						age = 150
						OR = { trait = creature_dwarf trait = creature_chaos_dwarf }
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = got_cancer
						set_character_flag = developing_illness
						if = {
							limit = { trait = creature_elf age = 300 }
							set_character_flag = immortal_old_age_flag
						}
						else_if = {
							limit = { trait = creature_dwarf age = 150 }
							set_character_flag = immortal_old_age_flag
						}
						else = { set_character_flag = generic_cancer_flag }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_character_flag = got_cancer }
						character_event = { id = symptom.26 days = 14 random = 14 }
					}
				}
				100 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_flag = developing_illness }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						random_list = { #chest pains can last a long time
							100 = {
								character_event = { id = treatment.7 days = 1725 random = 200 }
							}
							100 = {
								character_event = { id = treatment.7 days = 3650 random = 365 }
							}
							100 = {
								modifier = { factor = 3 age = 50 }
								character_event = { id = treatment.7 days = 7300 random = 3650 }
							}
							100 = {
								modifier = { factor = 5 NOT = { age = 30 } }
								character_event = { id = treatment.7 days = 30 random = 60 }
								set_character_flag = disease_not_serious #only protected from other illness if this is a short term effect
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# symptom.7 Cramps
# Adds the cramps symptom. Can lead on to food poisoning or the Black Plague.
# Scope: ROOT is the character. Fired by a symptom effect in
# 00_scripted_effects (add_<disease>_symptom_effect) or by another disease
# event.
character_event = {
	id = symptom.7
	desc = EVTDESCsymptom7
	picture = GFX_evt_illness
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		is_disease_immune_race = no
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTsymptom_alas
		add_trait = cramps

		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_playable = no }
				host = {
					random_courtier_or_vassal = { #Markus doing
						limit = {
							liege = { character = PREVPREV }
							has_minor_title = title_court_physician
						}
						character_event = { id = treatment.32 days = 1 }
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_playable = yes }
				random_courtier_or_vassal = { #Markus doing
					limit = { has_minor_title = title_court_physician }
					character_event = { id = treatment.32 days = 1 }
				}
			}
			random_list = {
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						OR = {
							AND = {
								NOT = { has_character_flag = got_food_poisoning }
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
							}
							has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
						}
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { age = 4 } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.1 NOT = { age = 6 } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 NOT = { age = 13 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = gluttonous }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = hedonist }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_focus = focus_carousing }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = temperate }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = got_food_poisoning
						set_character_flag = developing_illness
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_character_flag = got_food_poisoning }
						character_event = { id = symptom.24 days = 6 random = 6 }
					}
				}
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						OR = {
							AND = {
								NOT = { has_character_flag = got_bubonic_plague }
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
							}
							has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.01 #rare when not an epidemic
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_flag = immune_bubonic_plague }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = got_bubonic_plague
						set_character_flag = developing_illness
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_character_flag = got_bubonic_plague }
						character_event = { id = symptom.13 days = 14 random = 14 }
					}
				}
				100 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_flag = developing_illness }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = disease_not_serious
						character_event = { id = treatment.8 days = 10 random = 30 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

						factor = 0.01 #rare when not an epidemic
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_flag = immune_typhus }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = got_typhus
						set_character_flag = developing_illness
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_character_flag = got_typhus }
						character_event = { id = symptom.18 days = 14 random = 14 }
					}
				}
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						OR = {
							AND = {
								NOT = { has_character_flag = got_flu }
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
							}
							has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
						}
					}

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = got_flu
						set_character_flag = developing_illness
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_character_flag = got_flu }
						character_event = { id = symptom.21 days = 14 random = 14 }
					}
				}
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						OR = {
							AND = {
								NOT = { has_character_flag = got_gout }
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
							}
							has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
						}
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0.25 NOT = { age = 30 } }
					modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { age = 16 } }
					modifier = { factor = 2 age = 30 }
					modifier = { factor = 2 trait = gluttonous }
					modifier = { factor = 2 trait = drunkard }
					modifier = { factor = 2 trait = hedonist }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_focus = focus_carousing }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = temperate }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = got_gout
						set_character_flag = developing_illness
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_character_flag = got_gout }
						character_event = { id = symptom.23 days = 14 random = 14 }
					}
				}
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						OR = {
							AND = {
								NOT = { has_character_flag = got_rabies }
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
							}
							has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.1 # uncommon
					}

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = got_rabies
						set_character_flag = developing_illness
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_character_flag = got_rabies }
						character_event = { id = symptom.25 days = 14 random = 14 }
					}
				}
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						OR = {
							AND = {
								NOT = { has_character_flag = got_cancer }
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
							}
							has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							trait = creature_orc
							trait = creature_goblin
							trait = creature_snotling
						}
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0.1 NOT = { age = 16 } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.15 NOT = { age = 45 } }
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.05
						OR = { trait = immortal has_artifact = elixir_of_eternal_youth }
					}
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 age = 45 is_long_lived_character = no }
					modifier = { factor = 0.1 NOT = { age = 500 } is_long_lived_character = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 0.05 NOT = { age = 300 } is_long_lived_character = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { age = 100 } is_long_lived_character = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 age = 100 is_member_of_bretonnia_trigger = yes }
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.1
						age = 150
						OR = { trait = creature_dwarf trait = creature_chaos_dwarf }
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = got_cancer
						set_character_flag = developing_illness
						if = {
							limit = { trait = creature_elf age = 300 }
							set_character_flag = immortal_old_age_flag
						}
						else_if = {
							limit = { trait = creature_dwarf age = 150 }
							set_character_flag = immortal_old_age_flag
						}
						else = { set_character_flag = generic_cancer_flag }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_character_flag = got_cancer }
						character_event = { id = symptom.26 days = 14 random = 14 }
					}
				}
				100 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_flag = developing_illness }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = disease_not_serious
						random_list = {
							100 = { character_event = { id = treatment.12 days = 10 random = 30 } }
							100 = { character_event = { id = treatment.12 days = 90 random = 45 } }
							100 = { character_event = { id = treatment.12 days = 180 random = 60 } }
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# symptom.11 Malaise
# Adds the malaise symptom. Can lead on to the Ochre Pox, the Black Plague,
# the Ghoulpox, the Lustrian Fever, rabies, a cancer or the flu.
# Scope: ROOT is the character. Fired by a symptom effect in
# 00_scripted_effects (add_<disease>_symptom_effect) or by another disease
# event.
character_event = {
	id = symptom.11
	desc = EVTDESCsymptom11
	picture = GFX_evt_illness
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		is_disease_immune_race = no
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTsymptom_alas
		add_trait = malaise

		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_playable = no }
				host = {
					random_courtier_or_vassal = { #Markus doing
						limit = {
							liege = { character = PREVPREV }
							has_minor_title = title_court_physician
						}
						character_event = { id = treatment.32 days = 1 }
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_playable = yes }
				random_courtier_or_vassal = { #Markus doing
					limit = { has_minor_title = title_court_physician }
					character_event = { id = treatment.32 days = 1 }
				}
			}
			random_list = {
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						OR = {
							AND = {
								NOT = { has_character_flag = got_typhoid_fever }
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
							}
							has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.01 #rare when not an epidemic
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_flag = immune_typhoid_fever }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = got_typhoid_fever
						set_character_flag = developing_illness
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_character_flag = got_typhoid_fever }
						character_event = { id = symptom.17 days = 14 random = 14 }
					}
				}
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						OR = {
							AND = {
								NOT = { has_character_flag = got_bubonic_plague }
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
							}
							has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.01 #rare when not an epidemic
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_flag = immune_bubonic_plague }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = got_bubonic_plague

					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_character_flag = got_bubonic_plague }
						character_event = { id = symptom.13 days = 14 random = 14 }

				}
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0

							AND = {
								NOT = { has_character_flag = got_smallpox }
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
							}
							has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.01 #rare when not an epidemic
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_flag = immune_smallpox }

					if = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								has_character_flag = developing_illness
								has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
							}
						}
						set_character_flag = got_smallpox
						set_character_flag = developing_illness
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_character_flag = got_smallpox }

# symptom.12 Developing the Rattlelung
# Hidden. Counts the cough, chest pain and fever the character already has,
# adds the next one or, once enough are in place, fires the diagnosis
# (symptom.27).
# Scope: ROOT is the character, who has the got_pneumonia flag. Fired by a
# symptom event, the disease's on_character_infection or its symptom effect.
character_event = {
	id = symptom.12

	trigger = {
		has_character_flag = got_pneumonia
	}

	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			100 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 trait = cough trait = fever trait = chest_pain }
				add_pneumonia_symptom_effect = yes #Disease effect
			}
			50 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOR = { trait = cough trait = chest_pain trait = fever } }
				character_event = { id = symptom.27 days = 20 random = 20 } #Disease develops
			}
		}
	}
}

# symptom.20 Developing the Ghoulpox or the Lustrian Fever
# Hidden. Counts the malaise, fever, headache and rash the character already
# has, adds the next one or, once enough are in place, fires the diagnosis
# (symptom.31 or symptom.39).
# Scope: ROOT is the character, who has the got_smallpox flag. Fired by a
# symptom event, the disease's on_character_infection or its symptom effect.
character_event = {
	id = symptom.20

	trigger = {
		has_character_flag = got_smallpox
	}

	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			100 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 num_of_symptoms = 3 }
				add_smallpox_symptom_effect = yes #Disease effect
			}
			50 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					NOR = { trait = malaise trait = fever trait = headache trait = rash }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_character_flag = got_smallpox
						NOT = { has_character_flag = got_aztec_disease }
					}
					character_event = { id = symptom.31 days = 20 random = 20 } #Disease develops
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_character_flag = got_aztec_disease
						NOT = { has_character_flag = got_smallpox }
					}
					character_event = { id = symptom.39 days = 20 random = 20 } #Disease develops
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# symptom.21 Developing the flu
# Hidden. Counts the malaise, fever, cough, fatigue, headache, diarrhoea and
# abdominal pain the character already has, adds the next one or, once enough
# are in place, fires the diagnosis (symptom.33).
# Scope: ROOT is the character, who has the got_flu flag. Fired by a symptom
# event, the disease's on_character_infection or its symptom effect.
character_event = {
	id = symptom.21

	trigger = {
		has_character_flag = got_flu
	}

	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			100 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 num_of_symptoms = 3 }
				add_flu_symptom_effect = yes #Disease effect
			}
			50 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					NOR = {
						trait = malaise
						trait = fever
						trait = cough
						trait = fatigue
						trait = headache
						trait = diarrhea
						trait = abdominal_pain
					}
				}
				character_event = { id = symptom.33 days = 20 random = 20 } #Disease develops
			}
		}
	}
}

# symptom.22 Developing the Bloody Flux
# Hidden. Counts the diarrhoea, fever and abdominal pain the character already
# has, adds the next one or, once enough are in place, fires the diagnosis
# (symptom.34).
# Scope: ROOT is the character, who has the got_dysentery flag. Fired by a
# symptom event, the disease's on_character_infection or its symptom effect.
character_event = {
	id = symptom.22

	trigger = {
		has_character_flag = got_dysentery
	}

	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			100 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 trait = diarrhea trait = fever trait = abdominal_pain }
				add_dysentery_symptom_effect = yes #Disease effect
			}
			50 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					NOR = { trait = diarrhea trait = fever trait = abdominal_pain }
				}
				character_event = { id = symptom.34 days = 20 random = 20 } #Disease develops
			}
		}
	}
}

# symptom.27 Diagnosis: the Rattlelung
# The symptoms come together: adds pneumonic, clears the developing flags,
# pings the court physician and tells close family (symptom.61).
# Scope: ROOT is the character. Fired by symptom.12.
character_event = {
	id = symptom.27
	desc = EVTDESCsymptom27
	picture = GFX_evt_illness
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = { is_disease_immune_race = no }

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTsymptom_alas
		add_trait = pneumonic
		nurgle_blessing_check_effect = yes
		set_character_flag = manifest_pneumonia
		add_disease_level_effect = yes
		blessing_removes_disease_effect = yes
		deaths_gift_removes_disease_effect = yes
		scrying_omen_fulfilled_by_gaining_disease_effect = yes

		if = { limit = { trait = fever } remove_trait = fever }
		if = { limit = { trait = cough } remove_trait = cough }
		if = { limit = { trait = chest_pain } remove_trait = chest_pain }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = { limit = { is_unimportant_trigger = yes } health = -3 }
			if = {
				limit = { is_playable = no }
				host = {
					random_courtier_or_vassal = { #Markus doing
						limit = {
							liege = { character = PREVPREV }
							has_minor_title = title_court_physician
						}
						character_event = { id = treatment.32 days = 1 }
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_playable = yes }
				random_courtier_or_vassal = { #Markus doing
					limit = { has_minor_title = title_court_physician }
					character_event = { id = treatment.32 days = 1 }
				}
			}
			father = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						ai = no
						is_liege_of = ROOT
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion }
					}
					set_character_flag = announce_pneumonic
					character_event = { id = symptom.61 }
				}
			}
			mother = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						ai = no
						is_liege_of = ROOT
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion }
					}
					set_character_flag = announce_pneumonic
					character_event = { id = symptom.61 }
				}
			}
			spouse = {
				if = {
					limit = { ai = no NOT = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion } }
					set_character_flag = announce_pneumonic
					character_event = { id = symptom.61 }
				}
			}
			clr_character_flag = developing_illness
			clr_character_flag = got_pneumonia
		}
	}
}

# The symptoms come together: adds syphilitic, clears the developing flags,
# pings the court physician and tells close family (symptom.61).
# Scope: ROOT is the character. Fired by symptom.15; a spouse may learn of it
# (symptom.62).

	id = symptom.28
	desc = EVTDESCsymptom28
	picture = GFX_evt_illness
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	hide_from = yes

		set_character_flag = manifest_syphilis
		add_disease_level_effect = yes
		blessing_removes_disease_effect = yes
		deaths_gift_removes_disease_effect = yes
		scrying_omen_fulfilled_by_gaining_disease_effect = yes

		if = { limit = { trait = fever } remove_trait = fever }
		if = { limit = { trait = rash } remove_trait = rash }
		if = { limit = { trait = headache } remove_trait = headache }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = { limit = { is_unimportant_trigger = yes } health = -3 }
			if = {
				limit = { is_playable = no }
				host = {
					random_courtier_or_vassal = { #Markus doing
						limit = {
							liege = { character = PREVPREV }
							has_minor_title = title_court_physician
						}
						character_event = { id = treatment.32 days = 1 }
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_playable = yes }
				random_courtier_or_vassal = { #Markus doing
					limit = { has_minor_title = title_court_physician }
					character_event = { id = treatment.32 days = 1 }
				}
			}
			father = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						ai = no
						is_liege_of = ROOT
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion }
					}
					set_character_flag = announce_syphilis
					character_event = { id = symptom.61 }
				}
			}
			mother = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						ai = no
						is_liege_of = ROOT
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion }
					}
					set_character_flag = announce_syphilis
					character_event = { id = symptom.61 }
				}
			}
			spouse = {
				if = {
					limit = { ai = no NOT = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion } }
					character_event = { id = symptom.62 }
				}
			}
			any_lover = {
				limit = {
					ai = no
					is_liege_of = ROOT
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion }
				}
				character_event = { id = symptom.62 }
			}
			clr_character_flag = developing_illness
			clr_character_flag = got_syphilis
		}
	}
}

		}
	}
}

# symptom.42 A leper becomes disfigured
# Leprosy eats away at the face and the character becomes disfigured.
# Scope: ROOT is the leper. Fired by health.3 when leprosy is caught.
character_event = {
	id = symptom.42
	picture = GFX_evt_plague_spreading
	desc = EVTDESCsymptom42
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = leper
		NOT = { trait = disfigured }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTsymptom_alas
		add_trait = disfigured
	}
}

# symptom.51 Scurvy at sea
# Hidden. A character long at sea may come down with scurvy (symptom.52) and
# checks again later (symptom.57). Only commanders whose unit is at sea
# carry the flag scurvy_at_sea (naval.1 sets and clears it), so the yearly
# pulse skips everyone else.
# Scope: ROOT is the character. Fired from on_yearly_pulse.
character_event = {
	id = symptom.51
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	has_character_flag = scurvy_at_sea # set and cleared by naval.1 as the unit moves
	min_age = 16
	prisoner = no

	trigger = {
		in_command = yes
		is_at_sea = yes
		NOT = { trait = scurvy }
		is_disease_immune_race = no
	}

	option = {
		random_list = {
			10 = { # Scurvy
				modifier = { factor = 500 capital_scope = { location = { has_disease = yes } } }
				modifier = { factor = 3 trait = gluttonous }
				modifier = { factor = 2 has_character_flag = been_at_sea_1 }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_character_flag = been_at_sea_2 }
				modifier = { factor = 4 has_character_flag = been_at_sea_3 }

				character_event = { id = symptom.52 }
			}
			90 = { # Still healthy
				if = {
					limit = { has_character_flag = been_at_sea_2 }
					clr_character_flag = been_at_sea_2
					set_character_flag = been_at_sea_3
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_character_flag = been_at_sea_1 }
					clr_character_flag = been_at_sea_1
					set_character_flag = been_at_sea_2
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOR = {
							has_character_flag = been_at_sea_1
							has_character_flag = been_at_sea_2
							has_character_flag = been_at_sea_3
						}
					}
					set_character_flag = been_at_sea_1
				}

				character_event = { id = symptom.57 days = 100 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# symptom.52 Scurvy
# Months at sea bring tiredness, aching legs and bleeding gums: adds scurvy.
# Scope: ROOT is the character. Fired by symptom.51.
character_event = {
	id = symptom.52
	picture = "GFX_evt_merchant_ship_at_sea_republic"

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	desc = {
		trigger = { has_dlc = "The Republic" }
		text = EVTDESCsymptom52
		picture = "GFX_evt_merchant_ship_at_sea_republic"
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { NOT = { has_dlc = "The Republic" } }
		text = EVTDESCsymptom52
		picture = "GFX_evt_kraken"
	}

	trigger = {
		is_disease_immune_race = no
		has_dlc = "Reapers"
		in_command = yes
		location = { terrain = coastal_desert }
		NOT = { trait = scurvy }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAsymptom52
		add_trait = scurvy
		nurgle_blessing_check_effect = yes
		add_disease_level_effect = yes

			character_event = { id = symptom.53 days = 30 }
			clr_character_flag = been_at_sea_1
			clr_character_flag = been_at_sea_2
			clr_character_flag = been_at_sea_3
		}
	}
}

# symptom.53 Scurvy lingers
# Hidden. Back on land, scurvy passes on its own (symptom.56) or the court
# physician is called (symptom.54).
# Scope: ROOT is the character with scurvy. Fired by symptom.52 and itself.
character_event = {
	id = symptom.53
	hide_window = yes

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		trigger = { trait = scurvy NOT = { location = { terrain = coastal_desert } } }
		if = {
			limit = { any_courtier_or_vassal = { has_minor_title = title_court_physician } }
			set_character_flag = physician_scurvy_treatment
			random_courtier_or_vassal = {
				limit = { has_minor_title = title_court_physician }
				character_event = { id = symptom.54 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = physician_scurvy_treatment } }
			character_event = { id = symptom.56 days = 60 random = 20 }
		}
		clr_character_flag = physician_scurvy_treatment
	}

	option = {
		trigger = { trait = scurvy location = { terrain = coastal_desert } }
		character_event = { id = symptom.53 days = 60 }
	}
}

# symptom.61 Sickness in the family
# Tells a parent, child or spouse which disease a close relative has, with a
# text per disease. Can be silenced for five years.
# Scope: ROOT is the relative told, FROM the sick character. Fired by the
# diagnosis events.
character_event = {
	id = symptom.61
	picture = GFX_evt_doctor_tending_to_sick
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	trigger = {
		FROM = { character_disease_trigger = yes }
		OR = {
			NOT = { has_character_flag = no_relative_illness_notifications }
			had_character_flag = { flag = no_relative_illness_notifications days = 1825 }
		}
		NOT = { trait = in_hiding }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_landed = yes
				NOR = {
					has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
					any_courtier_or_vassal = {
						has_minor_title = title_court_physician
						liege = { character = ROOT }
					}
				}
				OR = { is_tribal = yes is_nomadic = yes }
			}

			any_courtier = {
				limit = { ai = yes age = 14 NOT = { character = FROM } }
				score_value = {
					value = 1
					#Find some kind of learned character, preferably...
					additive_modifier = { is_learned_trigger = yes value = 100 }
					additive_modifier = { has_education_learning_trigger = yes value = 200 }
					additive_modifier = { has_lifestyle_learning_trigger = yes value = 300 }
					additive_modifier = { learning = 8 value = 10 }
					additive_modifier = { learning = 10 value = 10 }
					additive_modifier = { learning = 12 value = 10 }
					additive_modifier = { learning = 15 value = 10 }
					additive_modifier = { trait = kind value = 10 }
					#Shouldn't be in prison/incapable, etc...
					additive_modifier = { block_general_event_trigger = yes value = -5000 }
					additive_modifier = {
						NOT = { character = FROM } #Who is not the sick person!
						value = -10000
					}
				}
				set_character_flag = is_court_physician
				save_event_target_as = new_physician
			}
		}
	}

	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = announce_tuberculosis }
		text = EVTDESCsymptom61_TUBERCULOSIS
		picture = GFX_evt_bring_out_your_dead
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = announce_typhoid }
		text = EVTDESCsymptom61_TYPHOID
		picture = GFX_evt_bring_out_your_dead
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = announce_typhus }
		text = EVTDESCsymptom61_TYPHUS
		picture = GFX_evt_bring_out_your_dead
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = announce_plague }
		text = EVTDESCsymptom61_PLAGUE
		picture = GFX_evt_plague_spreading
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = announce_measles }
		text = EVTDESCsymptom61_MEASLES
		picture = GFX_evt_bring_out_your_dead
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = announce_smallpox }
		text = EVTDESCsymptom61_SMALLPOX
		picture = GFX_evt_bring_out_your_dead
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = announce_syphilis }
		text = EVTDESCsymptom61_SYPHILITIC
		picture = GFX_evt_bring_out_your_dead
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = announce_lustrian_fever }
		text = EVTDESCsymptom61_LUSTRIAN_FEVER
		picture = GFX_evt_bring_out_your_dead
	}
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = announce_flu } text = EVTDESCsymptom61_FLU }
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = announce_dysentery }
		text = EVTDESCsymptom61_DYSENTERY
	}
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = announce_gout } text = EVTDESCsymptom61_GOUT }
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = announce_food_poisoning }
		text = EVTDESCsymptom61_FOOD_POISONING
	}
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = announce_rabies } text = EVTDESCsymptom61_RABIES }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = announce_cancer } text = EVTDESCsymptom61_CANCER }
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = announce_pneumonic }
		text = EVTDESCsymptom61_PNEUMONIC
	}

	option = {
		trigger = {
			is_landed = yes
			NOR = {
				has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
				any_courtier_or_vassal = {
					has_minor_title = title_court_physician
					liege = { character = ROOT }
				}
				is_tribal = yes
				is_nomadic = yes
			}
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = 3_year_physician_cooldown }
				had_character_flag = { flag = 3_year_physician_cooldown days = 1095 }
			}
		}
		name = EVTOPTCsymptom61
		custom_tooltip = { text = employ_court_physician_tooltip }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			set_character_flag = looking_for_court_physician
			character_event = { id = physician.2 days = 10 random = 30 }
			clr_character_flag = 3_year_physician_cooldown
			set_character_flag = 3_year_physician_cooldown
		}

		clr_character_flag = announce_syphilis
		clr_character_flag = announce_lustrian_fever
		clr_character_flag = announce_pneumonic
		clr_character_flag = announce_tuberculosis
		clr_character_flag = announce_typhoid
		clr_character_flag = announce_typhus
		clr_character_flag = announce_plague
		clr_character_flag = announce_measles
		clr_character_flag = announce_smallpox
		clr_character_flag = announce_flu
		clr_character_flag = announce_dysentery
		clr_character_flag = announce_gout
		clr_character_flag = announce_food_poisoning
		clr_character_flag = announce_rabies
		clr_character_flag = announce_cancer
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAsymptom61
		trigger = {
			is_landed = yes
			any_courtier_or_vassal = {
				has_minor_title = title_court_physician
				liege = { character = ROOT }
			}
		}

		clr_character_flag = announce_syphilis
		clr_character_flag = announce_lustrian_fever
		clr_character_flag = announce_pneumonic
		clr_character_flag = announce_tuberculosis
		clr_character_flag = announce_typhoid
		clr_character_flag = announce_typhus
		clr_character_flag = announce_plague
		clr_character_flag = announce_measles
		clr_character_flag = announce_smallpox
		clr_character_flag = announce_flu
		clr_character_flag = announce_dysentery
		clr_character_flag = announce_gout
		clr_character_flag = announce_food_poisoning
		clr_character_flag = announce_rabies
		clr_character_flag = announce_cancer
	}

	option = { #In case you are a tribal or nomad without a court physician...
		name = EVTOPTDsymptom61
		trigger = {
			is_landed = yes
			NOR = {
				has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
				any_courtier_or_vassal = {
					has_minor_title = title_court_physician
					liege = { character = ROOT }
				}
			}
			OR = { is_tribal = yes is_nomadic = yes }
			event_target:new_physician = { always = yes } #only show if someone fit the bill...
		}

		event_target:new_physician = {
			show_scope_change = no
			give_minor_title = title_court_physician
		}

		clr_character_flag = announce_syphilis
		clr_character_flag = announce_lustrian_fever
		clr_character_flag = announce_pneumonic
		clr_character_flag = announce_tuberculosis
		clr_character_flag = announce_typhoid
		clr_character_flag = announce_typhus
		clr_character_flag = announce_plague
		clr_character_flag = announce_measles
		clr_character_flag = announce_smallpox
		clr_character_flag = announce_flu
		clr_character_flag = announce_dysentery
		clr_character_flag = announce_gout
		clr_character_flag = announce_food_poisoning
		clr_character_flag = announce_rabies
		clr_character_flag = announce_cancer
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBsymptom61
		clr_character_flag = no_relative_illness_notifications
		set_character_flag = no_relative_illness_notifications

		clr_character_flag = announce_syphilis
		clr_character_flag = announce_lustrian_fever
		clr_character_flag = announce_pneumonic
		clr_character_flag = announce_tuberculosis
		clr_character_flag = announce_typhoid
		clr_character_flag = announce_typhus
		clr_character_flag = announce_plague
		clr_character_flag = announce_measles
		clr_character_flag = announce_smallpox
		clr_character_flag = announce_flu
		clr_character_flag = announce_dysentery
		clr_character_flag = announce_gout
		clr_character_flag = announce_food_poisoning
		clr_character_flag = announce_rabies
		clr_character_flag = announce_cancer

		custom_tooltip = { text = ignore_ill_relatives_tooltip }
	}
