# symptom.62 A spouse with the Kruts
# A spouse has been avoiding the bedchamber, and now the reason is out.
# Scope: ROOT is the spouse or lover told, FROM the sick character. Fired by
# symptom.28.
character_event = {
	id = symptom.62
	picture = GFX_evt_suspicious_noble
	desc = EVTDESCsymptom62
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			any_spouse = { character = FROM OR = { trait = has_aztec_disease trait = syphilitic } }
			any_lover = { character = FROM OR = { trait = has_aztec_disease trait = syphilitic } }
		}
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_courtier_or_vassal = {
			limit = { is_lover = FROM NOT = { character = ROOT } }
			save_event_target_as = syphilis_lover
		}
	}

	#Punish
	option = {
		trigger = { FROM = { is_landed = no } }
		name = {
			trigger = { OR = { trait = has_aztec_disease trait = syphilitic } }
			text = EVTOPTAsymptom62_A
		}

		name = {
			trigger = { NOR = { trait = has_aztec_disease trait = syphilitic } }
			text = EVTOPTAsymptom62_B
		}

		if = {
			limit = { event_target:syphilis_lover = { always = yes } }
			custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAsymptom62_TOOLTIP_A }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { event_target:syphilis_lover = { always = yes } } }
			custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAsymptom62_TOOLTIP_B }
		}

		opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_traitor years = 20 }

		reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = harsh_punishment years = 20 }

		hidden_tooltip = { reverse_imprison = FROM }

		if = {
			limit = { event_target:syphilis_lover = { always = yes } }
			opinion = { who = event_target:syphilis_lover modifier = opinion_traitor years = 20 }

			reverse_opinion = {
				who = event_target:syphilis_lover
				modifier = harsh_punishment
				years = 20
			}
		}

		hidden_tooltip = { reverse_imprison = event_target:syphilis_lover }
	}

	#Take care
	option = {
		name = {
			trigger = { OR = { trait = has_aztec_disease trait = syphilitic } }
			text = EVTOPTBsymptom62_A
		}

		name = {
			trigger = { NOR = { trait = has_aztec_disease trait = syphilitic } }
			text = EVTOPTBsymptom62_B
		}

		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBsymptom62_TT }

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = kind } }
			random = {
				chance = 30
				add_trait = kind
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = 38268 } }
			}
		}

		random_list = {
			60 = {
				modifier = { factor = 1.3 physician_good_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 1.3 is_smart_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 1.3 learning = 12 }

				FROM = { add_character_modifier = { name = good_care duration = 365 } }
			}
			40 = {
				modifier = { factor = 1.3 physician_bad_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 1.3 is_dumb_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 1.3 NOT = { learning = 8 } }

				FROM = { add_character_modifier = { name = bad_care duration = 365 } }
			}
		}
	}

	#Dissmissive
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCsymptom62
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Part 5: Treatment and recovery (namespace treatment)
# Faction: Generic
# Serves: every sick character, and every ruler with a court physician.
#
# How symptoms and diseases go away, and what the court physician does
# about them.
#   treatment.1          A maiming wound heals or festers
#   treatment.2-13       Symptoms pass on their own
#   treatment.16-31      Recovery from a disease (mean time to happen on the
#                        sick pulse; Nurgle's faithful never recover)
#   treatment.32-33      The court physician is called
#   treatment.36-48      Disease treatment: experimental, normal, cautious,
#                        mystic; symptom treatment and its outcome
#   treatment.61-66      Family hear of it; leprosy; born in seclusion; the
#                        physician becomes renowned
#   treatment.67-74      A sickly newborn
#   treatment.75-78      Recovery from the Warhammer plagues: the Creeping
#                        Buboes, the Rattlelung, the Bone Ague, the Tomb Rot
#                        (restored: they had no way to wear off). Nurgle's
#                        Rot stays incurable.
#
# Was events/rip_treatment_events.txt (RIP.11000-11073): treatment.N is
# RIP.N-10999. The commented-out leprosy cure (RIP.11023) stays out: leprosy
# is for life.
###############################################################################

namespace = treatment

# treatment.1 A maiming wound heals
# The severe wound from a maiming heals and scars, or festers into an
# infection (which clears up years later, treatment.2).
# Scope: ROOT is the character. Fired by the maiming effects in
# 00_scripted_effects.
character_event = {
	id = treatment.1
	picture = GFX_evt_garden
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	hide_from = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = severely_injured
	}

	desc = {
		picture = GFX_evt_recovery
		text = EVTDESCtreatment1_A
		trigger = { OR = { NOT = { has_character_flag = pending_infection } is_nurgle_immune_race = yes } }
	}

	desc = {
		picture = GFX_evt_bloody_tools
		text = EVTDESCtreatment1_B
		trigger = { has_character_flag = pending_infection is_nurgle_immune_race = no }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtreatment1
		trigger = { OR = { NOT = { has_character_flag = pending_infection } is_nurgle_immune_race = yes } }
		remove_trait = severely_injured
		add_scarred_tiered_effect = yes
		hidden_tooltip = { clr_character_flag = pending_infection }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtreatment1
		trigger = { has_character_flag = pending_infection is_nurgle_immune_race = no }
		remove_trait = severely_injured
		add_trait = infection
		add_scarred_tiered_effect = yes
		hidden_tooltip = {
			clr_character_flag = pending_infection
			character_event = { id = treatment.2 days = 365 random = 1095 }
		}
	}
}

# treatment.2 Infection passes
# The infection goes away; the physician may be pinged.
# Scope: ROOT is the character. Fired by the symptom event that gave it, days
# later, or by flavour events.
character_event = {
	id = treatment.2
	picture = GFX_evt_recovery
	desc = EVTDESCtreatment2
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtreatment2
		remove_trait = infection
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { trait = wounded }
				character_event = { id = health.20 days = 14 random = 30 } # wound should heal soon after
			}
		}
	}
}

# treatment.3 Cough passes
# The cough goes away; the physician may be pinged.
# Scope: ROOT is the character. Fired by the symptom event that gave it, days
# later, or by flavour events.
character_event = {
	id = treatment.3
	picture = GFX_evt_recovery
	desc = EVTDESCtreatment3
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = cough
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtreatment2
		remove_trait = cough
		clr_character_flag = disease_not_serious
		hidden_tooltip = {
			remove_character_modifier = symptom_treatment_1
			remove_character_modifier = symptom_treatment_2
			remove_character_modifier = symptom_treatment_3
			remove_character_modifier = symptom_treatment_4
		}
	}
}

# treatment.16 Recovery from the flu
# Removes flu. Followers of Nurgle and his plaguelords never shake it off.
# Healthier characters recover sooner.
# Scope: ROOT is the sick character. Mean time to happen on the sick pulse.
character_event = {
	id = treatment.16
	picture = GFX_evt_recovery
	desc = EVTDESCtreatment16
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_sick = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = flu
		NOR = {
			religion = nurgle
			trait = plaguelord
			society_member_of = cult_of_nurgle
			society_member_of = cult_of_nurgle2
			society_member_of = cult_of_nurgle3
		}
		OR = {
			had_character_flag = { flag = manifest_flu days = 30 }
			NOT = { has_character_flag = manifest_flu }
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 6
		modifier = { factor = 0.75 base_health = 5.1 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 base_health = 6.0 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 base_health = 7.0 }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 NOT = { base_health = 5.0 } }
		modifier = { factor = 2.0 NOT = { base_health = 4.1 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.66 NOT = { age = 20 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.75 NOT = { age = 30 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.75 NOT = { age = 40 } }
		modifier = { factor = 2.0 is_long_lived_character = no age = 50 }
		modifier = { factor = 2.0 is_long_lived_character = no age = 70 }
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			age = 100
			AND = { society_rank == 4 society_member_of = knights_of_bretonnia }
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			age = 150
			OR = { trait = creature_dwarf trait = creature_chaos_dwarf }
		}
		modifier = { factor = 2 age = 500 trait = creature_elf }
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			age = 2000
			OR = { trait = immortal has_artifact = elixir_of_eternal_youth }
		}
		modifier = { factor = 2.0 is_ill = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 4.0 trait = infirm }
		modifier = { factor = 5.0 trait = incapable }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtreatment2
		remove_trait = flu
		clear_disease_level_effect = yes
		clr_character_flag = developing_illness
		clr_character_flag = got_flu
		clr_character_flag = manifest_flu
		scrying_omen_fulfilled_by_removing_disease_effect = yes
		hidden_tooltip = { remove_treatment_modifiers_effect = yes }
	}
}

			NOT = { has_character_flag = manifest_dysentery }
		}
	}

		months = 6

		modifier = { factor = 0.5 base_health = 6.0 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 base_health = 7.0 }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 NOT = { base_health = 5.0 } }

		base_health = 4
		trait = pneumonic

		NOR = {
			religion = nurgle
			trait = plaguelord
			society_member_of = cult_of_nurgle
			society_member_of = cult_of_nurgle2
			society_member_of = cult_of_nurgle3
		}

			had_character_flag = { flag = manifest_pneumonia days = 30 }

		}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 4
		modifier = { factor = 2.0 NOT = { base_health = 5 } }
		modifier = { factor = 2.0 NOT = { base_health = 4 } }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = depressed }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = stressed }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 base_health = 6 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 base_health = 7 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 base_health = 8 }

	option = {

# treatment.25 Recovery from the Weevil Cough
# Removes has_tuberculosis. Followers of Nurgle and his plaguelords never
# shake it off. Healthier characters recover sooner.
# Scope: ROOT is the sick character. Mean time to happen on the sick pulse.
character_event = {
	id = treatment.25
	picture = GFX_evt_recovery
	desc = EVTDESCtreatment25
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_sick = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = has_tuberculosis
		has_dlc = "Reapers"
		NOR = {
			religion = nurgle
			trait = plaguelord
			society_member_of = cult_of_nurgle
			society_member_of = cult_of_nurgle2
			society_member_of = cult_of_nurgle3
		}
		OR = {
			had_character_flag = { flag = manifest_tuberculosis days = 60 }
			NOT = { has_character_flag = manifest_tuberculosis }
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {

		modifier = { factor = 2.0 NOT = { base_health = 4.1 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.66 NOT = { age = 20 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.75 NOT = { age = 30 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.75 NOT = { age = 40 } }
		modifier = { factor = 2.0 is_long_lived_character = no age = 50 }
		modifier = { factor = 2.0 is_long_lived_character = no age = 70 }
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			age = 100
			AND = { society_rank == 4 society_member_of = knights_of_bretonnia }
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			age = 150
			OR = { trait = creature_dwarf trait = creature_chaos_dwarf }
		}
		modifier = { factor = 2 age = 500 trait = creature_elf }
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			age = 2000
			OR = { trait = immortal has_artifact = elixir_of_eternal_youth }
		}
		modifier = { factor = 2.0 is_ill = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 4.0 trait = infirm }
		modifier = { factor = 5.0 trait = incapable }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtreatment2
		remove_trait = has_bone_auge
		scrying_omen_fulfilled_by_removing_disease_effect = yes
		hidden_tooltip = {
			remove_treatment_modifiers_effect = yes
			if = { limit = { ai = no } set_character_flag = achievement_i_got_better }
		}
	}
}

# treatment.78 Recovery from the Tomb Rot
# Removes has_tomb_rot, given by the Warhammer epidemics and Nurgle's gifts,
# which had no way to wear off. Nurgle's faithful never shake it off. Restored
# alongside the vanilla recoveries; Nurgle's Rot itself stays incurable.
# Scope: ROOT is the sick character. Mean time to happen on the sick pulse.
character_event = {
	id = treatment.78
	picture = GFX_evt_recovery
	desc = EVTDESCtreatment78
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_sick = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = has_tomb_rot
		NOR = {
			religion = nurgle
			trait = plaguelord
			society_member_of = cult_of_nurgle
			society_member_of = cult_of_nurgle2
			society_member_of = cult_of_nurgle3
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 8
		modifier = { factor = 0.75 base_health = 5.1 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 base_health = 6.0 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 base_health = 7.0 }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 NOT = { base_health = 5.0 } }
		modifier = { factor = 2.0 NOT = { base_health = 4.1 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.66 NOT = { age = 20 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.75 NOT = { age = 30 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.75 NOT = { age = 40 } }
		modifier = { factor = 2.0 is_long_lived_character = no age = 50 }
		modifier = { factor = 2.0 is_long_lived_character = no age = 70 }
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			age = 100
			AND = { society_rank == 4 society_member_of = knights_of_bretonnia }
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			age = 150
			OR = { trait = creature_dwarf trait = creature_chaos_dwarf }
		}
		modifier = { factor = 2 age = 500 trait = creature_elf }
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			age = 2000
			OR = { trait = immortal has_artifact = elixir_of_eternal_youth }
		}
		modifier = { factor = 2.0 is_ill = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 4.0 trait = infirm }
		modifier = { factor = 5.0 trait = incapable }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtreatment2
		remove_trait = has_tomb_rot
		scrying_omen_fulfilled_by_removing_disease_effect = yes
		hidden_tooltip = {
			remove_treatment_modifiers_effect = yes
			if = { limit = { ai = no } set_character_flag = achievement_i_got_better }
		}
	}
}

# treatment.32 Ping the court physician
# Hidden. The court physician looks at a sick patient: symptoms go to
# treatment.46, a new leper to treatment.63, a disease to treatment.36. An
# epidemic victim in seclusion needs the liege's leave first (seclusion.15).
# Scope: ROOT is the court physician, FROM the patient. Fired by the symptom
# and diagnosis events, health.3 and the employ_court_physician decision.
character_event = {
	id = treatment.32
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_minor_title = title_court_physician
		FROM = {
			prisoner = no
			OR = {
				character_disease_trigger = yes
				AND = { trait = leper has_character_flag = new_leper_character }
				AND = {
					NOR = {
						has_character_modifier = symptom_treatment_1
						has_character_modifier = symptom_treatment_2
						has_character_modifier = symptom_treatment_3
						has_character_modifier = symptom_treatment_4
					}
					OR = {
						NOT = { has_character_flag = being_treated }
						had_character_flag = { flag = being_treated days = 20 }
					}
					OR = {
						trait = cough
						trait = fever
						trait = diarrhea
						trait = vomiting
						trait = chest_pain
						trait = cramps
						trait = rash
						trait = headache
						trait = abdominal_pain
						trait = fatigue
						trait = malaise
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					NOT = { has_character_flag = seclusion_treatment_approved }
					has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
					has_epidemic_disease_trigger = yes
					host = { NOT = { character = PREV } }
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = court_physician
			FROM = { save_event_target_as = epidemic_victim }
			liege = { character_event = { id = seclusion.15 } }
			break = yes
		}
		FROM = { set_character_flag = being_treated }
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					OR = {
						trait = cough
						trait = fever
						trait = diarrhea
						trait = vomiting
						trait = chest_pain
						trait = cramps
						trait = rash
						trait = headache
						trait = abdominal_pain
						trait = fatigue
						trait = malaise
					}
				}
			}
			FROM = { character_event = { id = treatment.46 days = 1 random = 2 } }
			break = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { trait = leper has_character_flag = new_leper_character } }
			FROM = { character_event = { id = treatment.63 days = 2 random = 4 } }
			break = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { character_disease_trigger = yes } }
			FROM = { character_event = { id = treatment.36 days = 2 random = 4 } }
		}
	}
}

# treatment.33 Seclusion: physician allowed in
# Hidden. After the liege allows it, sends the physician back to treatment.32.
# Scope: ROOT is the liege. Fired by the seclusion events.
character_event = {
	id = treatment.33
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	# We target the court physician and send event back
	immediate = {
		event_target:court_physician = { character_event = { id = treatment.32 } }
	}
}

# treatment.36 The court physician offers treatment
# The physician offers a treatment for the disease: experimental, normal,
# cautious or mystic, each weighed by the physician's skill and traits.
# Scope: ROOT is the patient, FROM the physician. Fired by treatment.32.
character_event = {
	id = treatment.36
	picture = GFX_evt_court_physician
	desc = EVTDESCtreatment36
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		character_disease_trigger = yes
	}

	immediate = {
		remove_character_modifier = symptom_treatment_1
		remove_character_modifier = symptom_treatment_2
		remove_character_modifier = symptom_treatment_3
		remove_character_modifier = symptom_treatment_4
	}

	# Experimental treatment from great doctor
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtreatment36
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = EVTOPTAtreatment36_TT # Explain experimental treatment
		}

		trigger = {
			is_adult = yes
			OR = { FROM = { learning = 18 } FROM = { physician_good_trigger = yes } }
			NOR = { trait = craven trait = brave }
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 15

			modifier = { factor = 0 health = 2.5 }
			modifier = { factor = 0 trait = food_poisoning health = 1.5 }
			modifier = {
				factor = 10
				is_unimportant_trigger = yes
				OR = { FROM = { physician_bad_trigger = yes } FROM = { NOT = { learning = 10 } } }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = wroth }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = arbitrary }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = shy }
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 trait = cynical }
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 trait = mystic }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = humble }
		}

		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				#Good  experimental treatment
				40 = {
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 is_strong_trigger = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { trait = physician } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { learning = 25 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { learning = 20 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { learning = 15 } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.8 FROM = { NOT = { learning = 15 } } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.8 FROM = { NOT = { learning = 10 } } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.8 is_weak_trigger = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 0.6 FROM = { physician_bad_trigger = yes } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { physician_good_trigger = yes } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = 0 } } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = 25 } } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = 50 } } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = 75 } } }
					modifier = { factor = 0 FROM = { society_member_of = cult_of_nurgle } }

					random_list = {
						#Regular treatment

						#Drastic treatment
						20 = {
							modifier = {
								factor = 4
								OR = {
									FROM = { NOT = { learning = 15 } }
									FROM = { trait = wroth }
									FROM = { trait = arbitrary }
									FROM = { trait = brave }
									FROM = { trait = ambitious }
									FROM = { learning = 25 }
									FROM = { physician_good_trigger = yes }
								}
							}
							modifier = { factor = 0 FROM = { society_member_of = cult_of_nurgle } }

						}
					}
				}
				#Bad experimental treatment
				60 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0.8 is_strong_trigger = yes }

					modifier = { factor = 0.8 FROM = { learning = 15 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { NOT = { learning = 15 } } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { NOT = { learning = 10 } } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 is_weak_trigger = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { physician_bad_trigger = yes } }
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.2
						FROM = { NOT = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = 0 } } }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.2
						FROM = { NOT = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = -25 } } }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.2
						FROM = { NOT = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = -50 } } }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.2
						FROM = { NOT = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = -75 } } }
					}
					modifier = { factor = 0.8 FROM = { physician_good_trigger = yes } }

					random_list = {

			modifier = { factor = 3 trait = craven }

			modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = humble }

			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = trusting }
		}

		trigger = { OR = { is_adult = no trait = craven trait = paranoid } NOT = { trait = brave } }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				80 = {
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { trait = physician } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { learning = 25 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { learning = 20 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { learning = 15 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { learning = 10 } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.8 FROM = { NOT = { learning = 10 } } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { physician_good_trigger = yes } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = 0 } } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = 25 } } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = 50 } } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = 75 } } }
					set_character_flag = good_treatment_outcome
				}
				20 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0.8 FROM = { learning = 25 } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.8 FROM = { learning = 20 } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.8 FROM = { learning = 15 } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.8 FROM = { learning = 10 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { NOT = { learning = 10 } } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { physician_bad_trigger = yes } }
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.2
						FROM = { NOT = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = 0 } } }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.2
						FROM = { NOT = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = -25 } } }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.2
						FROM = { NOT = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = -50 } } }
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.2
						FROM = { NOT = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = -75 } } }
					}
					set_character_flag = bad_treatment_outcome
				}
			}
			character_event = { id = treatment.43 days = 3 }
		}
	}

	# Do nothing
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDtreatment36

		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }

		custom_tooltip = {
			text = EVTOPTDtreatment36_TT # Explain you'd rather be left alone
			if = { limit = { trait = craven } prestige = -20 break = yes }
			if = {
				limit = { NOR = { trait = craven trait = brave trait = berserker } }
				add_trait = craven
				break = yes
			}
			if = { limit = { trait = brave } remove_trait = brave }
		}
	}
}

		clr_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment42_7_FLAG
		clr_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment42_8_FLAG

		clr_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment42_11_FLAG
		clr_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment42_13_FLAG
	}
}

		name = EVTOPTAtreatment43

			limit = { NOR = { trait = cancer trait = gout trait = syphilitic } }

			add_character_modifier = { name = successful_treatment_1 duration = 1825 }

		clr_character_flag = being_treated
		set_character_flag = recent_treatment
		clr_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment43_1_FLAG
		clr_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment43_2_FLAG
		clr_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment43_3_FLAG
		clr_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment43_4_FLAG
		clr_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment43_5_FLAG
		clr_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment43_6_FLAG

		clr_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment43_8_FLAG
		clr_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment43_9_FLAG
		clr_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment43_10_FLAG

	option = { #nothing happens

# treatment.48 Mystic treatment
# The outcome of a mystic or magical treatment, which may leave the patient's
# mind the worse for it.
# Scope: ROOT is the patient, FROM the physician. Fired by treatment.36.
character_event = {
	id = treatment.48
	picture = GFX_evt_recovery
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCtreatment48_1
		trigger = { has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_1_FLAG }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCtreatment48_2
		trigger = { has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_2_FLAG }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCtreatment48_3
		trigger = { has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_3_FLAG }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCtreatment48_4
		trigger = { has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_4_FLAG }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCtreatment48_5
		trigger = { has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_5_FLAG }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCtreatment48_6
		trigger = { has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_6_FLAG }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCtreatment48_7
		trigger = { has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_7_FLAG }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCtreatment48_8
		trigger = { has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_8_FLAG }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCtreatment48_9
		trigger = { has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_9_FLAG }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCtreatment48_10
		trigger = { has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_10_FLAG }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCtreatment48_11
		trigger = { has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_11_FLAG }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCtreatment48_12
		trigger = { has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_12_FLAG }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			10 = { # Mirrors and candles
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_flag = MYSTIC_TERRIBLE_OUTCOME }
				set_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_1_FLAG
			}

			10 = { # Sacrificed animals, rubbed in ashes
				modifier = { factor = 0 AND = { trait = paranoid trait = possessed } }
				set_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_2_FLAG
			}

			10 = { # Conjuring, burnt fingers
				modifier = { factor = 0 trait = one_handed }
				set_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_3_FLAG
			}

			10 = { # Improv greatest poem
				modifier = { factor = 0 trait = poet }
				set_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_4_FLAG
			}

			10 = { # Bloodbath
				modifier = { factor = 0 trait = cruel }
				set_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_5_FLAG
			}

			5 = { # "special brew"
				modifier = { factor = 0 trait = drunkard }
				set_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_6_FLAG
			}

			10 = { # walk on fire
				modifier = { factor = 0 trait = brave }
				set_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_7_FLAG
			}

			10 = { # physician tries to drown you
				modifier = { factor = 0 FROMFROM = { NOR = { trait = zealous trait = cruel } } }
				set_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_8_FLAG
			}

			5 = { # physician sacrifices your child
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					NOT = {
						any_courtier = {
							is_child_of = ROOT
							is_ill = no
							NOT = { trait = inbred }
							is_abroad = no
						}
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					FROMFROM = {
						NOR = { trait = zealous trait = cruel trait = arbitrary trait = impaler }
					}
				}
				set_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_9_FLAG
			}

			10 = { # am I the beast?
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					OR = { trait = possessed trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 }
				}
				set_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_10_FLAG
			}

			10 = { set_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_11_FLAG } # the rat will carry your burdens
			10 = { set_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_12_FLAG } # physician suicide
		}
	}

	option = {
		set_character_flag = recent_treatment
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTBtreatment48
			trigger = { has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_8_FLAG }
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTCtreatment48
			trigger = { has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_9_FLAG }
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTDtreatment48
			trigger = { has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_12_FLAG }
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTEtreatment48
			trigger = { has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_3_FLAG }
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTAtreatment48
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_8_FLAG
					has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_9_FLAG
					has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_12_FLAG
					has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_3_FLAG
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = MYSTIC_AMAZING_OUTCOME }

			#Chance to remove the disease!
			custom_tooltip = { text = CURED_RIP_TOOLTIP }

			hidden_tooltip = {
				if = { limit = { trait = flu } remove_trait = flu }
				if = { limit = { trait = dysentery } remove_trait = dysentery }
				if = { limit = { trait = gout } remove_trait = gout }
				if = { limit = { trait = food_poisoning } remove_trait = food_poisoning }
				if = { limit = { trait = rabies } remove_trait = rabies }
				if = { limit = { trait = cancer } remove_trait = cancer }
				if = { limit = { trait = pneumonic } remove_trait = pneumonic }
				if = { limit = { trait = has_creeping_buboes } remove_trait = has_creeping_buboes }
				if = { limit = { trait = has_rattlelung } remove_trait = has_rattlelung }
				if = { limit = { trait = has_bone_auge } remove_trait = has_bone_auge }
				if = { limit = { trait = has_tomb_rot } remove_trait = has_tomb_rot }
				if = {
					limit = { trait = syphilitic }
					remove_trait = syphilitic
					set_character_flag = immune_syphilis
				}
				if = {
					limit = { trait = has_tuberculosis }
					remove_trait = has_tuberculosis
					set_character_flag = immune_tuberculosis
				}
				if = {
					limit = { trait = has_typhoid_fever }
					remove_trait = has_typhoid_fever
					set_character_flag = immune_typhoid_fever
				}
				if = {
					limit = { trait = has_typhus }
					remove_trait = has_typhus
					set_character_flag = immune_typhus
				}
				if = {
					limit = { trait = has_bubonic_plague ai = no }
					remove_trait = has_bubonic_plague
					set_character_flag = immune_bubonic_plague
					set_character_flag = achievement_i_got_better
				}
				if = {
					limit = { trait = has_bubonic_plague ai = yes }
					remove_trait = has_bubonic_plague
					set_character_flag = immune_bubonic_plague
				}
				if = {
					limit = { trait = has_measles }
					remove_trait = has_measles
					set_character_flag = immune_measles
				}
				if = {
					limit = { trait = has_small_pox }
					remove_trait = has_small_pox
					set_character_flag = immune_smallpox
				}
				if = {
					limit = { trait = has_aztec_disease }
					remove_trait = has_aztec_disease
					set_character_flag = immune_aztec_disease
				}
				remove_treatment_modifiers_effect = yes
				clear_disease_level_effect = yes
				remove_disease_flags_effect = yes
				set_character_flag = seen_the_light_of_modern_medicine
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = MYSTIC_DECENT_OUTCOME
				NOR = { trait = cancer trait = gout trait = syphilitic }
			}
			add_character_modifier = { name = mystic_treatment_decent duration = 730 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = MYSTIC_DECENT_OUTCOME
				OR = { trait = cancer trait = gout trait = syphilitic }
			}
			add_character_modifier = { name = mystic_treatment_decent duration = 1825 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = MYSTIC_POOR_OUTCOME
				NOR = { trait = cancer trait = gout trait = syphilitic }
			}
			add_character_modifier = { name = mystic_treatment_poor duration = 365 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = MYSTIC_POOR_OUTCOME
				OR = { trait = cancer trait = gout trait = syphilitic }
			}
			add_character_modifier = { name = mystic_treatment_poor duration = 1825 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = MYSTIC_TERRIBLE_OUTCOME
				NOR = { trait = cancer trait = gout trait = syphilitic }
			}
			custom_tooltip = { text = treatment48_TERRIBLE_TT }
			add_character_modifier = { name = mystic_treatment_terrible duration = 365 }
			hidden_tooltip = {
				random = {
					chance = 10
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { trait = possessed } }
						character_event = { id = 38315 }
					}
				}
				random = {
					chance = 10
					if = {
						limit = { NOR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 } }
						character_event = { id = 38304 }
					}
				}
				random = {
					chance = 10
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { trait = depressed } }
						character_event = { id = 38288 }
					}
				}
				random = {
					chance = 25
					if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = stressed } } character_event = { id = 38282 } }
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = MYSTIC_TERRIBLE_OUTCOME
				OR = { trait = cancer trait = gout trait = syphilitic }
			}
			custom_tooltip = { text = treatment48_TERRIBLE_TT }
			add_character_modifier = { name = mystic_treatment_terrible duration = 1825 }
			hidden_tooltip = {
				random = {
					chance = 10
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { trait = possessed } }
						character_event = { id = 38315 }
					}
				}
				random = {
					chance = 10
					if = {
						limit = { NOR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 } }
						character_event = { id = 38304 }
					}
				}
				random = {
					chance = 10
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { trait = depressed } }
						character_event = { id = 38288 }
					}
				}
				random = {
					chance = 25
					if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = stressed } } character_event = { id = 38282 } }
				}
			}
		}

		#Specific effects from the chosen event desc
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_1_FLAG }

			trigger_switch = {
				on_trigger = trait
				paranoid = { add_trait = trusting }
				trusting = { add_trait = paranoid }
			}
			trigger_switch = {
				on_trigger = trait
				ambitious = { add_trait = content }
				content = { add_trait = ambitious }
			}
			trigger_switch = {
				on_trigger = trait
				humble = { add_trait = proud }
				proud = { add_trait = humble }
			}
			trigger_switch = {
				on_trigger = trait
				shy = { add_trait = gregarious }
				gregarious = { add_trait = shy }
			}
			trigger_switch = {
				on_trigger = trait
				temperate = { add_trait = gluttonous }
				gluttonous = { add_trait = temperate }
			}
			trigger_switch = {
				on_trigger = trait
				charitable = { add_trait = greedy }
				greedy = { add_trait = charitable }
			}
			trigger_switch = {
				on_trigger = trait
				diligent = { add_trait = slothful }
				slothful = { add_trait = diligent }
			}
			trigger_switch = {
				on_trigger = trait
				envious = { add_trait = kind }
				kind = { add_trait = envious }
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_2_FLAG }
			if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = paranoid } } add_trait = paranoid }

			random = {
				chance = 25
				if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = possessed } } add_trait = possessed }
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_3_FLAG }
			if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = one_handed } } add_trait = one_handed }
			if = { limit = { NOR = { trait = wroth trait = patient } } add_trait = wroth }
			if = { limit = { trait = patient } remove_trait = patient }
		}

		if = { limit = { has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_4_FLAG } add_trait = poet }
		if = { limit = { has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_5_FLAG } add_trait = cruel }
		if = { limit = { has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_6_FLAG } add_trait = drunkard }

		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_7_FLAG }
			add_trait = brave
			if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = zealous } } add_trait = zealous }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_8_FLAG }
			# DROWNING
			opinion = { who = FROMFROM modifier = opinion_traitor }
			if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = paranoid } } add_trait = paranoid }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_9_FLAG }

			opinion = { who = FROMFROM modifier = opinion_traitor }

			random_courtier = {
				limit = { is_child_of = ROOT is_ill = no NOT = { trait = inbred } is_abroad = no }
				if = { limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } antideath_effect = yes break = yes }
				death = { death_reason = death_murder_unknown killer = FROMFROM }
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_10_FLAG }
			if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = possessed } } add_trait = possessed }

			random = {
				chance = 25
				if = {
					limit = { NOR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 } }
					add_trait = lunatic_1
					set_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 6 }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_11_FLAG }

			if = { limit = { NOR = { trait = content trait = ambitious } } add_trait = content }
			if = { limit = { trait = ambitious } remove_trait = ambitious }

			if = { limit = { NOR = { trait = kind trait = envious } } add_trait = kind }
			if = { limit = { trait = envious } remove_trait = envious }

			if = { limit = { NOR = { trait = honest trait = deceitful } } add_trait = honest }
			if = { limit = { trait = deceitful } remove_trait = deceitful }
			if = { limit = { NOR = { trait = patient trait = wroth } } add_trait = patient }
			if = { limit = { trait = wroth } remove_trait = wroth }
			if = { limit = { NOR = { trait = humble trait = proud } } add_trait = humble }
			if = { limit = { trait = proud } remove_trait = proud }
			if = { limit = { NOR = { trait = charitable trait = greedy } } add_trait = charitable }
			if = { limit = { trait = greedy } remove_trait = greedy }
			if = {
				limit = { NOR = { trait = temperate trait = gluttonous } }
				add_trait = temperate
			}
			if = { limit = { trait = gluttonous } remove_trait = gluttonous }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_12_FLAG }

			FROMFROM = {
				if = { limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } antideath_effect = yes break = yes }
				death = { death_reason = death_suicide }
			}

			if = { limit = { trait = humble } add_trait = humble }

			random = {
				chance = 25
				if = {
					limit = { NOR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 } }
					add_trait = lunatic_1
					set_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 6 }
				}
			}
		}

		clr_character_flag = MYSTIC_AMAZING_OUTCOME
		clr_character_flag = MYSTIC_DECENT_OUTCOME
		clr_character_flag = MYSTIC_POOR_OUTCOME
		clr_character_flag = MYSTIC_TERRIBLE_OUTCOME
		clr_character_flag = good_treatment_outcome
		clr_character_flag = bad_treatment_outcome
		clr_character_flag = being_treated
		clr_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_1_FLAG
		clr_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_2_FLAG
		clr_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_3_FLAG
		clr_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_4_FLAG
		clr_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_5_FLAG
		clr_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_6_FLAG
		clr_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_7_FLAG
		clr_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_8_FLAG
		clr_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_9_FLAG
		clr_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_10_FLAG
		clr_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_11_FLAG
		clr_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment48_12_FLAG
	}

# treatment.46 Treating the symptoms
# The physician guesses which disease the symptoms point to (with more or less
# certainty, or a horse doctor's bluster) and offers a remedy.
# Scope: ROOT is the patient, FROM the physician. Fired by treatment.32.
character_event = {
	id = treatment.46
	picture = GFX_evt_recovery
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCtreatment46_has_tuberculosis
		trigger = { has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment46_has_tuberculosis_flag }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCtreatment46_has_tuberculosis_horse
		trigger = { has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment46_has_tuberculosis_flag_horse }

		trigger = { has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment46_not_serious_flag_horse }

		text = EVTDESCtreatment46_not_serious_certain
		trigger = { has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment46_not_serious_flag_certain }

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCtreatment46_not_serious_sure
		trigger = { has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment46_not_serious_flag_sure }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCtreatment46_not_serious_uncertain
		trigger = { has_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment46_not_serious_flag_uncertain }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			##Symptoms
			trait = cough
			trait = fever
			trait = diarrhea
			trait = vomiting
			trait = chest_pain
			trait = cramps
			trait = rash
			trait = headache
			trait = abdominal_pain
			trait = fatigue
			trait = malaise
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			40 = {
				modifier = { factor = 2 num_of_symptoms = 2 }
				modifier = { factor = 2 num_of_symptoms = 3 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { OR = { trait = scholar trait = mutazilite } } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { is_smart_trigger = yes } }
				modifier = { factor = 5 FROM = { culture = horse } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { physician_good_trigger = yes } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { learning = 25 } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { learning = 20 } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { learning = 15 } }
				modifier = { factor = 0.8 FROM = { NOT = { learning = 15 } } }
				modifier = { factor = 0.8 FROM = { NOT = { learning = 10 } } }
				set_character_flag = correct_diagnosis
			}
			60 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 FROM = { culture = horse } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { is_dumb_trigger = yes } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { physician_bad_trigger = yes } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { NOT = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = 0 } } } }
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.2
					FROM = { NOT = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = -25 } } }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.2
					FROM = { NOT = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = -50 } } }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.2
					FROM = { NOT = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = -75 } } }
				}
				modifier = { factor = 0.8 FROM = { learning = 25 } }
				modifier = { factor = 0.8 FROM = { learning = 20 } }
				modifier = { factor = 0.8 FROM = { learning = 15 } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { NOT = { learning = 15 } } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { NOT = { learning = 10 } } }
				set_character_flag = faulty_diagnosis
			}
		}

		#Now we check to see what disease the character has
		random_list = {
			10 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					AND = {
						has_character_flag = correct_diagnosis
						NOT = { has_character_flag = got_tuberculosis }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					AND = {
						has_character_flag = faulty_diagnosis
						has_character_flag = got_tuberculosis
					}
				}

				if = {
					limit = { #horse
						FROM = { culture = horse }
					}
					set_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment46_has_tuberculosis_flag_horse
					break = yes
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_character_flag = correct_diagnosis #Really good doctors know for sure
						FROM = {
							OR = {
								learning = 25
								AND = { physician_good_trigger = yes learning = 20 }
							}
						}
					}
					set_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment46_has_tuberculosis_flag_certain
					break = yes
				}
				if = { #Good doctors are pretty sure
					limit = {
						FROM = {
							OR = {
								learning = 20
								AND = { physician_good_trigger = yes learning = 15 }
							}
						}
					}
					set_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment46_has_tuberculosis_flag_sure
					break = yes
				}
				if = {
					limit = { # awful doctors are uncertain
						FROM = {
							OR = {
								AND = { NOT = { learning = 10 } physician_bad_trigger = no }
								AND = { NOT = { learning = 15 } physician_bad_trigger = yes }
							}
						}
					}
					set_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment46_has_tuberculosis_flag_uncertain
					break = yes
				}

				set_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment46_has_tuberculosis_flag #otherwise, doctors are fairly sure
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					AND = {
						has_character_flag = correct_diagnosis
						NOT = { has_character_flag = got_typhoid_fever }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					AND = {
						has_character_flag = faulty_diagnosis
						has_character_flag = got_typhoid_fever
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { #horse
						FROM = { culture = horse }
					}
					set_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment46_has_typhoid_fever_flag_horse
					break = yes
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_character_flag = correct_diagnosis #Really good doctors know for sure
						FROM = {
							OR = {
								learning = 25
								AND = { physician_good_trigger = yes learning = 20 }
							}
						}
					}
					set_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment46_has_typhoid_fever_flag_certain
					break = yes
				}
				if = { #Good doctors are pretty sure
					limit = {
						FROM = {
							OR = {
								learning = 20
								AND = { physician_good_trigger = yes learning = 15 }
							}
						}
					}
					set_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment46_has_typhoid_fever_flag_sure
					break = yes
				}
				if = {
					limit = { # awful doctors are uncertain
						FROM = {
							OR = {
								AND = { NOT = { learning = 10 } physician_bad_trigger = no }
								AND = { NOT = { learning = 15 } physician_bad_trigger = yes }
							}
						}
					}
					set_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment46_has_typhoid_fever_flag_uncertain
					break = yes
				}

				set_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment46_has_typhoid_fever_flag
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					AND = {
						has_character_flag = correct_diagnosis
						NOT = { has_character_flag = got_typhus }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					AND = { has_character_flag = faulty_diagnosis has_character_flag = got_typhus }
				}

				if = {
					limit = { #horse
						FROM = { culture = horse }
					}
					set_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment46_has_typhus_flag_horse
					break = yes
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_character_flag = correct_diagnosis #Really good doctors know for sure
						FROM = {
							OR = {
								learning = 25
								AND = { physician_good_trigger = yes learning = 20 }
							}
						}
					}
					set_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment46_has_typhus_flag_certain
					break = yes
				}
				if = { #Good doctors are pretty sure
					limit = {
						FROM = {
							OR = {
								learning = 20
								AND = { physician_good_trigger = yes learning = 15 }
							}
						}
					}
					set_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment46_has_typhus_flag_sure
					break = yes
				}
				if = {
					limit = { # awful doctors are uncertain
						FROM = {
							OR = {
								AND = { NOT = { learning = 10 } physician_bad_trigger = no }
								AND = { NOT = { learning = 15 } physician_bad_trigger = yes }
							}
						}
					}
					set_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment46_has_typhus_flag_uncertain
					break = yes
				}

				set_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment46_has_typhus_flag
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					AND = {
						has_character_flag = correct_diagnosis
						NOT = { has_character_flag = got_bubonic_plague }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					AND = {
						has_character_flag = faulty_diagnosis
						has_character_flag = got_bubonic_plague
					}
				}

				if = {
					limit = { #horse
						FROM = { culture = horse }
					}
					set_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment46_has_bubonic_plague_flag_horse
					break = yes
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_character_flag = correct_diagnosis #Really good doctors know for sure
						FROM = {
							OR = {
								learning = 25
								AND = { physician_good_trigger = yes learning = 20 }
							}
						}
					}
					set_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment46_has_bubonic_plague_flag_certain
					break = yes
				}
				if = { #Good doctors are pretty sure
					limit = {
						FROM = {
							OR = {
								learning = 20
								AND = { physician_good_trigger = yes learning = 15 }
							}
						}
					}
					set_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment46_has_bubonic_plague_flag_sure
					break = yes
				}
				if = {
					limit = { # awful doctors are uncertain
						FROM = {
							OR = {
								AND = { NOT = { learning = 10 } physician_bad_trigger = no }
								AND = { NOT = { learning = 15 } physician_bad_trigger = yes }
							}
						}
					}
					set_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment46_has_bubonic_plague_flag_uncertain
					break = yes
				}

				set_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment46_has_bubonic_plague_flag
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					AND = {
						has_character_flag = correct_diagnosis
						NOT = { has_character_flag = got_measles }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					AND = { has_character_flag = faulty_diagnosis has_character_flag = got_measles }
				}

				if = {
					limit = { #horse
						FROM = { culture = horse }
					}
					set_character_flag = EVTDESCtreatment46_has_measles_flag_horse
					break = yes
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_character_flag = correct_diagnosis #Really good doctors know for sure
						FROM = {
							OR = {
								learning = 25
								AND = { physician_good_trigger = yes learning = 20 }
							}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtreatment46
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = EVTOPTAtreatment46_TT #symptom treatment notification
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 is_strong_trigger = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { trait = physician } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { learning = 25 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { learning = 20 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { learning = 15 } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.8 FROM = { NOT = { learning = 15 } } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.8 FROM = { NOT = { learning = 10 } } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.8 is_weak_trigger = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_character_flag = correct_diagnosis }
					set_character_flag = good_treatment_outcome
				}
				50 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0.8 is_strong_trigger = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 0.8 FROM = { learning = 25 } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.8 FROM = { learning = 20 } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.8 FROM = { learning = 15 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { NOT = { learning = 15 } } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { NOT = { learning = 10 } } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 is_weak_trigger = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_character_flag = faulty_diagnosis }
					set_character_flag = bad_treatment_outcome
				}
			}

			if = { #If good treatment, check for quality
				limit = { has_character_flag = good_treatment_outcome }
				random_list = {
					100 = {
						modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { learning = 25 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { learning = 20 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { learning = 15 } }
						modifier = { factor = 0.8 FROM = { NOT = { learning = 15 } } }
						modifier = { factor = 0.8 FROM = { NOT = { learning = 10 } } }
						modifier = { factor = 0 FROM = { society_member_of = cult_of_nurgle } }
						set_character_flag = best_symptom_treatment
					}
					100 = {
						modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { learning = 25 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { learning = 20 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { learning = 15 } }
						modifier = { factor = 0.8 FROM = { NOT = { learning = 15 } } }
						modifier = { factor = 0.8 FROM = { NOT = { learning = 10 } } }
						set_character_flag = good_symptom_treatment
					}
					100 = { set_character_flag = ok_symptom_treatment }
					100 = {
						modifier = { factor = 0.8 FROM = { learning = 25 } }
						modifier = { factor = 0.8 FROM = { learning = 20 } }
						modifier = { factor = 0.8 FROM = { learning = 15 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { NOT = { learning = 15 } } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { NOT = { learning = 10 } } }
						set_character_flag = poor_symptom_treatment
					}
				}
			}

			character_event = { id = treatment.47 days = 3 }
			#Clear all flags
			clr_character_flag = faulty_diagnosis
			clr_character_flag = correct_diagnosis

# treatment.61 The physician botched it
# The court physician has made a close relative worse.
# Scope: ROOT is the relative told, FROM the patient, FROMFROMFROM the
# physician. Fired by the treatment outcomes.
character_event = {
	id = treatment.61
	picture = GFX_evt_bloody_tools
	desc = EVTDESCtreatment61
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			ai = yes
			AND = {
				multiplayer = no
				war = no
				random = 75 # 25% chance
			}
		}
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = relative_treatment_reaction_timer }
		FROMFROMFROM = { NOT = { character = ROOT_FROM } }
	}

	immediate = {
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = relative_treatment_reaction_timer
			days = 30
			hidden = yes
		}
	}

	# throw them in prison
	option = {
		trigger = { NOT = { trait = wroth } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 5 }
		name = EVTOPTDtreatment61
		FROMFROMFROM = { imprison = yes }
		FROM = { opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_grateful years = 10 } }
	}

	# have their head
	option = {
		tooltip_info = wroth
		trigger = { trait = wroth }
		ai_chance = { factor = 5 }
		name = EVTOPTAtreatment61
		FROMFROMFROM = {
			if = { limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } antideath_effect = yes break = yes }
			death = { death_reason = death_execution_beheading killer = ROOT }
		}
		FROM = { opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_grateful years = 10 } }
	}

	# formal complaint
	option = {
		ai_chance = { factor = 60 }
		name = EVTOPTBtreatment61
		FROMFROMFROM = { opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_did_not_trust_me years = 5 } }
		FROM = { opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_grateful years = 5 } }
	}

	# do nothing
	option = {
		ai_chance = { factor = 35 }
		name = EVTOPTCtreatment61
		FROM = {
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = insulted years = 5 }
			random = { chance = 10 add_rival = ROOT }
		}
	}
}

# treatment.62 The physician cured them
# The court physician has cured a close relative.
# Scope: ROOT is the relative told, FROM the patient, FROMFROMFROM the
# physician. Fired by the treatment outcomes.
character_event = {
	id = treatment.62
	picture = GFX_evt_recovery
	desc = EVTDESCtreatment62
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			ai = yes
			AND = {
				multiplayer = no
				war = no
				random = 75 # 25% chance
			}
		}
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = relative_treatment_reaction_timer }
	}

	immediate = {
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = relative_treatment_reaction_timer
			days = 30
			hidden = yes
		}
	}

	#Praise them to the court
	option = {
		tooltip_info = gregarious
		trigger = { trait = gregarious }
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		name = EVTOPTCtreatment62

		reverse_opinion = { who = FROMFROMFROM modifier = opinion_was_praised years = 5 }

		random = { chance = 20 add_friend = FROMFROMFROM }

		any_courtier = {
			limit = {
				age = 16
				prisoner = no
				NOT = { character = ROOT }
				NOT = { trait = incapable }
			}
			opinion = { who = FROMFROMFROM modifier = opinion_good_work years = 5 }
		}
	}

	#Commend them
	option = {
		ai_chance = { factor = 20 }
		name = EVTOPTAtreatment62
		reverse_opinion = { who = FROMFROMFROM modifier = opinion_was_praised years = 5 }
		opinion = { who = FROMFROMFROM modifier = opinion_good_work years = 5 }
		transfer_scaled_wealth = { to = FROMFROMFROM value = 0.02 }
	}

	#It's their job
	option = {
		ai_chance = { factor = 80 modifier = { factor = 0 trait = gregarious } }
		prestige = 10
		name = EVTOPTBtreatment62
	}
}

# treatment.67 A sickly newborn: ping

	id = treatment.67

	}

# treatment.68 A sickly newborn
# The court physician is worried about a feeble newborn and offers a
# treatment, or the parent can trust to the gods.
# Scope: ROOT is the guardian, FROM the physician, sickly_infant the baby.
# Fired by treatment.67.
character_event = {
	id = treatment.68
	picture = GFX_evt_birth
	desc = EVTDESCtreatment68
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		event_target:sickly_infant = { is_alive = yes }
	}

	#Treat them!
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtreatment68
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAtreatment68_TT }

		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = treatment.69 days = 20 } } }
		event_target:sickly_infant = { show_portrait = yes }

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10

			modifier = { factor = 5 trait = kind }
			modifier = { factor = 10 current_heir = { character = event_target:sickly_infant } }
		}
	}

	#Don't do anything
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtreatment68
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBtreatment68_TT }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			random = {
				chance = 20
				modifier = {
					factor = 4
					event_target:sickly_infant = { is_unimportant_trigger = yes }
				}

				event_target:sickly_infant = {
					character_event = { id = treatment.73 days = 30 random = 100 }
				} #Child dies
			}
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1

			modifier = { factor = 50 event_target:sickly_infant = { is_unimportant_trigger = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = 20 OR = { trait = cruel trait = arbitrary } }
			modifier = {
				factor = 20
				OR = { liege = { is_tribal = yes } liege = { is_nomadic = yes } }
			}
		}
	}
}

# treatment.69 A sickly newborn: outcome
# Hidden. Picks the outcome of the treatment.
# Scope: ROOT is the guardian. Fired by treatment.68.
character_event = {
	id = treatment.69
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		event_target:sickly_infant = { trait = sickly }
	}

	option = {
		random_list = {
			#Perfect treatment
			15 = {
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 is_smart_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 physician_good_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 25 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 20 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 15 }
				modifier = { factor = 0.8 NOT = { learning = 15 } }
				modifier = { factor = 0.8 NOT = { learning = 10 } }
				FROM = { character_event = { id = treatment.70 } }
			}
			#A bit of a treatment
			35 = {
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 25 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 20 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 15 }
				modifier = { factor = 0.8 NOT = { learning = 15 } }
				modifier = { factor = 0.8 NOT = { learning = 10 } }
				FROM = { character_event = { id = treatment.71 } }
			}
			#Bad treatment
			50 = {
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 is_dumb_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 physician_bad_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 0.8 learning = 25 }
				modifier = { factor = 0.8 learning = 20 }
				modifier = { factor = 0.8 learning = 15 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 NOT = { learning = 15 } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 NOT = { learning = 10 } }
				FROM = { character_event = { id = treatment.72 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtreatment72
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAtreatment72_TT }
	}
}

# treatment.73 A sickly child dies: ping
# Hidden. Sends the news to the guardian (treatment.74).
# Scope: ROOT is the sickly child. Fired by treatment.68.
character_event = {
	id = treatment.73
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		event_target:infant_guardian = { character_event = { id = treatment.74 } }
	}
}

# treatment.74 A sickly child dies
# The child was not meant for this world, and dies.
# Scope: ROOT is the guardian, FROM the child. Fired by treatment.73.
character_event = {
	id = treatment.74
	picture = GFX_evt_birth
	desc = EVTDESCtreatment74
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { trait = sickly }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtreatment74

		FROM = { death = { death_reason = death_trait } }
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Part 6: Finding a court physician (namespace physician)
# Faction: Generic
# Serves: any ruler who takes the employ_court_physician decision.
#
#   physician.1     The search begins
#   physician.2     Hidden: who the search turns up (one of many healers,
#                   from a village drunkard to a High Elf erudite)
#   physician.3-4   The healer arrives and may be hired
#   physician.10-22 The court physician's advice: rest, a guide dog,
#                   fertility treatment, lessons, apothecaries
#   physician.30-34 A treatise on the humours
#   physician.40-61 Training the physician (herbs, books, a laboratory)
#   physician.70-71 A foreign healer-missionary in a tribal or nomad realm
#
# physician.10-71 fire from the on_yearly_pulse random list.
# Was events/rip_physician_events.txt (RIP.11050-11053), in order, and the
# physician part of rip_flavor_events.txt (RIP.4010-4061 = physician.10-61,
# RIP.4100-4101 = physician.70-71).
###############################################################################

namespace = physician

# physician.1 Searching for a physician
# Messengers ride out to find a court physician. The search turns one up some
# weeks later (physician.2).
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by the employ_court_physician decision.
character_event = {
	id = physician.1
	desc = EVTDESCphysician1
	picture = GFX_evt_courier
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = looking_for_court_physician
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAphysician1
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = physician.2 days = 10 random = 10 } }
	}
}

# physician.2 A physician is found
# Hidden. Rolls which kind of healer the search found (a village quack, a
# ship's surgeon, a Strigany, a Cathayan, an Arabyan, a High Elf...), creates
# them and offers them (physician.3). The Ormazdan doctor and the Ivory Road
# merchant-preacher are restored (their hire options were left with no way to
# roll them).
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by physician.1 or symptom.61.
character_event = {
	id = physician.2
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		clr_character_flag = looking_for_court_physician
		set_character_flag = found_court_physician
		random_list = {
			4 = { # Drunk Village Physician (Unschooled, 18+ Learning)
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					AND = {
						has_character_flag = got_physician_village_drunkard
						NOT = {
							had_character_flag = {
								flag = got_physician_village_drunkard
								days = 3650
							}
						}
					}
				}
				random_list = {
					75 = { # Same Religion & Culture as ROOT
						create_character = {
							religion = ROOT
							culture = ROOT
							dynasty = none
							age = 35
							random_traits = yes
							female = no
							attributes = { learning = 20 }
							health = 6
							trait = detached_priest
							trait = drunkard
							flag = is_court_physician
							flag = physician_village_drunkard
						}
						new_character = {
							physician_new_character_effect = yes # racial trait, no marriage or concubinage
							set_character_flag = no_court_invites

							remove_trait = imbecile
							remove_trait = slow

								limit = { religion_group = ormazd_gods_group }

						}
					}

						modifier = {
							factor = 0
							NOR = {
								religion_group = old_world_group
								religion_group = ormazd_gods_group
								religion_group = necromantic_group
							}
						}
						create_character = {
							culture = albion_main
							religion = old_faith
							dynasty = culture
							age = 35
							random_traits = yes
							female = no
							attributes = { learning = 20 }
							health = 6
							trait = detached_priest
							trait = drunkard
							flag = ai_flag_refuse_conversion
							flag = is_court_physician
							flag = physician_village_drunkard
						}
						new_character = {
							physician_new_character_effect = yes # racial trait, no marriage or concubinage
							set_character_flag = no_court_invites
							remove_trait = temperate
							remove_trait = scholar

					remove_trait = architect
					remove_trait = gardener
					remove_trait = schemer
					remove_trait = impaler
					remove_trait = seducer
					remove_trait = seductress
					remove_trait = mystic
					save_event_target_as = recruited_physician_target
					character_event = { id = physician.3 }
				}
			}
			4 = { # Ormazdan Doctor (Physician, Scholar); restored, was the Mazdan doctor
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					AND = {
						has_character_flag = got_physician_mazdan_doctor
						NOT = {
							had_character_flag = { flag = got_physician_mazdan_doctor days = 3650 }
						}
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					OR = {
						NOT = { trait = creature_human }
						NOR = {
							religion_group = old_world_group
							religion_group = ormazd_gods_group
						}
					}
				}
				modifier = { factor = 2 religion_group = ormazd_gods_group }
				create_character = {
					culture = ROOT
					religion = ormazd
					dynasty = none
					age = 45
					random_traits = yes
					female = no
					attributes = { learning = 9 }
					health = 6
					trait = physician
					trait = scholar
					trait = patient
					flag = is_court_physician
					flag = physician_mazdan_doctor
					flag = ai_flag_refuse_conversion
				}
				new_character = {
					physician_new_character_effect = yes # racial trait, no marriage or concubinage
					remove_trait = mystic
					remove_trait = socializer
					remove_trait = gamer
					remove_trait = hedonist
					remove_trait = duelist
					remove_trait = hunter
					remove_trait = strategist
					remove_trait = impaler
					remove_trait = seducer
					remove_trait = seductress
					physician_quack_roll_effect = yes
					save_event_target_as = recruited_physician_target
					character_event = { id = physician.3 }
				}
			}
			3 = { # Ivory Road Merchant-Preacher (Mystic); restored, was the Manichean merchant
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					AND = {
						has_character_flag = got_physician_manichean_merchant
						NOT = {
							had_character_flag = {
								flag = got_physician_manichean_merchant
								days = 3650
							}
						}
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					OR = {
						NOT = { trait = creature_human }
						religion = draconite
						NOT = {
							any_realm_province = {
								OR = {
									trade_route = ivory_road
									trade_route = old_silk_road
									trade_route = khyprian_road
									trade_route = spice_route
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				create_character = {
					religion = draconite
					culture = cathayan
					dynasty = none
					age = 40
					random_traits = yes
					female = no
					attributes = { diplomacy = 7 learning = 7 }
					health = 6
					trait = mystic
					trait = zealous
					trait = gregarious
					flag = is_court_physician
					flag = physician_manichean_merchant
					flag = ai_flag_refuse_conversion
				}
				new_character = {
					physician_new_character_effect = yes # racial trait, no marriage or concubinage
					remove_trait = cynical
					remove_trait = shy
					remove_trait = scholar
					remove_trait = duelist
					remove_trait = hunter
					remove_trait = impaler
					remove_trait = theologian
					random_list = {
						60 = { }
						40 = { add_trait = physician }
					}
					physician_quack_roll_effect = yes
					save_event_target_as = recruited_physician_target
					character_event = { id = physician.3 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# physician.3 Physician ping
# Hidden. Sends the new physician back to the ruler (physician.4).
# Scope: ROOT is the new physician, FROM the ruler. Fired by physician.2.
character_event = {
	id = physician.3

	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { character_event = { id = physician.4 } }
	}
}
