###############################################################################
# The court physician's advice (physician.10-22)
###############################################################################

# physician.10 Take it easy
# The court physician bursts in and orders a stressed ruler to rest.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, physician_target their court physician. Fired by
# the on_yearly_pulse random list.
character_event = {
	id = physician.10
	desc = EVTDESCphysician10
	picture = GFX_evt_court_physician
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	only_playable = yes
	prisoner = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOR = {
			has_character_flag = stress_relief
			is_tribal = yes
			is_nomadic = yes
			trait = slothful
		}
		OR = { NOT = { health = 5 } trait = stressed trait = diligent trait = wroth }
		any_courtier_or_vassal = {
			liege = { character = ROOT }
			has_minor_title = title_court_physician
			OR = { has_character_flag = is_court_physician trait = physician }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1

		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = diligent }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = stressed }
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			any_courtier_or_vassal = {
				liege = { character = ROOT }
				has_minor_title = title_court_physician
				OR = { has_character_flag = is_court_physician trait = physician }
				learning = 16
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.2
			any_courtier_or_vassal = {
				liege = { character = ROOT }
				has_minor_title = title_court_physician
				OR = { has_character_flag = is_court_physician trait = physician }
				NOT = { learning = 14 }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.1
			any_courtier_or_vassal = {
				liege = { character = ROOT }
				has_minor_title = title_court_physician
				OR = { has_character_flag = is_court_physician trait = physician }
				NOT = { learning = 10 }
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier_or_vassal = {
			limit = {
				liege = { character = ROOT }
				has_minor_title = title_court_physician
				OR = { has_character_flag = is_court_physician trait = physician }
			}
			save_event_target_as = physician_target
		}
		set_character_flag = stress_relief
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTphysician10_A
		prestige = -50
		if = {
			limit = { trait = stressed }
			random = {
				chance = 50
				remove_trait = stressed
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = { id = 38313 days = 1 } # Notify lost Stressed
				}
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { trait = diligent } remove_trait = diligent }
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = taking_it_easy duration = 10950 }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTphysician10_B
		prestige = 50
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = stressed } }
			random = {
				chance = 30
				add_trait = stressed
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = 38282 #Notify Stressed
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = diligent } }
			random = {
				chance = 30
				add_trait = diligent
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = 38256 #Notify Diligent
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# physician.11 A guide dog
# The physician gives a blind ruler a guide dog.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, physician_target their court physician. Fired by
# the on_yearly_pulse random list.
character_event = {
	id = physician.11
	desc = EVTDESCphysician11
	picture = GFX_evt_court_physician
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	only_playable = yes
	prisoner = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = blinded
		NOR = {
			has_character_modifier = guide_dog
			has_character_flag = denied_guide_dog
			is_tribal = yes
			is_nomadic = yes
		}
		any_courtier_or_vassal = {
			liege = { character = ROOT }
			has_minor_title = title_court_physician
			OR = { has_character_flag = is_court_physician trait = physician }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1

		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			any_courtier_or_vassal = {
				liege = { character = ROOT }
				has_minor_title = title_court_physician
				OR = { has_character_flag = is_court_physician trait = physician }
				learning = 16
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.2
			any_courtier_or_vassal = {
				liege = { character = ROOT }
				has_minor_title = title_court_physician
				OR = { has_character_flag = is_court_physician trait = physician }
				NOT = { learning = 14 }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.1
			any_courtier_or_vassal = {
				liege = { character = ROOT }
				has_minor_title = title_court_physician
				OR = { has_character_flag = is_court_physician trait = physician }
				NOT = { learning = 10 }
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier_or_vassal = {
			limit = {
				liege = { character = ROOT }
				has_minor_title = title_court_physician
				OR = { has_character_flag = is_court_physician trait = physician }
			}
			save_event_target_as = physician_target
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTphysician11_A
		tooltip_info = charitable

		trigger = { trait = charitable }

		transfer_scaled_wealth = { to = event_target:physician_target value = 0.3 }

		event_target:physician_target = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_was_praised who = ROOT years = 10 }
		}

		add_character_modifier = { modifier = guide_dog duration = -1 }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = physician.12 days = 3500 random = 2000 } } # Dog dies
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTphysician11_B
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = guide_dog duration = -1 }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = physician.12 days = 3500 random = 2000 } } # Dog dies
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTphysician11_C

		event_target:physician_target = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_insulted who = ROOT years = 10 }
			set_character_flag = denied_guide_dog
		}
	}
}

# physician.12 The guide dog dies
# The faithful dog is gone one morning.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by physician.11.
character_event = {
	id = physician.12
	desc = EVTDESCphysician12
	picture = GFX_evt_hunting_scene
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_character_modifier = guide_dog
	}

	option = { # She taught me a lot
		name = EVTOPTphysician12_A
		remove_character_modifier = guide_dog
		prestige = 5
	}
	option = { # Give in to despair
		name = EVTOPTphysician12_B
		remove_character_modifier = guide_dog
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = depressed } }
			random = {
				chance = 50
				add_trait = depressed
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = 38288 #Notify Depressed
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# physician.13 Fertility treatment
# The physician offers a cure for a ruler with no son.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, physician_target their court physician. Fired by
# the on_yearly_pulse random list.
character_event = {
	id = physician.13
	desc = EVTDESCphysician13
	picture = GFX_evt_court_physician
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	only_playable = yes
	prisoner = no
	only_men = yes
	is_married = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		spouse = { NOT = { age = 45 } is_pregnant = no ai = yes }
		NOR = {
			has_character_flag = fertility_treatment
			is_ascetic_trigger = yes
			trait = celibate
			trait = eunuch
			num_of_children = 1
			is_merchant_republic = yes
		}
		any_courtier_or_vassal = {
			#is_female = no
			ai = yes
			liege = { character = ROOT }
			has_minor_title = title_court_physician
			OR = { has_character_flag = is_court_physician trait = physician }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1

		modifier = { factor = 2 age = 40 }
		modifier = { factor = 2 age = 60 }
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			any_courtier_or_vassal = {
				liege = { character = ROOT }
				has_minor_title = title_court_physician
				OR = { has_character_flag = is_court_physician trait = physician }
				learning = 16
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier_or_vassal = {
			limit = {
				ai = yes
				liege = { character = ROOT }
				has_minor_title = title_court_physician
				OR = { has_character_flag = is_court_physician trait = physician }
			}
			save_event_target_as = physician_target
		}
		spouse = { save_event_target_as = spouse_target }
	}

	option = { # Yes, I need children!
		name = EVTOPTphysician13_A
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = fertility_treatment_custom_tooltip
			hidden_tooltip = {
				set_character_flag = fertility_treatment
				event_target:physician_target = { character_event = { id = physician.14 } }
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # No, I know I can do it without help
		name = EVTOPTphysician13_B
		prestige = 5
	}
}

# physician.14 Fertility treatment outcome
# Hidden. Rolls how the treatment goes (15-19).
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by physician.13.
character_event = {
	id = physician.14
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			10 = { # Physician seduces and impregnates your spouse
				modifier = { factor = 0 event_target:physician_target = { is_woman_trigger = yes } }
				modifier = { factor = 0 event_target:spouse_target = { is_lover = FROM } }
				modifier = { factor = 0 event_target:spouse_target = { is_friend = FROM } }
				modifier = { factor = 0 is_no_females_race = yes }
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					OR = { is_ascetic_trigger = yes trait = celibate trait = eunuch trait = chaste }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.1
					NOR = { trait = deceitful trait = lustful trait = hedonist trait = seducer }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 5
					AND = {
						OR = { trait = deceitful trait = lustful trait = hedonist trait = seducer }
						event_target:spouse_target = {
							OR = {
								trait = deceitful
								trait = lustful
								trait = hedonist
								trait = seductress
							}
						}
					}
				}

				FROM = { character_event = { id = physician.15 } }
			}

			10 = { # Physician concocts a herbal mixture that is TOO effective...
				# modifier = {
				# factor = 0
				# has_game_rule = {
				# name = supernatural_events
				# value = off
				# }
				# }
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 FROM = { ai = yes } }
				modifier = {
					factor = 5
					OR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 }
				}
				modifier = { factor = 5 trait = possessed }

				FROM = {
					set_character_flag = fertility_increased_too_much
					character_event = { id = physician.16 days = 14 random = 7 }
				}
			}

			50 = { # Physician concocts a herbal mixture that increases your fertility
				modifier = { factor = 2 learning = 14 }
				modifier = { factor = 2 learning = 18 }

				FROM = {
					set_character_flag = fertility_increased
					character_event = { id = physician.16 days = 14 random = 7 }
				}
			}

			30 = { # Physician concocts a herbal mixture that DECREASES your fertility
				modifier = { factor = 0.1 learning = 12 }
				modifier = { factor = 2 NOT = { learning = 10 } }
				modifier = { factor = 2 NOT = { learning = 8 } }
				modifier = { factor = 2 has_character_flag = physician_is_quack }

				FROM = {
					set_character_flag = fertility_decreased
					character_event = { id = physician.16 days = 14 random = 7 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# physician.15 A suspicious success
# The physician seduces the spouse instead, and a child is on the way.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by physician.14.
character_event = {
	id = physician.15
	desc = EVTDESCphysician15
	picture = GFX_evt_court_physician
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		event_target:physician_target = { is_alive = yes }
		event_target:spouse_target = { is_alive = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		event_target:spouse_target = {
			if = { limit = { is_no_females_race = no event_target:physician_target = { is_no_females_race = no } } add_lover = event_target:physician_target }
			impregnate_cuckoo = event_target:physician_target
		}
	}

	option = { # Something is fishy here!
		name = EVTOPTphysician15_A
		tooltip_info = paranoid

		trigger = { trait = paranoid }

		event_target:spouse_target = { father_of_unborn_known = yes }
	}

	option = { # But... you didn't do anything?
		name = EVTOPTphysician15_B

		trigger = { NOT = { trait = paranoid } }
	}
}

# physician.16 The concoction
# The physician hands over the draught: drink or back out.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by physician.14.
character_event = {
	id = physician.16
	desc = EVTDESCphysician16
	picture = GFX_evt_performing_experiments
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		event_target:physician_target = { is_alive = yes }
	}

	immediate = { }

	option = { # Drink it
		name = EVTOPTphysician16_A

		custom_tooltip = {
			text = drink_the_concoction_custom_tooltip
			hidden_tooltip = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_character_flag = fertility_increased_too_much }
					character_event = { id = physician.17 days = 3 random = 5 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_character_flag = fertility_increased }
					character_event = { id = physician.18 days = 14 random = 7 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_character_flag = fertility_decreased }
					character_event = { id = physician.19 days = 14 random = 7 }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Back out
		name = EVTOPTphysician16_B

		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }

		prestige = -5
		event_target:physician_target = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_insulted who = ROOT years = 10 }
		}
	}
}

# physician.17 Too well
# The draught works far too well.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by physician.16.
character_event = {
	id = physician.17
	desc = EVTDESCphysician17
	picture = GFX_evt_performing_experiments
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_new = yes

	trigger = { }

	immediate = {
		random_realm_character = {
			limit = {
				prisoner = no
				is_woman_trigger = yes
				NOT = { character = ROOT }
				NOT = { character = event_target:lover_2 }
				NOT = { character = event_target:lover_3 }
				NOT = { age = 45 }
				is_marriage_adult = yes
				is_pregnant = no
				NOT = { trait = incapable }
				has_lover = no
				NOT = { spouse = { character = ROOT } }
				NOT = { consort = { character = ROOT } }
			}
			impregnate = ROOT
			save_event_target_as = lover_1
		}
		random_realm_character = {
			limit = {
				prisoner = no
				is_woman_trigger = yes
				NOT = { character = ROOT }
				NOT = { character = event_target:lover_1 }
				NOT = { character = event_target:lover_3 }
				NOT = { age = 45 }
				is_marriage_adult = yes
				is_pregnant = no
				NOT = { trait = incapable }
				has_lover = no
				NOT = { spouse = { character = ROOT } }
				NOT = { consort = { character = ROOT } }
			}
			impregnate = ROOT
			save_event_target_as = lover_2
		}
		random_realm_character = {
			limit = {
				prisoner = no
				is_woman_trigger = yes
				NOT = { character = ROOT }
				NOT = { character = event_target:lover_1 }
				NOT = { character = event_target:lover_2 }
				NOT = { character = ROOT }
				NOT = { age = 45 }
				is_marriage_adult = yes
				is_pregnant = no
				NOT = { trait = incapable }
				has_lover = no
				NOT = { spouse = { character = ROOT } }
				NOT = { consort = { character = ROOT } }
			}
			impregnate = ROOT
			save_event_target_as = lover_3
		}
		random_realm_character = {
			limit = {
				prisoner = no
				is_female = no
				is_adult = yes
				has_lover = no
				trait = homosexual
				NOT = { character = ROOT }
				NOT = { trait = incapable }
				NOT = { character = event_target:lover_h_2 }
			}
			save_event_target_as = lover_h_1
		}
		random_realm_character = {
			limit = {
				prisoner = no
				is_female = no
				is_adult = yes
				has_lover = no
				trait = homosexual
				NOT = { character = ROOT }
				NOT = { trait = incapable }
				NOT = { character = event_target:lover_h_1 }
			}
			save_event_target_as = lover_h_2
		}
	}

	option = { # Worked TOO well!
		name = EVTOPTphysician17_A

		if = { limit = { trait = chaste } remove_trait = chaste }
		add_trait = lustful
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = homosexual } } add_trait = homosexual }
		fertility = 0.25

		any_spouse = {
			limit = { prisoner = no NOT = { trait = incapable } }
			hidden_tooltip = {
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { age = 45 } is_woman_trigger = yes is_pregnant = no }
					impregnate = ROOT
				}
			}
			if = { limit = { NOT = { is_lover = ROOT } } if = { limit = { is_no_females_race = no ROOT = { is_no_females_race = no } } add_lover = ROOT } }
		}

		any_consort = {
			limit = { prisoner = no NOT = { trait = incapable } }
			hidden_tooltip = {
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { age = 45 } is_woman_trigger = yes is_pregnant = no }
					impregnate = ROOT
				}
			}
			if = { limit = { NOT = { is_lover = ROOT } } if = { limit = { is_no_females_race = no ROOT = { is_no_females_race = no } } add_lover = ROOT } }
		}
		event_target:lover_1 = { if = { limit = { is_no_females_race = no ROOT = { is_no_females_race = no } } add_lover = ROOT } }
		event_target:lover_2 = { if = { limit = { is_no_females_race = no ROOT = { is_no_females_race = no } } add_lover = ROOT } }
		event_target:lover_3 = { if = { limit = { is_no_females_race = no ROOT = { is_no_females_race = no } } add_lover = ROOT } }
		event_target:lover_h_1 = { if = { limit = { is_no_females_race = no ROOT = { is_no_females_race = no } } add_lover = ROOT } }
		event_target:lover_h_2 = { if = { limit = { is_no_females_race = no ROOT = { is_no_females_race = no } } add_lover = ROOT } }
	}
}

# physician.19 Botched

	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

		event_target:physician_target = { is_alive = yes }

	}

		event_target:physician_target = { imprison = ROOT }
	}

		name = EVTOPTphysician19_B

		prestige = -5
		fertility = -0.05
	}
}

# physician.20 Learning medicine
# A physician friend offers to teach the ruler medicine.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, physician_target their court physician. Fired by
# the on_yearly_pulse random list.
character_event = {
	id = physician.20
	desc = EVTDESCphysician20
	picture = GFX_evt_court_physician
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	only_playable = yes
	prisoner = no
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_character_flag = physician_learning }
		any_courtier_or_vassal = {
			liege = { character = ROOT }
			has_minor_title = title_court_physician
			OR = { has_character_flag = is_court_physician trait = physician }
			is_friend = ROOT
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1

		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = scholar }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = mystic }
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			any_courtier_or_vassal = {
				liege = { character = ROOT }
				has_minor_title = title_court_physician
				OR = { has_character_flag = is_court_physician trait = physician }
				is_friend = ROOT
				learning = 16
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.2
			any_courtier_or_vassal = {
				liege = { character = ROOT }
				has_minor_title = title_court_physician
				OR = { has_character_flag = is_court_physician trait = physician }
				is_friend = ROOT
				NOT = { learning = 14 }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.1
			any_courtier_or_vassal = {
				liege = { character = ROOT }
				has_minor_title = title_court_physician
				OR = { has_character_flag = is_court_physician trait = physician }
				is_friend = ROOT
				NOT = { learning = 10 }
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier_or_vassal = {
			limit = {
				liege = { character = ROOT }
				has_minor_title = title_court_physician
				OR = { has_character_flag = is_court_physician trait = physician }
				is_friend = ROOT
			}
			save_event_target_as = physician_target
		}
		set_character_flag = physician_learning
	}

	option = { # This seems interesting!
		name = EVTOPTphysician20_A

		custom_tooltip = {
			text = physician_learning_custom_tooltip
			hidden_tooltip = {
				random_list = {
					15 = {
						modifier = { factor = 3 trait = scholar }
						modifier = { factor = 2 trait = mystic }
						modifier = { factor = 2 trait = diligent }
						modifier = { factor = 2 trait = patient }
						set_character_flag = physician_learning_excellent_outcome
					}
					20 = {
						modifier = { factor = 3 trait = scholar }
						modifier = { factor = 2 trait = mystic }
						modifier = { factor = 2 trait = diligent }
						modifier = { factor = 2 trait = patient }
						set_character_flag = physician_learning_good_outcome
					}
					50 = { set_character_flag = physician_learning_middling_outcome }
					10 = {
						modifier = { factor = 2 trait = wroth }
						modifier = { factor = 2 trait = arbitrary }
						set_character_flag = physician_learning_poor_outcome
					}
					5 = {
						modifier = { factor = 2 trait = wroth }
						modifier = { factor = 2 trait = arbitrary }
						set_character_flag = physician_learning_disastrous_outcome
					}
				}
				character_event = { id = physician.21 days = 7 random = 10 }
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Rather not disturb
		name = EVTOPTphysician20_B

		prestige = 5
	}
}

		trigger = { has_character_flag = physician_learning_good_outcome }
	}

		text = EVTDESCphysician21_MIDDLING
		picture = GFX_evt_performing_experiments
		trigger = { has_character_flag = physician_learning_middling_outcome }
	}

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCphysician21_BAD
		picture = GFX_evt_experiments_failed
		trigger = { has_character_flag = physician_learning_poor_outcome }
	}

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCphysician21_TERRIBLE
		picture = GFX_evt_experiments_failed
		trigger = { has_character_flag = physician_learning_disastrous_outcome }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTphysician21_A
		trigger = { has_character_flag = physician_learning_excellent_outcome }

		prestige = 5

		change_learning = 2
		add_character_modifier = { name = invigorated duration = 5475 }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTphysician21_B
		trigger = { has_character_flag = physician_learning_good_outcome }

		prestige = 5

		change_learning = 1
		add_character_modifier = { name = new_insights duration = 1095 }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTphysician21_C
		trigger = { has_character_flag = physician_learning_middling_outcome }

		prestige = 5

		change_learning = 1
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTphysician21_D
		trigger = { has_character_flag = physician_learning_poor_outcome }

		prestige = -5

		change_learning = 1

		if = {
			limit = { is_nurgle_immune_race = no }
			add_trait = headache
			set_character_flag = disease_not_serious
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = treatment.10 days = 100 random = 365 } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTphysician21_E
		trigger = { has_character_flag = physician_learning_disastrous_outcome }

		if = {
			limit = { is_nurgle_immune_race = yes }
			prestige = -20
		}
		else = {
			prestige = -5
			add_trait = infection
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = treatment.2 days = 365 random = 1095 } }
		}
	}
}

# physician.22 Provincial apothecaries
# The physician asks the ruler to fund apothecaries across the realm.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, physician_target their court physician. Fired by
# the on_yearly_pulse random list.
character_event = {
	id = physician.22
	desc = EVTDESCphysician22
	picture = GFX_evt_court_physician
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	only_playable = yes
	prisoner = no
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		higher_tier_than = COUNT
		NOT = { has_character_flag = physician_apothecaries }
		any_courtier_or_vassal = {
			liege = { character = ROOT }
			has_minor_title = title_court_physician
			OR = { has_character_flag = is_court_physician trait = physician }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1

		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = charitable }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = diligent }
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			any_courtier_or_vassal = {
				liege = { character = ROOT }
				has_minor_title = title_court_physician
				OR = { has_character_flag = is_court_physician trait = physician }
				learning = 16
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.2
			any_courtier_or_vassal = {
				liege = { character = ROOT }
				has_minor_title = title_court_physician
				OR = { has_character_flag = is_court_physician trait = physician }
				NOT = { learning = 14 }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.1
			any_courtier_or_vassal = {
				liege = { character = ROOT }
				has_minor_title = title_court_physician
				OR = { has_character_flag = is_court_physician trait = physician }
				NOT = { learning = 10 }
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier_or_vassal = {
			limit = {
				liege = { character = ROOT }
				has_minor_title = title_court_physician
				OR = { has_character_flag = is_court_physician trait = physician }
			}
			save_event_target_as = physician_target
		}
		set_character_flag = physician_apothecaries
	}

	option = { # I will invest in this!
		name = EVTOPTphysician22_A

		ai_chance = { factor = 50 modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { treasury = 300 } } }

		scaled_wealth = -0.5

		prestige = 50

		custom_tooltip = {
			text = physician_apothecaries_custom_tooltip
			hidden_tooltip = {
				any_demesne_province = {
					limit = {
						NOR = {
							any_province_holding = { has_siege = yes }
							any_province_holding = { is_occupied = yes }
							has_disease = yes
						}
					}
					change_variable = { which = prosperity_value value = 15 }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # I can't afford it
		name = EVTOPTphysician22_B
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}
}

# physician.30 A treatise on the humours
# The physician wants patronage to write a book.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, physician their court physician. Fired by the
# on_yearly_pulse random list.
character_event = {
	id = physician.30
	desc = EVTDESCphysician30
	picture = GFX_evt_court_physician
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		any_courtier_or_vassal = {
			liege = { character = ROOT }
			has_minor_title = title_court_physician
			NOT = { has_character_flag = physician_wants_to_research }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier_or_vassal = {
			limit = {
				liege = { character = ROOT }
				has_minor_title = title_court_physician
				NOT = { has_character_flag = physician_wants_to_research }
			}

			save_event_target_as = physician
			set_character_flag = physician_wants_to_research
		}
	}

	#Sure
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTphysician30_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTphysician30_A_TOOLTIP }

		scaled_wealth = { value = -0.2 min = -20 }

		reverse_opinion = { who = event_target:physician modifier = opinion_grateful years = 5 }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:physician = { character_event = { id = physician.31 days = 3 } }
		}
	}

	#Nope
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTphysician30_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTphysician30_B_TOOLTIP }

		reverse_opinion = { who = event_target:physician modifier = opinion_disappointed years = 5 }
	}
}

# physician.31 Patronage granted
# The physician hears they have a patron.
# Scope: ROOT is the physician, FROM the liege. Fired by physician.30.
character_event = {
	id = physician.31
	desc = EVTDESCphysician31
	picture = GFX_evt_court_physician
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTphysician31_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTphysician31_A_TOOLTIP }

		treasury = 20

		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				#Writes a great book
				15 = {
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 is_smart_trigger = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 physician_good_trigger = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 25 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 20 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 15 }
					modifier = { factor = 0.8 NOT = { learning = 15 } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.8 NOT = { learning = 10 } }

					random_list = {
						10 = { set_character_flag = physician_great_book_1 }
						10 = { set_character_flag = physician_great_book_2 }
						10 = { set_character_flag = physician_great_book_3 }
					}

					FROM = { character_event = { id = physician.32 days = 400 } }
					character_event = { id = physician.33 days = 401 }
				}
				#Writes a good book
				35 = {
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 25 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 20 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 15 }
					modifier = { factor = 0.8 NOT = { learning = 15 } }
					modifier = { factor = 0.8 NOT = { learning = 10 } }

					set_character_flag = physician_good_book

					FROM = { character_event = { id = physician.32 days = 400 } }
					character_event = { id = physician.33 days = 401 }
				}
				#Writes a meh book
				35 = {
					set_character_flag = physician_meh_book

					FROM = { character_event = { id = physician.32 days = 400 } }
					character_event = { id = physician.33 days = 401 }
				}
				#Writes a laughable book
				15 = {
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 is_dumb_trigger = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 physician_bad_trigger = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 0.8 learning = 25 }
					modifier = { factor = 0.8 learning = 20 }
					modifier = { factor = 0.8 learning = 15 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 NOT = { learning = 15 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 NOT = { learning = 10 } }

					set_character_flag = physician_bad_book

					FROM = { character_event = { id = physician.32 days = 400 } }
					character_event = { id = physician.33 days = 401 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# physician.32 The book is published (liege)
# The liege hears how the treatise was received.
# Scope: ROOT is the liege. Fired by physician.31.
character_event = {
	id = physician.32
	picture = GFX_evt_court_physician
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = physician_great_book_1 } }
		text = EVTDESCphysician32_GREAT_1
		picture = GFX_evt_doctor_crazy_treatment
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = physician_great_book_2 } }
		text = EVTDESCphysician32_GREAT_2
		picture = GFX_evt_doctor_crazy_treatment
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = physician_great_book_3 } }
		text = EVTDESCphysician32_GREAT_3
		picture = GFX_evt_doctor_crazy_treatment
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = physician_good_book } }
		text = EVTDESCphysician32_GOOD
		picture = GFX_evt_library
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = physician_meh_book } }
		text = EVTDESCphysician32_MEH
		picture = GFX_evt_performing_experiments
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = physician_bad_book } }
		text = EVTDESCphysician32_BAD
		picture = GFX_evt_experiments_failed
	}

	trigger = {
		FROM = {
			is_alive = yes
			prisoner = no
			OR = {
				has_character_flag = physician_great_book_1
				has_character_flag = physician_great_book_2
				has_character_flag = physician_great_book_3
				has_character_flag = physician_good_book
				has_character_flag = physician_meh_book
				has_character_flag = physician_bad_book
			}
		}
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	#Good book outcome
	option = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = physician_good_book } }

		name = EVTOPTphysician32_B

		tooltip = { FROM = { change_learning = 1 } }
	}

	#Meh book outcome
	option = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = physician_meh_book } }

		name = EVTOPTphysician32_C
	}

	#Bad outcome
	option = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = physician_bad_book } }

		name = EVTOPTphysician32_D
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTphysician32_D_TOOLTIP }

		prestige = -50

		tooltip = { FROM = { if = { limit = { is_dumb_trigger = no } add_trait = dull } } }

		opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_disappointed years = 5 }
	}
	#Great book outcome
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				FROM = { has_character_flag = physician_great_book_1 }
				FROM = { has_character_flag = physician_great_book_2 }
				FROM = { has_character_flag = physician_great_book_3 }
			}
		}

		name = EVTOPTphysician32_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTphysician32_A_TOOLTIP }

		prestige = 100

		tooltip = {
			FROM = {
				if = { limit = { trait = physician } change_learning = 2 }
				if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = physician } } add_trait = physician }
			}
		}

		opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_impressed years = 5 }
	}
}

# physician.33 The book is published (physician)
# The physician hears how the treatise was received.
# Scope: ROOT is the physician. Fired by physician.31.
character_event = {
	id = physician.33
	picture = GFX_evt_court_physician
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = physician_great_book_1 }
		text = EVTDESCphysician33_GREAT_1
		picture = GFX_evt_doctor_crazy_treatment
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = physician_great_book_2 }
		text = EVTDESCphysician33_GREAT_2
		picture = GFX_evt_doctor_crazy_treatment
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = physician_great_book_3 }
		text = EVTDESCphysician33_GREAT_3
		picture = GFX_evt_doctor_crazy_treatment
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = physician_good_book }
		text = EVTDESCphysician33_GOOD
		picture = GFX_evt_library
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = physician_meh_book }
		text = EVTDESCphysician33_MEH
		picture = GFX_evt_experiments_failed
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = physician_bad_book }
		text = EVTDESCphysician33_BAD
		picture = GFX_evt_bloody_tools
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		character_event = { id = physician.34 days = 10 }
	}

	#Great book outcome
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_character_flag = physician_great_book_1
				has_character_flag = physician_great_book_2
				has_character_flag = physician_great_book_3
			}
		}

		name = EVTOPTphysician33_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTphysician33_A_TOOLTIP }

		prestige = 15

		if = { limit = { trait = physician } change_learning = 2 }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = physician } } add_trait = physician }

		tooltip = { FROMFROM = { opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_impressed years = 5 } } }
	}

	#Good book outcome
	option = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = physician_good_book }

		name = EVTOPTphysician33_B

		change_learning = 1
	}

	#Meh book outcome
	option = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = physician_meh_book }

		name = EVTOPTphysician33_C
	}

	#Bad book outcome outcome
	option = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = physician_bad_book }

		name = EVTOPTphysician33_D
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTphysician33_D_TOOLTIP }

		prestige = -50

		if = { limit = { is_dumb_trigger = no } add_trait = dull }

		tooltip = {
			FROMFROM = { opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_disappointed years = 5 } }
		}
	}
}

# physician.34 The book's fame
# Hidden. Passes the book's reception on.
# Scope: ROOT is the physician. Fired by physician.33.
character_event = {
	id = physician.34

	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		clr_character_flag = physician_great_book_1
		clr_character_flag = physician_great_book_2
		clr_character_flag = physician_great_book_3
		clr_character_flag = physician_good_book
		clr_character_flag = physician_meh_book
		clr_character_flag = physician_bad_book
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Training the physician (physician.40-61)
###############################################################################

# physician.40 Physician training
# Hidden. A physician who is not yet trained asks the liege for herbs, a
# laboratory, books or tools.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by the on_yearly_pulse random list.
character_event = {
	id = physician.40

	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		any_courtier_or_vassal = {
			liege = { character = ROOT }
			has_minor_title = title_court_physician
		}
	}

	option = {
		random_courtier_or_vassal = {
			limit = { liege = { character = ROOT } has_minor_title = title_court_physician }

			random_list = {
				#Nothing happens
				20 = { }
				#Receives training
				10 = {
					modifier = { factor = 1.8 NOT = { learning = 6 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.6 NOT = { learning = 8 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 NOT = { learning = 10 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.4 NOT = { learning = 12 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 NOT = { learning = 14 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 NOT = { learning = 16 } }
					modifier = { factor = 0 trait = physician }
					ROOT = { character_event = { id = physician.41 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# physician.41 Buy herbs, tools or books
# The ruler decides whether to buy the physician's request.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by physician.40.
character_event = {
	id = physician.41
	picture = GFX_evt_court_physician
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = physician_books }
		text = EVTDESCphysician41_BOOKS
		picture = GFX_evt_court_physician
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = physician_herbs }
		text = EVTDESCphysician41_HERBS
		picture = GFX_evt_garden
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = physician_tools }
		text = EVTDESCphysician41_TOOLS
		picture = GFX_evt_bloody_tools
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = physician_lab_equipment }
		text = EVTDESCphysician41_LAB_EQUIPMENT
		picture = GFX_evt_performing_experiments
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier_or_vassal = {
			limit = { liege = { character = ROOT } has_minor_title = title_court_physician }
			save_event_target_as = physician
		}

		random_list = {
			10 = { set_character_flag = physician_books }
			10 = { set_character_flag = physician_herbs }
			10 = { set_character_flag = physician_tools }
			10 = { set_character_flag = physician_lab_equipment }
		}
	}

	#Yes
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTphysician41_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTphysician41_A_TOOLTIP }

		scaled_wealth = { value = -0.15 min = -10 }

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = charitable }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 is_smart_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_disease_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { treasury = 10 } }
		}

		#Outcome
		hidden_tooltip = {
			save_event_target_as = physician_liege

			#outcome
			event_target:physician = {
				random_list = {
					#Best outcome
					25 = {
						modifier = { factor = 1.5 is_smart_trigger = yes }
						modifier = { factor = 1.5 physician_good_trigger = yes }
						modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 25 }
						modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 20 }
						modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 15 }
						modifier = { factor = 0.8 NOT = { learning = 15 } }
						modifier = { factor = 0.8 NOT = { learning = 10 } }

						character_event = { id = physician.42 days = 30 }
					}
					#Good outcome
					50 = {
						modifier = { factor = 1.5 is_dumb_trigger = yes }
						modifier = { factor = 1.5 physician_bad_trigger = yes }
						modifier = { factor = 0.8 learning = 25 }
						modifier = { factor = 0.8 learning = 20 }
						modifier = { factor = 0.8 learning = 15 }
						modifier = { factor = 1.2 NOT = { learning = 15 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.2 NOT = { learning = 10 } }

						character_event = { id = physician.44 days = 30 }
					}
					#Fail
					25 = { character_event = { id = physician.46 days = 30 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	#No
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTphysician41_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTphysician41_B_TOOLTIP }

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = greedy }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 is_dumb_trigger = yes }
		}

		clr_character_flag = physician_books
		clr_character_flag = physician_herbs
		clr_character_flag = physician_tools
		clr_character_flag = physician_lab_equipment
	}
}

# physician.42 Best training (physician)
# The physician's side: the purchase gave a best result.
# Scope: ROOT is the physician, FROM the liege. Fired by physician.41.
character_event = {
	id = physician.42
	picture = GFX_evt_court_physician
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = physician_books } }
		text = EVTDESCphysician42_BOOKS
		picture = GFX_evt_court_physician
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = physician_herbs } }
		text = EVTDESCphysician42_HERBS
		picture = GFX_evt_performing_experiments
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = physician_tools } }
		text = EVTDESCphysician42_TOOLS
		picture = GFX_evt_bloody_tools
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = physician_lab_equipment } }
		text = EVTDESCphysician42_LAB_EQUIPMENT
		picture = GFX_evt_performing_experiments
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTphysician42_A

		change_learning = 2

		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { trait = physician } }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = training_level value = 1 } }
					change_variable = { which = training_level value = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { which = training_level value = 1 } } }
					set_variable = { which = training_level value = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = training_level value = 3 } }
					character_event = { id = physician.60 days = 7 }
				}
			}

			FROM = { character_event = { id = physician.43 } }
		}
	}
}

# physician.60 A trained physician
# The physician has become a renowned healer.
# Scope: ROOT is the physician. Fired by physician.42, physician.44.
character_event = {
	id = physician.60
	desc = EVTDESCphysician60
	picture = GFX_evt_court_physician
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { trait = physician }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTphysician60_A

		add_trait = physician
		hidden_tooltip = { liege = { character_event = { id = physician.61 } } }
	}
}

# physician.61 A renowned physician
# The liege hears the physician is now renowned.
# Scope: ROOT is the liege, FROM the physician. Fired by physician.60.
character_event = {
	id = physician.61
	desc = EVTDESCphysician61
	picture = GFX_evt_court_physician
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTphysician61_A

		tooltip = { FROM = { add_trait = physician } }
	}
}

###############################################################################
# The healer-missionary (physician.70-71)
###############################################################################

# physician.70 A healer-missionary
# A foreign healer preaching their faith is caught in a tribal or nomad realm:
# hire them or burn them. Healers come from the Old World faiths, Ormazd, the
# Dawi, the Wood Elves, the halflings and the eastern faiths.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by the on_yearly_pulse random list.
character_event = {
	id = physician.70
	desc = EVTDESCphysician70
	picture = GFX_evt_court_physician
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	only_playable = yes
	prisoner = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		higher_tier_than = BARON
		OR = { is_tribal = yes is_nomadic = yes }
		NOR = {
			religion = nurgle
			trait = creature_forestspirit
			culture_group = daemonic_group
			trait = creature_treekin
			trait = creature_nehekharan
			trait = creature_slann
			trait = vampire_carstein_visible
			trait = vampire_blood_visible
			trait = vampire_strigoi_visible
			trait = vampire_necrarch_visible
			trait = vampire_jade_visible
			trait = vampire_mahtmasi_visible
			trait = vampire_lahmian_visible
			trait = vampire_carstein_hidden
			trait = vampire_blood_hidden
			trait = vampire_strigoi_hidden
			trait = vampire_necrarch_hidden
			trait = vampire_jade_hidden
			trait = vampire_mahtmasi_hidden
			trait = vampire_lahmian_hidden
			is_daemon_race_trigger = yes
			trait = creature_skink
			trait = creature_saurus
			trait = creature_orc
			trait = creature_goblin
			trait = creature_hobgoblin
			trait = creature_snotling
		}
		OR = {
			#Races/faction where it would make sense
			religion_group = old_world_group
			religion_group = ormazd_gods_group
			religion_group = dwarf_gods_group
			religion_group = cadai_group
			religion_group = halfling_gods_group
			religion_group = oriental_gods_group
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { has_character_flag = tribal_physician }
			had_character_flag = { flag = tribal_physician days = 9125 }
		}
		NOT = {
			any_courtier_or_vassal = {
				liege = { character = ROOT }
				has_minor_title = title_court_physician
			}
		}
		any_independent_ruler = {
			ai = yes
			is_adult = yes
			is_feudal = yes
			higher_tier_than = DUKE
			NOT = { religion_group = ROOT }
			is_within_diplo_range = ROOT
			mercenary = no
			holy_order = no
			controls_religion = no
			primary_title = { is_landless_type_title = no }
			NOR = {
				religion = nurgle
				trait = creature_forestspirit
				culture_group = daemonic_group
				trait = creature_treekin
				trait = creature_nehekharan
				trait = creature_slann
				trait = vampire_carstein_visible
				trait = vampire_blood_visible
				trait = vampire_strigoi_visible
				trait = vampire_necrarch_visible
				trait = vampire_jade_visible
				trait = vampire_mahtmasi_visible
				trait = vampire_lahmian_visible
				trait = vampire_carstein_hidden
				trait = vampire_blood_hidden
				trait = vampire_strigoi_hidden
				trait = vampire_necrarch_hidden
				trait = vampire_jade_hidden
				trait = vampire_mahtmasi_hidden
				trait = vampire_lahmian_hidden
				is_daemon_race_trigger = yes
				trait = creature_skink
				trait = creature_saurus
				trait = creature_orc
				trait = creature_goblin
				trait = creature_hobgoblin
				trait = creature_snotling
			}
			OR = {
				#Races/faction where it would make sense
				religion_group = old_world_group
				religion_group = ormazd_gods_group
				religion_group = dwarf_gods_group
				religion_group = cadai_group
				religion_group = halfling_gods_group
				religion_group = oriental_gods_group
			}
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1

		modifier = { factor = 2.5 num_of_symptoms = 1 }
		modifier = { factor = 2.5 is_ill = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = cynical }
	}

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = tribal_physician
		random_independent_ruler = {
			limit = {
				ai = yes
				is_adult = yes
				is_feudal = yes
				higher_tier_than = DUKE
				NOT = { religion_group = ROOT }
				is_within_diplo_range = ROOT
				mercenary = no
				holy_order = no
				controls_religion = no
				primary_title = { is_landless_type_title = no }
				OR = {
					#Races/faction where it would make sense
					religion_group = old_world_group
					religion_group = ormazd_gods_group
					religion_group = dwarf_gods_group
					religion_group = cadai_group
					religion_group = halfling_gods_group
					religion_group = oriental_gods_group
				}
				NOR = {
					religion = nurgle
					trait = creature_forestspirit
					culture_group = daemonic_group
					trait = creature_treekin
					trait = creature_nehekharan
					trait = creature_slann
					trait = vampire_carstein_visible
					trait = vampire_blood_visible
					trait = vampire_strigoi_visible
					trait = vampire_necrarch_visible
					trait = vampire_jade_visible
					trait = vampire_mahtmasi_visible
					trait = vampire_lahmian_visible
					trait = vampire_carstein_hidden
					trait = vampire_blood_hidden
					trait = vampire_strigoi_hidden
					trait = vampire_necrarch_hidden
					trait = vampire_jade_hidden
					trait = vampire_mahtmasi_hidden
					trait = vampire_lahmian_hidden
					is_daemon_race_trigger = yes
					trait = creature_skink
					trait = creature_saurus
					trait = creature_orc
					trait = creature_goblin
					trait = creature_hobgoblin
					trait = creature_snotling
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				religion = THIS
				culture = THIS
				dynasty = none
				age = 35
				random_traits = yes
				female = no
				attributes = { learning = 6 }
				health = 6
				trait = scholarly_theologian
				trait = zealous
				trait = physician
				flag = is_court_physician
			}
			new_character = {
				# Set Racial Trait
				character_event = { id = aiclean.31 }
				remove_trait = cynical
				remove_trait = mystic
				remove_trait = poet
				remove_trait = socializer
				remove_trait = gamer
				remove_trait = hedonist
				remove_trait = duelist
				remove_trait = hunter
				remove_trait = strategist
				remove_trait = administrator
				remove_trait = architect
				remove_trait = gardener
				remove_trait = schemer
				remove_trait = impaler
				remove_trait = seducer
				remove_trait = seductress
				remove_trait = theologian
				remove_trait = scholar
				save_event_target_as = missionary_physician_target
				move_character = ROOT
			}
			save_event_target_as = origin_realm_of_missionary_physician_target
		}
	}

	option = { # Recruit him
		name = EVTOPTphysician70_A

		ai_chance = { factor = 70 modifier = { factor = 5 trait = cynical } }

		event_target:missionary_physician_target = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_loyal_servant who = ROOT years = 10 }
			give_minor_title = title_court_physician
		}

		if = {
			limit = { controls_religion = no }
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = physician.71 days = 30 random = 30 } }
		}
	}

	option = { # Seize his assets and execute him!
		name = EVTOPTphysician70_B

		ai_chance = { factor = 30 modifier = { factor = 5 trait = zealous } }

		wealth = 50
		event_target:missionary_physician_target = {
			death = { death_reason = death_execution_burning killer = ROOT }
		}
		event_target:origin_realm_of_missionary_physician_target = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_dishonorable_scum who = ROOT years = 15 }
		}
	}
}

# physician.71 The healer's faith
# The foreign physician keeps preaching that their god grants health: convert
# or not.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by physician.70.
character_event = {
	id = physician.71
	desc = EVTDESCphysician71
	picture = GFX_evt_court_physician
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		event_target:missionary_physician_target = {
			NOT = { religion_group = ROOT }
			#Only if from a sensible religion groups
			OR = {
				religion_group = old_world_group
				religion_group = ormazd_gods_group
				religion_group = oriental_gods_group
			}
		}
		controls_religion = no
		#Only for sensible religion groups
		OR = {
			religion_group = old_world_group
			religion_group = ormazd_gods_group
			religion_group = oriental_gods_group
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTphysician71_A

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 60
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = humble }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = craven }
		}

		event_target:missionary_physician_target = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_did_not_trust_me who = ROOT years = 10 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTphysician71_B

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 5
			modifier = { factor = 3 trait = cynical }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = arbitrary }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = trusting }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 NOT = { year = 900 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 trait = paranoid }
		}

		event_target:missionary_physician_target = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_glad who = ROOT years = 10 }
		}
		religion = event_target:missionary_physician_target
		prestige = -500
		piety = 250
		any_courtier = {
			limit = { is_close_relative = ROOT NOT = { trait = zealous } }
			religion = event_target:missionary_physician_target
		}
		any_spouse = {
			limit = { is_consort = no NOT = { trait = zealous } }
			religion = event_target:missionary_physician_target
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTphysician71_C

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 30
			modifier = { factor = 5 trait = zealous }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = stubborn }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = cruel }
		}

		wealth = 50
		event_target:missionary_physician_target = {
			death = { death_reason = death_execution_burning killer = ROOT }
		}
		event_target:origin_realm_of_missionary_physician_target = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_dishonorable_scum who = ROOT years = 15 }
		}
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Part 7: Hospitals (namespace hospital)
# Faction: Generic
# Serves: any count or higher who owns a hospital in a province of their own
# faith.
#
#   hospital.1        A learned pilgrim at the hospital
#   hospital.4-5      Charity for the hospital
#   hospital.12       The clergy want a tithe
#   hospital.14       A leper at court
#   hospital.20       The soup kitchen burns
#   hospital.30-34    The court physician studies at the hospital
#
# Fired from on_yearly_pulse (00_on_actions.txt, hospital list).
# Was events/rip_hospital_events.txt (RIP.4501-4534): hospital.N is RIP.N-4500.
###############################################################################

namespace = hospital

# hospital.1 A learned pilgrim
# A pilgrim with healing skill has stayed on at the hospital; the ruler may
# invite them to court.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler with a hospital. Fired from on_yearly_pulse.
character_event = {
	id = hospital.1
	desc = EVTDESChospital1
	picture = GFX_evt_court_physician
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	only_playable = yes
	prisoner = no
	war = no
	has_dlc = "Reapers"

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		higher_tier_than = BARON
		any_demesne_province = { AND = { hospital_level = 1 religion = ROOT } }
		OR = {
			NOT = { any_courtier_or_vassal = { has_minor_title = title_court_physician } }
			any_courtier_or_vassal = {
				has_minor_title = title_court_physician
				NOT = { learning = 13 }
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		any_courtier_or_vassal = {
			limit = { has_minor_title = title_court_physician }
			save_event_target_as = previous_physician
		}
		random_demesne_province = {
			limit = { AND = { hospital_level = 1 religion = ROOT } }
			save_event_target_as = pilgrim_physician_hospital
		}
		create_character = {
			religion = ROOT
			culture = ROOT
			dynasty = none
			age = 38
			random_traits = yes
			female = no
			attributes = { learning = 6 }
			health = 6
			trait = scholarly_theologian
			trait = theologian
			trait = pilgrim
			trait = humble
		}
		new_character = {
			# Set Racial Trait
			character_event = { id = aiclean.31 }
			remove_trait = scholar
			remove_trait = proud
			remove_trait = socializer
			remove_trait = gamer
			remove_trait = hedonist
			remove_trait = duelist
			remove_trait = hunter
			remove_trait = strategist
			remove_trait = administrator
			remove_trait = architect
			remove_trait = gardener
			remove_trait = schemer
			remove_trait = impaler
			remove_trait = seducer
			remove_trait = seductress
			remove_trait = mystic
			random_list = { # Random chance to add Physician
				40 = { remove_trait = theologian add_trait = physician change_learning = 2 }
				60 = { }
			}
			if = { limit = { religion_group = old_world_group } add_trait = monk }
			if = {
				limit = { religion_group = ormazd_gods_group }
				remove_trait = pilgrim
				add_trait = hajjaj
			}
			set_character_flag = is_court_physician
			save_event_target_as = pilgrim_physician
		}
	}

	option = { # Appoint the pilgrim to be your Physician
		name = EVTOPTAhospital1
		event_target:previous_physician = { remove_title = title_court_physician }
		event_target:pilgrim_physician = { give_minor_title = title_court_physician }
	}
	option = { # Ignore the pilgrim
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTBhospital1_A
			trigger = { event_target:previous_physician = { always = no } }
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTBhospital1_B
			trigger = { event_target:previous_physician = { always = yes } }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:pilgrim_physician = {
				if = { limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } antideath_effect = yes break = yes }
				death = { death_reason = death_missing }
			}
		}
	}
}

# hospital.4 Charity for the hospital
# The hospital cannot feed everyone who comes. The ruler can give alms and
# make a show of it (hospital.5).
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler with a hospital. Fired from on_yearly_pulse.
character_event = {
	id = hospital.4
	desc = EVTDESChospital4
	picture = GFX_evt_plague_spreading

	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	only_playable = yes
	prisoner = no
	has_dlc = "Reapers"
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		any_demesne_province = { hospital_level = 1 }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_demesne_province = {
			limit = { hospital_level = 1 }
			save_event_target_as = hospital_location
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAhospital4
		scaled_wealth = -0.5
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAhospital4_tooltip }
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = increase_prosperity_custom_tooltip
			hidden_tooltip = {
				event_target:hospital_location = {
					change_variable = { which = prosperity_value value = 6 }
				}
			}
		}
		character_event = { id = hospital.5 }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBhospital4
	}
}

# hospital.5 Charity: the speech
# How the ruler presents the gift to the people.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by hospital.4.
character_event = {
	id = hospital.5
	desc = EVTDESChospital5
	picture = GFX_evt_culture_change

	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	only_playable = yes
	prisoner = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Speak of your greatness
		name = EVTOPTAhospital5

		prestige = 100
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = proud } NOT = { trait = humble } }
			random = {
				chance = 50
				add_trait = proud
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = { id = 38261 } # Notify
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Speak of God
		trigger = { NOT = { trait = cynical } }
		name = EVTOPTBhospital5

		piety = 50
		add_character_modifier = { name = religious_appreciate months = 60 }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = zealous } }
			random = {
				chance = 50
				add_trait = zealous
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = { id = 38269 } # Notify
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Speak of the realm
		name = EVTOPTChospital5
		add_character_modifier = { name = wol_respected_ruler months = 60 }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = gregarious } NOT = { trait = shy } }
			random = {
				chance = 50
				add_trait = gregarious
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = { id = 38265 } # Notify
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Speak of the people
		name = EVTOPTDhospital5

		add_character_modifier = { name = gave_funny_speech months = 36 }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = proud } NOT = { trait = humble } }
			random = {
				chance = 50
				add_trait = humble
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = { id = 38260 } # Notify
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# hospital.12 The clergy want a tithe
# The religious head asks for a cut, since the clergy work in the hospital.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler with a hospital. Fired from on_yearly_pulse.
character_event = {
	id = hospital.12
	desc = EVTDESChospital12
	picture = GFX_evt_bloody_tools
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	religion_group = old_world_group
	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	only_playable = yes
	prisoner = no
	has_dlc = "Reapers"
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		higher_tier_than = BARON
		any_demesne_province = { AND = { hospital_level = 1 religion = ROOT } }
		rightful_religious_head_scope = { always = yes NOT = { character = ROOT } }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_demesne_province = {
			limit = { AND = { hospital_level = 1 religion = ROOT } }
			save_event_target_as = hospital_location
		}
		rightful_religious_head_scope = { save_event_target_as = religious_head_fee }
	}

	option = { # Accept demand
		name = EVTOPTAhospital12
		transfer_scaled_wealth = { to = event_target:religious_head_fee value = 0.40 }
		piety = 20
		event_target:religious_head_fee = {
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_fulfilled_demands months = 60 }
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = zealous }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = humble }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = theologian }
			modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { scaled_wealth = 1 } }
		}
	}
	option = { # Refuse demand
		name = EVTOPTBhospital12
		event_target:religious_head_fee = {
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_ignored_demands months = 60 }
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = cynical }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = paranoid }
		}
	}
}

# hospital.14 A leper at court
# A leper turned away from the crowded leper house asks the ruler for help.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler with a hospital. Fired from on_yearly_pulse.
character_event = {
	id = hospital.14
	desc = EVTDESChospital14
	picture = GFX_evt_plague_spreading
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	only_playable = yes
	prisoner = no
	has_dlc = "Reapers"
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		higher_tier_than = BARON
		any_demesne_province = { hospital_level = 1 hospital_has_building = leper_colony_1 }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_demesne_province = {
			limit = { hospital_level = 1 hospital_has_building = leper_colony_1 }
			save_event_target_as = hospital_location
		}
		create_character = {
			religion = ROOT
			culture = ROOT
			dynasty = none
			age = 31
			random_traits = yes
			trait = leper
			trait = disfigured
		}
		new_character = {
			# Set Racial Trait
			character_event = { id = aiclean.31 }
			save_event_target_as = homeless_leper
		}
	}
	option = { # Offer a place to stay
		name = EVTOPTAhospital14
		piety = 300
		event_target:homeless_leper = {
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_very_grateful months = 60 }
		}

		ai_chance = { factor = 20 modifier = { factor = 3 trait = kind } }
	}
	option = { # Send the leper away
		name = EVTOPTBhospital14
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = EVTOPTBhospital14_tooltip
			hidden_tooltip = {
				event_target:homeless_leper = {
					if = {
						limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
						antideath_effect = yes
						break = yes
					}
					death = { death_reason = death_missing }
				}
			}
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 80

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = paranoid }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = proud }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = cruel }
		}
	}
}

# hospital.20 The soup kitchen burns
# The hospital's soup kitchen has burned down; rebuild it or let the poor go
# hungry.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler with a hospital. Fired from on_yearly_pulse.
character_event = {
	id = hospital.20
	desc = EVTDESChospital20
	picture = GFX_evt_end_of_days
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	only_playable = yes
	has_dlc = "Reapers"
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		higher_tier_than = BARON
		any_demesne_province = {
			owner = { character = ROOT }
			NOT = { has_province_modifier = damaged_hospital }
			hospital_level = 1
			hospital_has_building = soup_kitchen_1
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_demesne_province = {
			limit = {
				AND = {
					owner = { character = ROOT }
					NOT = { has_province_modifier = damaged_hospital }
					hospital_level = 1
					hospital_has_building = soup_kitchen_1
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = kitchen_hospital
		}
	}

	option = { # Pay for the repairs
		name = EVTOPTAhospital20
		scaled_wealth = -0.20
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = cruel } NOT = { trait = envious } NOT = { trait = kind } }
			random = {
				chance = 25
				add_trait = kind
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = 38268 } }
			}
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 30

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = kind }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = humble }
			modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { scaled_wealth = 0.20 } }
		}
	}
	option = { # Pay for the repairs and send additional food
		trigger = { trait = charitable }
		tooltip_info = charitable
		name = EVTOPTBhospital20
		scaled_wealth = -0.50
		piety = 100

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 20

			modifier = { factor = 5 trait = charitable }
			modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { scaled_wealth = 0.50 } }
		}
	}
	option = { # Pay minimum costs
		trigger = { trait = greedy }
		tooltip_info = greedy
		name = EVTOPTChospital20
		scaled_wealth = -0.10
		piety = -20

		ai_chance = { factor = 20 modifier = { factor = 4 trait = greedy } }
	}
	option = { # Let it be destroyed
		name = EVTOPTDhospital20
		event_target:kitchen_hospital = {
			add_province_modifier = { name = damaged_hospital duration = 1825 }
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 20

			modifier = { factor = 2 OR = { trait = cruel trait = wroth } }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = arbitrary }
		}
	}
}

# hospital.30 The physician wants to study
# The court physician asks leave to study at the hospital for a time.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler with a hospital and a court physician. Fired from
# on_yearly_pulse.
character_event = {
	id = hospital.30
	desc = EVTDESChospital30
	picture = GFX_evt_court_physician

	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	only_playable = yes
	prisoner = no
	has_dlc = "Reapers"
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		any_demesne_province = {
			hospital_level = 1
			OR = {
				hospital_has_building = library_1
				hospital_has_building = pharmacology_laboratory_1
				hospital_has_building = medical_academy_1
			}
		}
		any_courtier_or_vassal = {
			has_minor_title = title_court_physician
			NOT = { has_character_flag = spent_time_at_hospital }
			ai = yes
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_demesne_province = {
			limit = {
				hospital_level = 1
				OR = {
					hospital_has_building = library_1
					hospital_has_building = pharmacology_laboratory_1
					hospital_has_building = medical_academy_1
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = hospital_location
		}
		any_courtier_or_vassal = {
			limit = { has_minor_title = title_court_physician ai = yes }
			save_event_target_as = learning_physician
		}
	}

	option = { # Accept
		name = EVTOPTAhospital30
		scaled_wealth = -0.05
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:learning_physician = {
				set_character_flag = spent_time_at_hospital
				character_event = { id = hospital.31 days = 30 random = 30 }
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Refuse
		name = EVTOPTBhospital30
	}
}

# hospital.31 The physician returns
# Hidden. Picks how the stay went, weighted by the physician's traits, and
# tells the ruler (hospital.32-34).
# Scope: ROOT is the physician, FROM the ruler. Fired by hospital.30.
character_event = {
	id = hospital.31

	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		random_list = {
			40 = { # Good outcome
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = diligent }
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = ambitious }
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = erudite }
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = physician }
				modifier = { factor = 2 OR = { trait = genius trait = quick trait = shrewd } }

				if = {
					limit = { trait = physician }
					change_learning = 2
					FROM = { set_character_flag = physician_got_learning }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { trait = physician } }
					add_trait = physician
					FROM = { set_character_flag = physician_got_trait }
				}

				FROM = { character_event = { id = hospital.32 } }
			}
			40 = { # Ok outcome
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = diligent }
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = erudite }
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = content }

				change_learning = 1

				FROM = { character_event = { id = hospital.33 } }
			}
			20 = { # Bad outcome
				modifier = { factor = 3 trait = slothful }
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = shy }

				FROM = { character_event = { id = hospital.34 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

			limit = { has_character_flag = physician_got_trait }

		}

		}

###############################################################################
# Part 8: Seclusion (namespace seclusion)
# Faction: Generic
# Serves: any ruler who shuts the gates of the capital against an epidemic,
# and their court.
#
# Shutting the gates keeps the sickness out, for a while. Life inside goes
# sour: food runs out, lovers sneak about, a child slips away to a peasant
# lover, someone uses the old tunnels, and during the Red Pox or a Nurgle
# plague a masked guest joins the revels.
#   seclusion.1-2       The gates are shut (shut_the_gates decision)
#   seclusion.4-15      Life in seclusion: corpses at the gate, symptoms,
#                       lovers, boredom, nightmares, a friend, the sick
#   seclusion.31-40     A child's peasant lover
#   seclusion.51-67     The tunnel lurker
#   seclusion.71-83     The masque: a herald of Nurgle at the party
#   seclusion.98-102    Housekeeping: prison, the list, councillors
#   seclusion.110-117   Food runs out
#
# seclusion.100 rolls the list about every nine months from the moment the
# gates shut (seclusion_list_start_effect) instead of a nine-month MTTH
# checked on every playable character.
# Was events/rip_seclusion_events.txt: seclusion.N is RIP.(N+11999) for
# 1-102, and RIP.11550-11557 became 110-117.
###############################################################################

namespace = seclusion

# seclusion.1 The gates are shut
# The ruler seals the castle against the epidemic and the seclusion list
# starts.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by the shut_the_gates decision and
# seclusion.2.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.1
	picture = GFX_evt_suspicious_noble
	desc = EVTDESCseclusion1
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		seclusion_list_start_effect = yes
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseclusion1
	}
}

# seclusion.2 The liege shuts the gates
# An AI vassal hears the liege has gone into seclusion: approve, scorn, or
# shut their own gates.
# Scope: ROOT is the vassal. Fired by the shut_the_gates decision.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.2
	picture = GFX_evt_house_arrest_escape
	desc = EVTDESCseclusion2
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes
	only_rulers = yes
	only_capable = yes
	prisoner = no
	ai = yes # Not really an interesting event for player vassals

	immediate = {
		liege = { capital_holding = { save_event_target_as = gates_closed_target } }
	}

	# It's necessary
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseclusion2
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 40
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = craven }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = cruel }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = shy }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = arbitrary }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = trusting }
		}

		if = { limit = { trait = craven } opinion = { modifier = craven_pos who = FROM years = 5 } }
	}

	# Such cowardice!
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseclusion2
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 40
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = brave }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = kind }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = charitable }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = just }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = paranoid }
		}

		opinion = { modifier = opinion_coward who = FROM years = 5 }
	}

	option = { # Perhaps I should go into seclusion too...
		name = EVTOPTCseclusion2

		trigger = {
			is_landed = yes
			NOR = {
				is_tribal = yes
				is_nomadic = yes
				is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
				has_character_modifier = recently_opened_gates
				has_character_flag = do_not_disturb
			}
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				capital_scope = { NOT = { has_disease = yes } }
				location = { NOT = { has_disease = yes } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				OR = { capital_scope = { OR = { disease = tuberculosis disease = measles } } }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 age = 65 }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = brave }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = depressed }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = stressed }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = craven }
			modifier = { factor = 5 trait = paranoid }
			modifier = {
				factor = 10
				OR = {
					capital_scope = {
						OR = {
							disease = bubonic_plague
							disease = nurgles_rot
							disease = creeping_buboes
						}
					}
					location = {
						OR = {
							disease = bubonic_plague
							disease = nurgles_rot
							disease = creeping_buboes
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		#Achievement flag
		if = { limit = { ai = no } set_character_flag = achievement_no_solicitors }
		if = { limit = { has_minor_title = title_commander } remove_title = title_commander }
		set_character_flag = do_not_disturb
		custom_tooltip = { text = shut_the_gates_info_tooltip }
		add_character_modifier = { name = in_seclusion duration = -1 }
		if = { # If you are a councillor, stop working and return home
			limit = {
				host = { NOT = { character = ROOT } }
				OR = {
					is_councillor = yes
					has_minor_title = title_councilmember_king
					has_minor_title = title_councilmember_emperor
				}
			}
			force_host = ROOT
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			character_event = { id = seclusion.1 }
			any_courtier = { # Unlanded councillors stop working in order to go into seclusion with their liege
				limit = {
					prisoner = no
					is_councillor = yes
					is_landed = no
					liege = { character = ROOT }
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
				}
				add_character_modifier = { name = in_seclusion duration = -1 }
			}
			any_courtier = { # Unlanded non-councillors join their liege in seclusion
				limit = {
					prisoner = no
					is_councillor = no
					is_landed = no
					liege = { character = ROOT }
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
				}
				add_character_modifier = { name = in_seclusion duration = -1 }
			}
			any_courtier = { # Landed councillors return home while you, their liege, is in seclusion
				limit = {
					prisoner = no
					OR = {
						is_councillor = yes
						has_minor_title = title_councilmember_king
						has_minor_title = title_councilmember_emperor
					}
					is_landed = yes
					liege = { character = ROOT }
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
				}
				force_host = THIS
			}
			any_courtier = { # Landed children being educated in your court are sent home, with the guardianship ending
				limit = { prisoner = no is_landed = yes is_adult = no has_guardian = yes }
				remove_guardian = yes
			}
			any_vassal = { character_event = { id = seclusion.2 days = 7 random = 7 } }
			if = { #Notify employer that councillor is going into seclusion
				limit = { is_councillor = yes liege = { ai = no } }
				liege = { letter_event = { id = seclusion.102 days = 1 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

# seclusion.110 The larders run low
# Food is nearly gone. A gardener ruler stretches it further. Starts the food
# chain (111-117).
# Scope: ROOT is the secluded ruler. Fired by the seclusion list
# (seclusion.100).
narrative_event = {
	id = seclusion.110
	picture = GFX_evt_castle_construction
	title = EVTNAMEseclusion110
	desc = EVTDESCseclusion110 #notice food is low
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_rulers = yes

	trigger = {
		has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
	}

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = seclusion_food_events
		set_character_flag = seclusion_events_on
	}

	option = {
		tooltip_info = gardener
		trigger = { trait = gardener }
		name = EVTOPTAseclusion110 #gardens provide for a time
		character_event = { id = seclusion.111 days = 60 random = 10 }
		set_variable = { which = remaining_food value = 7 }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = { NOT = { trait = gardener } }
		name = EVTOPTBseclusion110 #this isn't good
		character_event = { id = seclusion.111 days = 25 random = 10 }
		set_variable = { which = remaining_food value = 5 }
	}
}

# seclusion.111 Food is a problem
# Ration, convince the court food is unneeded, or eat while you can.
# Scope: ROOT is the secluded ruler. Fired by seclusion.110.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.111
	picture = GFX_evt_castle_construction
	desc = EVTDESCseclusion111 #food is now a problem
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
	}

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = seclusion_food_shortage
	}

	option = {
		trigger = { stewardship = 15 }
		name = EVTOPTAseclusion111 #good rationing
		tooltip_info = stewardship
		add_character_modifier = { name = tiny_food_shortage duration = -1 }
		any_courtier = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion }
			add_character_modifier = { name = tiny_food_shortage duration = -1 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = { diplomacy = 15 }
		name = EVTOPTBseclusion111 #convince people to eat less
		tooltip_info = diplomacy
		add_character_modifier = { name = tiny_food_shortage duration = -1 }
		any_courtier = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion }
			add_character_modifier = { name = tiny_food_shortage duration = -1 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		tooltip_info = gluttonous
		trigger = { trait = gluttonous }
		name = EVTOPTCseclusion111 #gotta eat while you still can
		change_variable = { which = remaining_food value = -2 }
		#give everyone modifier with health penalty
		add_character_modifier = { name = greedy_food_shortage duration = -1 }
		any_courtier = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion }
			add_character_modifier = { name = minor_food_shortage duration = -1 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		tooltip_info = hedonist
		trigger = {
			NOT = { trait = gluttonous } #duplicating prev option since we can only have 1 tooltip info
			trait = hedonist
		}
		name = EVTOPTCseclusion111 #gotta eat while you still can
		change_variable = { which = remaining_food value = -2 }
		#give everyone modifier with health penalty
		add_character_modifier = { name = greedy_food_shortage duration = -1 }
		any_courtier = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion }
			add_character_modifier = { name = minor_food_shortage duration = -1 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = { NOR = { trait = gluttonous trait = hedonist } }
		name = EVTOPTEseclusion111 #average rationing
		change_variable = { which = remaining_food value = -1 }
		#give everyone modifier with small health penalty
		add_character_modifier = { name = minor_food_shortage duration = -1 }
		any_courtier = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion }
			add_character_modifier = { name = minor_food_shortage duration = -1 }
		}
	}
}

# seclusion.112 Rats in the cellar
# With food short, the rats could be eaten.
# Scope: ROOT is the secluded ruler with seclusion_food_shortage. Mean time to
# happen.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.112
	picture = GFX_evt_castle_construction
	desc = EVTDESCseclusion112 #eat rats?
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	only_rulers = yes
	has_character_flag = seclusion_food_shortage

	trigger = {
		has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
		OR = {
			had_character_flag = { flag = seclusion_refuse_to_eat_rats days = 180 }
			NOT = { has_character_flag = seclusion_refuse_to_eat_rats }
		}
		NOT = { has_character_flag = seclusion_rats_eaten }
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 60
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseclusion112 # yum
		set_character_flag = seclusion_rats_eaten
	}
	option = {
		trigger = { NOR = { trait = gluttonous trait = hedonist } }
		name = EVTOPTBseclusion112 #don't eat
		change_variable = { which = remaining_food value = -1 }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = proud } } add_trait = proud }
		set_character_flag = seclusion_refuse_to_eat_rats
	}
}

# seclusion.113 The rats eat the food
