# seclusion.114 A greedy courtier
# A courtier is caught stealing food: scold, imprison or expel them.
# Scope: ROOT is the secluded ruler with seclusion_food_shortage. Mean time to
# happen.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.114
	picture = GFX_evt_castle_construction
	desc = EVTDESCseclusion114 #courtier ate your food
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	only_rulers = yes
	has_character_flag = seclusion_food_shortage

	trigger = {
		has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
		check_variable = { which = remaining_food value = 1 }
		#check for gluttons or hedonists
		any_courtier = {
			OR = { trait = gluttonous trait = hedonist }
			prisoner = no
			has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
			is_incapable = no
			NOT = { has_character_flag = stole_food }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				OR = { trait = gluttonous trait = hedonist }
				has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
			}

			remove_character_modifier = minor_food_shortage
			remove_character_modifier = normal_food_shortage
			add_character_modifier = { name = greedy_food_shortage duration = -1 }
			save_event_target_as = target_greedy_courtier
			set_character_flag = stole_food
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 60
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseclusion114 #angry but keep them in
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = kind }

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = charitable }

			modifier = { factor = 2 reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 50 } }
		}
		change_variable = { which = remaining_food value = -1 }
		event_target:target_greedy_courtier = {
			#opinion modifier
			reverse_opinion = { modifier = gluttonous_neg who = ROOT years = 5 }
		}
	}

	option = { # prison
		name = EVTOPTCseclusion114
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = wroth }

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = cruel }

			modifier = { factor = 2 reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 10 } }
		}
		change_variable = { which = remaining_food value = -1 }
		event_target:target_greedy_courtier = { prisoner = ROOT }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseclusion114 #expel them.
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = wroth }

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = cruel }

			modifier = { factor = 2 NOT = { reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 10 } } }
		}
		change_variable = { which = remaining_food value = -1 }
		event_target:target_greedy_courtier = {
			#unset seclusion
			reverse_opinion = { modifier = gluttonous_neg who = ROOT years = 5 }
			remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion
			remove_character_modifier = greedy_food_shortage
		}
	}
	#cannibal option - eat them in turn?
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseclusion115
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10

			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOR = { has_character_modifier = cannibal trait = cannibal_trait }
			}

			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					father = { character = ROOT }
					mother = { character = ROOT }
					is_married = ROOT
					is_child_of = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		event_target:target_greedy_courtier = {
			if = { limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } antideath_effect = yes break = yes }
			death = { death_reason = death_execution_eaten killer = ROOT }
		}
		change_variable = { which = remaining_food value = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { NOR = { has_character_modifier = cannibal trait = cannibal_trait } }
			add_trait = cannibal_trait
		}
	}
}

# seclusion.115 Meat is back on the menu
# Starving, the ruler eyes the courtiers. Eating one is possible.
# Scope: ROOT is the secluded ruler with seclusion_food_shortage. Mean time to
# happen.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.115
	picture = GFX_evt_castle_construction
	desc = EVTDESCseclusion115 #eat people
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	only_rulers = yes
	has_character_flag = seclusion_food_shortage

	trigger = {
		has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
		any_courtier = {
			ai = yes
			at_location = ROOT
			NOT = { father = { character = ROOT } }
			NOT = { mother = { character = ROOT } }
			NOT = { is_married = ROOT }
			NOT = { is_child_of = ROOT }
			has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
		}
		OR = {
			had_character_flag = { flag = seclusion_refuse_to_eat_fellow_man days = 180 }
			NOT = { has_character_flag = seclusion_refuse_to_eat_fellow_man }
		}
		NOT = { check_variable = { which = remaining_food value = 1 } }
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 60
		#
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseclusion115
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				ai = yes
				at_location = ROOT
				NOT = { father = { character = ROOT } }
				NOT = { mother = { character = ROOT } }
				NOT = { is_married = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_child_of = ROOT }
				has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
			}
			if = { limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } antideath_effect = yes break = yes }
			death = { death_reason = death_execution_eaten killer = ROOT }
		}
		change_variable = { which = remaining_food value = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { NOR = { has_character_modifier = cannibal trait = cannibal_trait } }
			add_trait = cannibal_trait
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				trait = gluttonous
				trait = hedonist
				has_character_modifier = cannibal
				trait = cannibal_trait
			}
		}
		name = EVTOPTBseclusion115
		change_variable = { which = remaining_food value = -1 }
		#if not temperate, add it
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = temperate } }
			add_trait = temperate
			lose_weight_small_effect = yes
			weight_trait_check_effect = yes
		}
		set_character_flag = seclusion_refuse_to_eat_fellow_man
	}
}

# seclusion.116 The food is gone
# The stores are empty; the seclusion cannot last.
# Scope: ROOT is the secluded ruler with seclusion_food_shortage. Mean time to
# happen.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.116
	picture = GFX_evt_castle_construction
	desc = EVTDESCseclusion116 #out of food
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	only_rulers = yes
	has_character_flag = seclusion_food_shortage

	trigger = {
		has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
		NOT = { has_character_flag = seclusion_starving }
		NOT = { check_variable = { which = remaining_food value = 1 } }
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 90
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseclusion116
		set_character_flag = seclusion_starving
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = tiny_food_shortage }
			remove_character_modifier = tiny_food_shortage
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = minor_food_shortage }
			remove_character_modifier = minor_food_shortage
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = normal_food_shortage }
			remove_character_modifier = normal_food_shortage
		}
		add_character_modifier = { name = starving_food_shortage duration = -1 }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_courtier = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion }
				remove_character_modifier = tiny_food_shortage
				remove_character_modifier = minor_food_shortage
				remove_character_modifier = normal_food_shortage
				add_character_modifier = { name = starving_food_shortage duration = -1 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# seclusion.117 The shortage is over
# Clears the starvation modifiers after seclusion ends.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler or a courtier. Fired by the open_the_gates decision
# and the end-of-seclusion effect.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.117
	picture = GFX_evt_castle_construction
	desc = EVTDESCseclusion117 #recovery
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes #triggered by "Open the gates"

	notification = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			has_character_modifier = tiny_food_shortage
			has_character_modifier = minor_food_shortage
			has_character_modifier = normal_food_shortage
			has_character_modifier = starving_food_shortage
			has_character_modifier = greedy_food_shortage
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = tiny_food_shortage }
			remove_character_modifier = tiny_food_shortage
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = minor_food_shortage }
			remove_character_modifier = minor_food_shortage
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = normal_food_shortage }
			remove_character_modifier = normal_food_shortage
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = starving_food_shortage }
			remove_character_modifier = starving_food_shortage
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = greedy_food_shortage }
			remove_character_modifier = greedy_food_shortage
		}
		clr_character_flag = seclusion_food_shortage
		clr_character_flag = seclusion_starving
	}

	option = {
		name = OK
	}
}

# seclusion.4 Corpses at the gate
# The commoners pile their dead outside the gates in protest.
# Scope: ROOT is the secluded ruler. Fired by the seclusion list
# (seclusion.100).
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.4
	picture = GFX_evt_castle_construction
	desc = EVTDESCseclusion4
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = seclusion_piling_corpses
	}

	option = { # guards can clear them out
		ai_chance = { factor = 30 }
		set_character_flag = exposed_to_outside
		name = EVTOPTAseclusion4
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAseclusion4_TT }
	}

	option = { # we should all help clear out the dead
		name = EVTOPTBseclusion4
		tooltip_info = kind
		trigger = { NOR = { trait = paranoid trait = craven } trait = kind }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 20

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = brave }

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = charitable }

			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = wroth }

			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = cruel }
		}
		any_courtier = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion }
			set_character_flag = exposed_to_outside
		}
		set_character_flag = exposed_to_outside
		piety = 100
	}

	option = { # leave it as is
		name = EVTOPTCseclusion4
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = slothful }

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = paranoid }

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = craven }
		}
		any_courtier = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion }
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_disgusted years = 5 }
		}
	}
}

# seclusion.5 A courtier shows symptoms
# A courtier may have caught it: send them away, lock them up, or trust to
# luck.
# Scope: ROOT is the secluded ruler. Fired by the seclusion list
# (seclusion.100).
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.5
	picture = GFX_evt_castle_construction
	desc = EVTDESCseclusion5
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = { prisoner = no has_character_modifier = in_seclusion num_of_symptoms = 1 }
			save_event_target_as = sick_courtier
		}
	}

	option = { # they have to go, leave seclusion
		name = EVTOPTAseclusion5
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 25

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = paranoid }

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = craven }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:sick_courtier = {
					OR = {
						has_character_flag = got_bubonic_plague
						has_character_flag = manifest_bubonic_plague
						trait = has_bubonic_plague
					}
				}
				ai = no
			}
			set_character_flag = achievement_close_call
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:sick_courtier = { NOT = { has_character_flag = got_bubonic_plague } }
				ai = no
			}
			set_character_flag = achievement_an_honest_mistake
		}
		event_target:sick_courtier = {
			remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = expelled_me_from_seclusion years = 2 }
		}
	}

	option = { # they can stay, in prison
		name = EVTOPTBseclusion5
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 25

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = wroth }

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = cruel }
		}
		event_target:sick_courtier = { prisoner = ROOT remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion }
		any_courtier = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion }
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_tyrant years = 5 }
		}
	}

	option = { # we shouldn't be worried
		name = EVTOPTCseclusion5
		trigger = { NOR = { trait = paranoid trait = craven } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 25

			modifier = { factor = 2 is_dumb_trigger = yes }

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = brave }

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = stubborn }
		}
	}

	option = { # comfort courtier and hold them close
		name = EVTOPTDseclusion5
		trigger = {
			OR = { trait = lustful trait = kind }
			event_target:sick_courtier = { NOR = { is_friend = ROOT is_rival = ROOT } }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 25

			modifier = { factor = 2 is_dumb_trigger = yes }

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = charitable }

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = proud }

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = brave }
		}
		event_target:sick_courtier = { add_friend = ROOT }
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:sick_courtier = { developing_epidemic_disease_trigger = yes } }
			set_character_flag = exposed_to_outside
		}
	}
}

# seclusion.6 Lovers in the castle
# Two courtiers have been sneaking off together: stop it, marry them, or let
# it be.
# Scope: ROOT is the secluded ruler. Fired by the seclusion list
# (seclusion.100).
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.6
	picture = GFX_evt_castle_construction
	desc = EVTDESCseclusion6
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				ai = yes
				at_location = ROOT
				has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
				any_lover = {
					ai = yes
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
					at_location = ROOT
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = lover_one
			random_lover = {
				limit = {
					ai = yes
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
					at_location = ROOT
				}
				save_event_target_as = lover_two
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # stop lovers
		name = EVTOPTAseclusion6
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 25

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = wroth }

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = cruel }

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = celibate }
		}
		event_target:lover_one = {
			remove_lover = event_target:lover_two
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_angry years = 5 }
		}
		event_target:lover_two = { opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_angry years = 5 } }
	}

	option = { # let them marry
		name = EVTOPTBseclusion6
		trigger = {
			event_target:lover_one = { NOT = { spouse = { always = yes } } can_marry = yes }
			event_target:lover_two = { NOT = { spouse = { always = yes } } can_marry = yes }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 25

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = zealous }

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = proud }

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = chaste }
		}
		event_target:lover_one = { add_spouse = event_target:lover_two }
	}

	option = { # it entertains me
		name = EVTOPTCseclusion6
		tooltip_info = lustful
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTCseclusion6_TT }
		trigger = { trait = lustful }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = { }
				50 = { character_event = { id = seclusion.7 days = 60 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

# seclusion.7 The grateful lovers
# The lovers thank the ruler for letting them be.
# Scope: ROOT is the secluded ruler. Fired by seclusion.6.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.7
	picture = GFX_evt_castle_construction
	desc = EVTDESCseclusion7
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		event_target:lover_one = { any_lover = { character = event_target:lover_two } }
	}

	option = { # receive gratitude
		name = EXCELLENT
		add_favor = event_target:lover_one
		add_favor = event_target:lover_two
		reverse_opinion = { who = event_target:lover_one modifier = opinion_grateful years = 5 }
		reverse_opinion = { who = event_target:lover_two modifier = opinion_grateful years = 5 }
	}
}

# seclusion.8 Boredom and a courtier
# Bored, the ruler considers a courtier as a lover (also used by sway,
# WHG_sway_events).
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by the seclusion list and sway.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.8
	picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseclusion8
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_character_flag = seclusion_lazy_seduction_V2@event_target:target_courtier }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseclusion8_sway_version
		trigger = { has_character_flag = seclusion_lazy_seduction_V2@event_target:target_courtier }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = seclusion_lazy_seduction
		if = { # For Sway event-edge case...
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = seclusion_lazy_seduction_V2@persistent_event_target:sway_target
			}
			persistent_event_target:sway_target = { save_event_target_as = target_courtier }
			break = yes
		}
		random_realm_character = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
				is_opposite_sex = ROOT is_no_females_race = no
				reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 0 }
				at_location = ROOT
				has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
				is_married = no
				is_consort = no
				has_lover = no
				can_marry = yes
			}
			preferred_limit = {
				character = persistent_event_target:sway_target # For Sway events...
			}
			save_event_target_as = target_courtier
		}
	}

	option = { # go for it
		name = EVTOPTAseclusion8
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 25

			modifier = { factor = 10 trait = lustful }

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = proud }

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = brave }
		}
		event_target:target_courtier = { character_event = { id = seclusion.9 } }
	}

	option = { # nah
		name = EVTOPTBseclusion8
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 25

			modifier = { factor = 10 trait = craven }

			modifier = { factor = 5 trait = shy }
		}
	}
}

# seclusion.12 Nightmares
# The people outside haunt the ruler's sleep: open the gates, harden the
# heart, or decide only the chosen survive.
# Scope: ROOT is the secluded ruler. Fired by the seclusion list
# (seclusion.100).
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.12
	picture = GFX_evt_shadow
	desc = EVTDESCseclusion12
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = seclusion_nightmares
	}

	# Open the gates
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseclusion12
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 25

			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = craven }

			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = paranoid }

			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				location = {
					OR = {
						disease = bubonic_plague
						disease = nurgles_rot
						disease = creeping_buboes
					}
				}
			}

			modifier = { factor = 0.5 location = { disease = typhoid_fever } }

			modifier = { factor = 0.5 location = { disease = tuberculosis } }

			modifier = { factor = 0.5 location = { disease = small_pox } }

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = brave }

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = charitable }

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = kind }

			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 trait = stubborn }
			}
		}
		#This is a copy paste from the decision to open the gates
		clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb
		clr_character_flag = exposed_to_outside
		clr_character_flag = seclusion_piling_corpses
		clr_character_flag = seclusion_lazy_seduction
		clr_character_flag = seclusion_nightmares
		clr_character_flag = seclusion_gained_friend
		clr_character_flag = seclusion_child_has_lover
		clr_character_flag = seclusion_tunnel
		clr_character_flag = seclusion_food_events

		custom_tooltip = { text = open_the_gates_info_tooltip }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			clr_character_flag = seclusion_events_on
			remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion
			character_event = { id = seclusion.117 days = 40 random = 20 } #clears starvation modifiers
			any_courtier = {
				clr_character_flag = seclusion_events_on
				remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion
				character_event = { id = seclusion.117 days = 40 random = 20 } #clears starvation modifiers
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = secluded_revelry }
			remove_character_modifier = secluded_revelry
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = savoring_the_end_days }
			remove_character_modifier = savoring_the_end_days
		}
		add_character_modifier = { name = recently_opened_gates duration = 365 }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseclusion12
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBseclusion12_TT }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 25

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = craven }

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = paranoid }

			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 trait = stubborn }
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { trait = depressed } prestige = -100 break = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { trait = stressed NOT = { trait = depressed } }
			add_trait = depressed
			break = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = stressed } trait = craven }
			add_trait = stressed
			break = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = craven } NOT = { trait = brave } NOT = { trait = berserker } }
			add_trait = craven
			break = yes
		}
		if = { limit = { trait = brave } remove_trait = brave }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCseclusion12
		tooltip_info = stubborn
		ai_chance = { factor = 200 }
		trigger = { trait = stubborn }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDseclusion12
		tooltip_info = lunatic
		ai_chance = { factor = 200 }
		trigger = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 }
	}
}

# seclusion.13 A new friend
# Close quarters make a friend of a courtier.
# Scope: ROOT is the secluded ruler. Fired by the seclusion list
# (seclusion.100).
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.13
	picture = GFX_evt_council
	desc = EVTDESCseclusion13
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = seclusion_gained_friend
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				at_location = ROOT
				has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
				NOT = { is_friend = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_rival = ROOT }
				opinion = { who = ROOT value = 0 }
				OR = {
					AND = { age = 6 NOT = { age = 16 } ROOT = { age = 6 NOT = { age = 16 } } }
					AND = { age = 16 ROOT = { age = 16 } }
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = new_friend
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseclusion13
		add_friend = event_target:new_friend
	}
}

# seclusion.14 An epidemic victim at court
# A secluded courtier has the epidemic: throw them out or stick together.
# Scope: ROOT is the secluded ruler. Fired by the seclusion list
# (seclusion.100).
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.14
	picture = GFX_evt_illness
	desc = EVTDESCseclusion14
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				at_location = ROOT
				has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
				NOT = { is_friend = ROOT }
				has_epidemic_disease_trigger = yes
			}
			save_event_target_as = plague_victim
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseclusion14
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 25

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = craven }

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = paranoid }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:plague_victim = { has_epidemic_disease_trigger = yes }
				ai = no
				NOT = { has_character_flag = achievement_close_call }
			}
			set_character_flag = achievement_close_call
		}
		event_target:plague_victim = {
			remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = expelled_me_from_seclusion years = 5 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseclusion14
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 25

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = kind }

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = brave }
		}
	}
}

# seclusion.15 The physician's diagnosis
# The court physician reports a courtier has the epidemic and asks leave to
# treat them.
# Scope: ROOT is the liege. Fired by treatment.32.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.15
	picture = GFX_evt_illness
	desc = EVTDESCseclusion15
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		event_target:court_physician = { always = yes }
		event_target:epidemic_victim = { always = yes }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseclusion15
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 25

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = craven }

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = paranoid }

			modifier = {
				factor = 5
				NOT = { opinion = { who = event_target:epidemic_victim value = 0 } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:epidemic_victim = { has_epidemic_disease_trigger = yes }
				ai = no
				NOT = { has_character_flag = achievement_close_call }
			}
			set_character_flag = achievement_close_call
		}
		event_target:epidemic_victim = {
			remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = expelled_me_from_seclusion years = 5 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseclusion15
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 25

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = brave }

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = charitable }

			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = kind }

			modifier = { factor = 5 opinion = { who = event_target:epidemic_victim value = 50 } }
		}
		event_target:epidemic_victim = {
			set_character_flag = seclusion_treatment_approved
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_grateful years = 5 }
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = EVTOPTBseclusion15_TT
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = treatment.33 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

# seclusion.31 A child has a lover outside
# Hidden. Picks a secluded child of age to have a lover beyond the walls.
# Scope: ROOT is the secluded ruler. Fired by the seclusion list
# (seclusion.100).
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.31

	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = seclusion_child_has_lover
		save_event_target_as = seclusion_parent
		random_child = {
			limit = {
				age = 16
				NOT = { trait = craven }
				NOT = { trait = celibate }
				at_location = ROOT
				has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
			}
			save_event_target_as = seclusion_child
		}
		event_target:seclusion_child = { character_event = { id = seclusion.32 } }
	}
}

# seclusion.32 Ping the child
# Hidden. The child asks the parent to leave (seclusion.33).
# Scope: ROOT is the child, FROM the parent. Fired by seclusion.31.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.32
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		FROM = { character_event = { id = seclusion.33 } }
	}
}

# seclusion.33 The child asks to leave
# The child begs to leave the keep on a matter of life and death.
# Scope: ROOT is the parent, FROM the child. Fired by seclusion.32.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.33
	picture = GFX_evt_quarrel
	desc = EVTDESCseclusion33
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		trigger = { NOT = { trait = paranoid } }
		name = EVTOPTAseclusion33
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = seclusion.34 } } }
		reverse_opinion = { modifier = let_me_leave who = FROM }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseclusion33
		FROM = { remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion }
		FROM = { set_character_flag = seclusion_child_kicked_out }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = seclusion.34 } } }
		reverse_opinion = { modifier = expelled_me_from_seclusion who = FROM }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCseclusion33
		reverse_opinion = { modifier = forbade_me_to_leave who = FROM }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROM = {
				random_list = {
					50 = {
						#Nothing happens
						modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = honest }
						modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = content }
						modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = chaste }
					}
					50 = {
						#Sneak out
						modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = ambitious }
						modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = deceitful }
						modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = brave }
						modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = lustful }
						modifier = { factor = 0 is_no_females_race = yes }
						set_character_flag = escaping_seclusion
						character_event = { id = seclusion.34 }
						character_event = { id = seclusion.39 days = 5 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# seclusion.34 The child finds the lover
# Hidden. Creates the peasant lover and sends the child back (seclusion.35).
# Scope: ROOT is the child. Fired by seclusion.33.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.34
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		if = {
			limit = { prefers_men_trigger = yes }
			create_character = { age = 21 female = no dynasty = none random_traits = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { prefers_men_trigger = no }
			create_character = { age = 21 female = yes dynasty = none random_traits = yes }
		}
		new_character = {
			# Set Racial Trait
			character_event = { id = aiclean.31 }
			add_trait = lustful
			add_trait = fair
			remove_trait = chaste
			remove_trait = ugly
			save_event_target_as = seclusion_childs_lover

			random_list = {
				33 = { } #Nothing
				33 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0 is_disease_immune_race = yes }
					set_character_flag = disease_not_serious
					add_trait = fever
					character_event = { id = treatment.4 days = 5 random = 10 }
				}
				33 = { set_character_flag = lover_is_ill }
			}

			if = { limit = { ROOT = { trait = homosexual } } add_trait = homosexual }

			if = { limit = { is_no_females_race = no ROOT = { is_no_females_race = no } } add_lover = ROOT }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = seclusion_child_kicked_out } }
			FROM = { character_event = { id = seclusion.35 days = 12 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = seclusion_child_kicked_out }
			character_event = { id = seclusion.36 days = 7 }
			character_event = { id = seclusion.38 }
		}
	}
}

# seclusion.35 The child returns with a lover
# The child is back with a ragged peasant lover: bless, hide, or throw them
# out.
# Scope: ROOT is the parent, FROM the child. Fired by seclusion.34.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.35
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	picture = GFX_evt_lovers

	is_triggered_only = yes

	desc = {
		trigger = {
			FROM = { NOT = { has_character_flag = escaping_seclusion } }
			FROM = { NOT = { trait = homosexual } }
		}
		text = EVTDESCseclusion35_A
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				FROM = { has_character_flag = escaping_seclusion }
				FROM = { trait = homosexual }
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCseclusion35_B
	}

	immediate = {
		event_target:seclusion_childs_lover = {
			add_character_modifier = { name = in_seclusion duration = -1 }
		}
	}

	#Stay and get married
	option = {
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = escaping_seclusion }
				is_married = no
				NOT = { trait = homosexual }
			}
			NOT = { trait = paranoid }
		}

		name = EVTOPTAseclusion35
		FROM = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
				add_spouse_matrilineal = event_target:seclusion_childs_lover
			}
			if = { limit = { is_female = no } add_spouse = event_target:seclusion_childs_lover }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:seclusion_child = { character_event = { id = seclusion.38 } }
		}
		reverse_opinion = { modifier = let_us_stay_in_seclusion who = FROM }
		reverse_opinion = {
			modifier = let_us_stay_in_seclusion
			who = event_target:seclusion_childs_lover
		}
	}

	#Stay but don't get married
	option = {
		trigger = {
			FROM = { NOT = { has_character_flag = escaping_seclusion } }
			NOT = { trait = paranoid }
		}
		name = EVTOPTBseclusion35
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = seclusion.36 } } }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:seclusion_child = { character_event = { id = seclusion.38 } }
		}
		reverse_opinion = { modifier = let_us_stay_in_seclusion who = FROM }
		reverse_opinion = {
			modifier = let_us_stay_in_seclusion
			who = event_target:seclusion_childs_lover
		}
	}

	#Make the lover go away
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCseclusion35
		event_target:seclusion_childs_lover = { remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion }
		event_target:seclusion_child = { add_rival = ROOT }
		reverse_opinion = { modifier = expelled_lover_from_seclusion who = FROM }
		FROM = { clr_character_flag = escaping_seclusion }
	}

	#Make them both go away
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDseclusion35
		FROM = { remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion }
		event_target:seclusion_childs_lover = { remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = seclusion.36 } } }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:seclusion_child = { character_event = { id = seclusion.38 } }
		}
		reverse_opinion = { modifier = expelled_me_from_seclusion who = FROM }
		FROM = { clr_character_flag = escaping_seclusion }
	}

	#Let them carry on
	option = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = escaping_seclusion } NOT = { trait = paranoid } }
		name = EVTOPTEseclusion35
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = seclusion.36 } } }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:seclusion_child = { character_event = { id = seclusion.38 } }
		}
		reverse_opinion = { modifier = let_us_stay_in_seclusion who = FROM }
		FROM = { clr_character_flag = escaping_seclusion }
	}
}

# seclusion.36 The banished child
# Hidden. A child sent away may marry the lover in secret (seclusion.37).
# Scope: ROOT is the child. Fired by seclusion.34 and seclusion.35.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.36
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				#Nothing happens
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = lustful }
			}
			50 = {
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = chaste }
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { is_married = yes trait = homosexual } }
				event_target:seclusion_parent = {
					character_event = { id = seclusion.37 days = 14 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# seclusion.37 A secret marriage
# The parent learns the child married the peasant.
# Scope: ROOT is the parent, FROM the child. Fired by seclusion.36.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.37
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	picture = GFX_evt_spymaster

	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion } }
		text = EVTDESCseclusion37_A
		picture = GFX_evt_spymaster
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion } } }
		text = EVTDESCseclusion37_B
		picture = GFX_evt_recieve_letter
	}

	hide_from = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { job_spymaster = { always = yes } }
			job_spymaster = { save_event_target_as = seclusion_informant }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { job_spymaster = { always = no } }
			random_courtier = { save_event_target_as = seclusion_informant }
		}
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseclusion37
		FROM = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
				add_spouse_matrilineal = event_target:seclusion_childs_lover
			}
			if = { limit = { is_female = no } add_spouse = event_target:seclusion_childs_lover }
		}
	}
}

# seclusion.51 Maps of the tunnels
# The treasurer wants the castle tunnels mapped and maintained. Starts the
# tunnel chain (52-67).
# Scope: ROOT is the secluded ruler. Fired by the seclusion list
# (seclusion.100).
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.51
	picture = GFX_evt_council
	desc = EVTDESCseclusion51
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_economy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = seclusion_tunnel
		set_character_flag = seclusion_events_on
		job_treasurer = { save_event_target_as = secluded_steward }
		ROOT = { save_event_target_as = secluded_friendly_ruler }
	}

	#Hand over maps
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseclusion51
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAseclusion51_TT }
		event_target:secluded_steward = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_took_my_advice who = ROOT }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:secluded_steward = {
				character_event = { id = seclusion.52 days = 10 random = 5 }
			}
		}
	}

	#Hand over FALSE maps
	option = {
		tooltip_info = intrigue
		name = EVTOPTBseclusion51
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBseclusion51_TT }

		trigger = {
			OR = { trait = paranoid intrigue = 12 }
			event_target:secluded_steward = {
				OR = { trait = deceitful NOT = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = 25 } } }
			}
		}

		set_character_flag = did_not_trust_steward

		event_target:secluded_steward = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_took_my_advice who = ROOT }
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = seclusion.52 days = 10 random = 5 } }
		}
	}

	#Do nothing
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCseclusion51
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTCseclusion51_TT }

		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = paranoid } } add_trait = paranoid }
	}
}

# seclusion.53 Someone uses the tunnels
# Signs of intruders in the tunnels: ambush them or seal the tunnels.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM the treasurer. Fired by seclusion.52.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.53
	picture = GFX_evt_emissary
	desc = EVTDESCseclusion53
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_economy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	#Plan an ambush
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseclusion53
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAseclusion53_TT }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = seclusion.54 days = 10 } }
	}

	#Seal the tunnels!
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseclusion53
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBseclusion53_TT }

		clr_character_flag = did_not_trust_steward

		if = {
			limit = { NOR = { trait = brave trait = craven trait = berserker } }
			random = {
				chance = 45
				add_trait = craven
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = 38257 } } # Notify craven
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { trait = brave } random = { chance = 45 remove_trait = brave } }
	}
}

# seclusion.54 The tunnel lurker
# Hidden. Creates the lurker: a lover, a rebel leader or an assassin.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by seclusion.53.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.54

	is_triggered_only = yes

	hide_window = yes

	#Create Random Person: a lover, instigator or assassin
	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 is_female = no prefers_men_trigger = no }
				create_character = {
					female = no
					age = 25
					random_traits = yes
					attributes = { intrigue = 12 diplomacy = 10 }
					trait = robust
					trait = homosexual
					dynasty = none
					religion = ROOT
					culture = ROOT
					race = ROOT
					health = 7
				}
				new_character = {
					# Set Racial Trait
					character_event = { id = aiclean.31 }
					save_event_target_as = captured_sneaker
					remove_trait = homosexual
				}
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 is_female = no prefers_men_trigger = no }
				create_character = {
					female = no
					age = 19
					random_traits = yes
					attributes = { intrigue = 9 diplomacy = 9 martial = 7 }
					trait = robust
					trait = lustful
					dynasty = none
					religion = ROOT
					culture = ROOT
					race = ROOT
					health = 7
				}
				new_character = {
					# Set Racial Trait
					character_event = { id = aiclean.31 }
					save_event_target_as = captured_sneaker
					remove_trait = homosexual
				}
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 is_woman_trigger = yes prefers_men_trigger = yes }
				create_character = {
					female = yes
					age = 33
					random_traits = yes
					attributes = { martial = 12 diplomacy = 10 }
					trait = lustful
					dynasty = none
					religion = ROOT
					culture = ROOT
					race = ROOT
					health = 7
				}
				new_character = {
					# Set Racial Trait
					character_event = { id = aiclean.31 }
					save_event_target_as = captured_sneaker
					remove_trait = homosexual
				}
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 is_woman_trigger = yes prefers_men_trigger = yes }
				create_character = {
					female = yes
					age = 19
					random_traits = yes
					attributes = { intrigue = 13 diplomacy = 14 }
					trait = shrewd
					trait = poet
					dynasty = none
					religion = ROOT
					culture = ROOT
					race = ROOT
					health = 7
				}
				new_character = {
					# Set Racial Trait
					character_event = { id = aiclean.31 }
					save_event_target_as = captured_sneaker
					remove_trait = homosexual
				}
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 is_woman_trigger = yes prefers_men_trigger = yes }
				create_character = {
					female = yes
					age = 52
					random_traits = yes
					attributes = { intrigue = 13 learning = 14 }
					trait = lustful
					trait = gardener
					dynasty = none
					religion = ROOT
					culture = ROOT
					race = ROOT
					health = 7
				}
				new_character = {
					# Set Racial Trait
					character_event = { id = aiclean.31 }
					save_event_target_as = captured_sneaker
					remove_trait = homosexual
				}
			}
		}
		event_target:captured_sneaker = {
			random_list = {
				10 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0 is_female = no }
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.1
						ROOT = { any_courtier = { has_nickname = nick_the_silent } }
					}
					give_nickname = nick_the_silent
				}
				10 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.1
						ROOT = { any_courtier = { has_nickname = nick_the_hollow } }
					}
					give_nickname = nick_the_hollow
				}
				10 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.1
						ROOT = { any_courtier = { has_nickname = nick_the_recluse } }
					}
					give_nickname = nick_the_recluse
				}
				10 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0 is_woman_trigger = yes }
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.1
						ROOT = { any_courtier = { has_nickname = nick_the_bard } }
					}
					give_nickname = nick_the_bard
				}
				10 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.1
						ROOT = { any_courtier = { has_nickname = nick_the_troubadour } }
					}
					give_nickname = nick_the_troubadour
				}
				10 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.1
						ROOT = { any_courtier = { has_nickname = nick_the_fox } }
					}
					give_nickname = nick_the_fox
				}
				10 = { modifier = { factor = 0 is_woman_trigger = yes } give_nickname = nick_the_handsome }
			}

			random_list = {
				10 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						event_target:secluded_friendly_ruler = {
							OR = { trait = lustful trait = kind }
						}
					}

					set_character_flag = sneaky_lover
				}

				10 = {
					set_character_flag = sneaky_revolutionary
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						event_target:secluded_friendly_ruler = {
							OR = { trait = greedy trait = cruel trait = wroth trait = impaler }
						}
					}
				}

				60 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						NOT = { has_character_flag = did_not_trust_steward }
						event_target:secluded_steward = {
							NOR = {
								trait = deceitful
								NOT = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = 25 } }
							}
						}
					}

					set_character_flag = sneaky_assassin
					remove_trait = honest
				}
			}
			add_trait = stressed
			character_event = { id = seclusion.63 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_woman_trigger = yes prefers_men_trigger = no }
			event_target:captured_sneaker = { add_trait = homosexual }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_female = no prefers_men_trigger = yes }
			event_target:captured_sneaker = { add_trait = homosexual }
		}
	}
}

# seclusion.63 Captured: ping
# Hidden. Tells the ruler the lurker was caught (seclusion.55).
# Scope: ROOT is the lurker. Fired by seclusion.54.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.63

	is_triggered_only = yes

	hide_window = yes

	immediate = {
		event_target:secluded_friendly_ruler = { character_event = { id = seclusion.55 } }
	}
}

# seclusion.55 A lurker caught
# The guards bring in a starved, jumpy lurker: question, imprison or execute
# them.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by seclusion.63.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.55
	picture = GFX_evt_drunk
	desc = EVTDESCseclusion55
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_from = no

	trigger = {
		has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
	}

	#Hear them out
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseclusion55
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAseclusion55_TT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:captured_sneaker = { character_event = { id = seclusion.61 days = 2 } }
		}
	}

	#Imprison them!
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseclusion55
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBseclusion55_TT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:captured_sneaker = {
				imprison = ROOT

				# Could lead to pitchforks coming at you:
				if = {
					limit = { has_character_flag = sneaky_revolutionary }
					character_event = { id = seclusion.62 days = 30 random = 10 }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	#Execute them, quickly!
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCseclusion55
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTCseclusion55_TT }

		event_target:captured_sneaker = {
			if = { limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } antideath_effect = yes break = yes }
			death = { death_reason = death_execution_beheading Killer = ROOT }

			hidden_tooltip = {
				clr_character_flag = did_not_trust_steward
				if = {
					limit = { has_character_flag = sneaky_lover }
					#event for grieving lover?
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# seclusion.62 The lurker acts
# Hidden. A lurker at court or in prison makes their move: rebels, an assassin
# or a seduction.
# Scope: ROOT is the lurker. Fired by seclusion.55 and seclusion.56.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.62

	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_tooltip = {
			# Could lead to pitchforks coming at you:
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = sneaky_revolutionary }
				event_target:secluded_friendly_ruler = { character_event = { id = seclusion.57 } }
			}
			# Could lead to assassination attempt:
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = sneaky_assassin }
				event_target:secluded_friendly_ruler = { character_event = { id = seclusion.58 } }
			}
			# Could lead to seduction attempt: add block for prison
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = sneaky_lover }
				event_target:secluded_friendly_ruler = { character_event = { id = seclusion.59 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

# seclusion.56 The lurker's story
# The lurker claims a lover at court died; let them join or throw them out.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM the lurker. Fired by seclusion.61.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.56
	desc = EVTDESCseclusion56
	picture = GFX_evt_spymaster
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_from = no

	#I believe! They join court
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseclusion56
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAseclusion56_TT }

		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion }
			FROM = { add_character_modifier = { name = in_seclusion duration = -1 } }
		}
		FROM = {
			hidden_tooltip = {
				remove_trait = stressed
				character_event = { id = seclusion.62 days = 25 random = 10 }
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { FROM = { NOT = { has_character_flag = sneaky_lover } } }
				set_character_flag = exposed_to_outside
			}
			set_character_flag = sneaker_joined_court
		}
	}

	#Send them out of seclusion
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseclusion56
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBseclusion56_TT }
		FROM = {
			add_trait = depressed
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_hate years = 2 }
			hidden_tooltip = {
				# Could still lead to pitchforks coming at you:
				if = {
					limit = { has_character_flag = sneaky_revolutionary }
					character_event = { id = seclusion.62 days = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = sneaky_revolutionary } }
					clr_character_flag = did_not_trust_steward
				}
				event_target:captured_sneaker = { leave_court_effect = yes }
			}
		}
	}
}

# seclusion.57 Pitchforks in the night
# The lurker's friends storm the castle through the tunnels.

	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

		text = EVTDESCseclusion57_B

	}

	immediate = {
		event_target:captured_sneaker = { add_trait = peasant_leader }
	}

	#OH WTF
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseclusion57
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAseclusion57_TT }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {

					modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						has_character_flag = did_not_trust_steward
						event_target:secluded_steward = {
							OR = { trait = deceitful NOT = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = 25 } } }
						}
					}
					hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = seclusion.60 days = 1 } }
				}

				#The rebels made it inside the castle
				50 = {
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_character_flag = did_not_trust_steward }

					hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = seclusion.64 days = 1 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.5

							NOT = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = 25 } }
						}

					character_event = { id = seclusion.67 }
				}
			}
			clr_character_flag = did_not_trust_steward
		}
	}
}

# seclusion.59 A night visit
# The lurker comes to the ruler's bedchamber.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by seclusion.62.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.59
	picture = GFX_evt_lovers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_from = no

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			AND = { event_target:captured_sneaker = { is_woman_trigger = yes } prefers_men_trigger = no }
			AND = { event_target:captured_sneaker = { is_female = no } prefers_men_trigger = yes }
		}
	}

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseclusion59_A
		trigger = { event_target:captured_sneaker = { is_woman_trigger = yes } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseclusion59_B
		trigger = { event_target:captured_sneaker = { is_woman_trigger = no } }
	}

	#I like it!
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseclusion59
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAseclusion59_TT }
		event_target:captured_sneaker = {
			opinion = {
				who = event_target:secluded_friendly_ruler
				modifier = opinion_budding_romance
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { is_no_females_race = no event_target:captured_sneaker = { is_no_females_race = no } } add_lover = event_target:captured_sneaker }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			clr_character_flag = did_not_trust_steward
			clr_character_flag = seclusion_events_on
		}
	}

	#I don't think so, I'm sorry
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseclusion59
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBseclusion59_TT }
		event_target:captured_sneaker = {
			opinion = {
				who = event_target:secluded_friendly_ruler
				modifier = opinion_budding_friendship
			}
		}
		add_friend = event_target:captured_sneaker
		hidden_tooltip = {
			clr_character_flag = did_not_trust_steward
			clr_character_flag = seclusion_events_on
		}
	}

	#Back off!
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCseclusion59
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTCseclusion59_TT }
		event_target:captured_sneaker = {
			opinion = {
				who = event_target:secluded_friendly_ruler
				modifier = opinion_advance_rejected
				months = 36
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			clr_character_flag = did_not_trust_steward
			clr_character_flag = seclusion_events_on
		}
	}
}

# seclusion.60 The rebels crushed
# The ruler and the guards cut down the rebels.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by seclusion.57.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.60
	picture = GFX_evt_pale_rider
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_from = yes

	desc = { text = EVTDESCseclusion60_A trigger = { has_character_flag = did_not_trust_steward } }
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseclusion60_B
		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = did_not_trust_steward } }
	}

	#Victory!
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseclusion60
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAseclusion60_TT }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = cynical } } add_trait = cynical }
		change_martial = 1
		add_character_modifier = { name = crusched_rebels_from_seclusion duration = 730 }

		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:captured_sneaker = {
					AND = { prisoner = no has_character_flag = sneaker_joined_court }
				}
			}
			event_target:captured_sneaker = {
				prisoner = yes
				clr_character_flag = sneaker_joined_court
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			clr_character_flag = did_not_trust_steward
			clr_character_flag = seclusion_events_on
		}
	}
}

# seclusion.64 The rebels wreak havoc
# The rebels are beaten but the ruler is maimed.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by seclusion.57.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.64
	picture = GFX_evt_bloody_man
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_from = yes

	desc = { text = EVTDESCseclusion64_A trigger = { has_character_flag = did_not_trust_steward } }
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseclusion64_B
		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = did_not_trust_steward } }
	}

	#This sucks
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseclusion64
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAseclusion64_TT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			add_maimed_trait_effect = yes
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:captured_sneaker = { prisoner = no } }

				event_target:captured_sneaker = {
					leave_court_effect = yes
					remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion
				}
			}
			clr_character_flag = did_not_trust_steward
			clr_character_flag = seclusion_events_on
		}

		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = cynical } } add_trait = cynical }
		trigger = { event_target:captured_sneaker = { prisoner = no } }
	}
	#This sucks + the lurker was in prison
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseclusion64
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBseclusion64_TT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			add_maimed_trait_effect = yes
			clr_character_flag = did_not_trust_steward
			clr_character_flag = seclusion_events_on
		}
		trigger = { event_target:captured_sneaker = { prisoner = yes } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = cynical } } add_trait = cynical }
	}
}

# seclusion.71 The comforts of confinement
# The bored court wants revelry. Starts the masque (72-83): during the Red Pox
# or a Nurgle plague, a masked guest joins the party. It is a herald of the
# Plague God.
# Scope: ROOT is the secluded ruler. Fired by the seclusion list
# (seclusion.100).
narrative_event = {
	id = seclusion.71
	title = EVTNAMEseclusion71
	picture = GFX_evt_bring_out_your_dead
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_religion

	only_rulers = yes

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseclusion71_A # PIOUS - will prio over benevolent_trigger
		trigger = { is_pious_trigger = yes }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseclusion71_B # BENEVOLENT - will prio over impious_trigger
		trigger = { is_benevolent_trigger = yes is_pious_trigger = no }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseclusion71_C # EVIL - will prio over impious
		trigger = { is_evil_trigger = yes }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseclusion71_D # IMPIOUS - will be rare?
		trigger = { is_impious_trigger = yes is_evil_trigger = no }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseclusion71_E # Fallback (neutral)
		trigger = {
			is_pious_trigger = no
			is_benevolent_trigger = no
			is_impious_trigger = no
			is_evil_trigger = no
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = seclusion_events_on

		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
				prisoner = no
				is_incapable = no
				has_vice_trigger = yes
				has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
			}
			save_event_target_as = bored_courtier_target
		}
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_close_relative = yes
				is_adult = yes
				prisoner = no
				is_incapable = no
				is_pious_trigger = no
				has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
			}
			save_event_target_as = bored_courtier_target
		}
		event_target:bored_courtier_target = {
			add_character_modifier = { name = bright_eyed duration = -1 }
		}
		ROOT = { save_event_target_as = secluded_party_host }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseclusion71 # They're right, we should enjoy ourselves
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAseclusion71_TT }
		set_character_flag = masque_of_red_death
		scaled_wealth = -1

		if = {
			limit = { trait = lustful NOT = { trait = greedy } }
			random = {
				chance = 30
				add_trait_greedy_effect = yes
				remove_trait_charitable_effect = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = lustful } }
			random = { chance = 30 add_trait_lustful_effect = yes remove_trait_chaste_effect = yes }
		}
		add_character_modifier = { name = savoring_the_end_days duration = -1 }
		event_target:bored_courtier_target = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_took_my_advice who = ROOT }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = seclusion.72 days = 30 random = 10 } }
	}

	option = { # don't go overboard with the arrangements
		name = EVTOPTBseclusion71
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBseclusion71_TT }
		set_character_flag = masque_of_red_death

		add_character_modifier = { name = savoring_the_end_days duration = -1 }

		event_target:bored_courtier_target = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_took_my_advice who = ROOT }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = seclusion.72 days = 30 random = 10 } }
		trigger = { NOR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 } }
	}

	option = { # lunatic option - yes
		name = EVTOPTEseclusion71
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTEseclusion71_TT }
		set_character_flag = masque_of_red_death

		add_character_modifier = { name = savoring_the_end_days duration = -1 }

		event_target:bored_courtier_target = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_took_my_advice who = ROOT }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = seclusion.72 days = 30 random = 10 } }

		trigger = { OR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 } }
	}

	option = { # lunatic option - no
		name = EVTOPTFseclusion71
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTFseclusion71_TT }

		trigger = { OR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCseclusion71 # I refuse to have fun while other suffer
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTCseclusion71_TT }
		trigger = {
			OR = { is_benevolent_trigger = yes is_pious_trigger = yes }
			NOR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 }
			NOT = { trait = depressed }
		}
	}

	# Opt out - I am boring
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDseclusion71
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTDseclusion71_TT }
		trigger = {
			OR = { AND = { is_benevolent_trigger = no is_pious_trigger = no } trait = depressed }
			NOR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 }
		}
	}
}

# seclusion.72 The revelry
# The castle is spruced up and the party goes on, or is called off.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler with masque_of_red_death. Mean time to happen.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.72
	picture = GFX_evt_castle_construction
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	only_rulers = yes

	trigger = {
		has_character_flag = masque_of_red_death
		OR = {
			event_target:bored_courtier_target = { is_alive = yes }
			any_courtier = {
				is_adult = yes
				prisoner = no
				is_incapable = no
				has_vice_trigger = yes
				has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
			}
			any_courtier = {
				is_close_relative = yes
				is_adult = yes
				prisoner = no
				is_incapable = no
				is_pious_trigger = no
				has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
			}
		}
	}

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseclusion72_A # we've spruced up the castle
		trigger = { NOR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseclusion72_B # we've spruced up the castle - lunatic version
		trigger = { OR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 } }
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:bored_courtier_target = { is_alive = no } } # fetch new event_target if old one is dead (preferably a relative)
			random_courtier = {
				limit = {
					is_adult = yes
					prisoner = no
					is_incapable = no
					has_vice_trigger = yes
					has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
				}
				save_event_target_as = bored_courtier_target
			}
			random_courtier = {
				limit = {
					is_close_relative = yes
					is_adult = yes
					prisoner = no
					is_incapable = no
					is_pious_trigger = no
					has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
				}
				save_event_target_as = bored_courtier_target
			}
			event_target:bored_courtier_target = {
				add_character_modifier = { name = bright_eyed duration = -1 }
			}
		}
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Can you do something about the lights?
		name = EVTOPTAseclusion72
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAseclusion72_TT }

		scaled_wealth = -1
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = seclusion.73 days = 30 random = 15 } }

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = charitable } }
			random = { chance = 30 add_trait_charitable_effect = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = savoring_the_end_days }
			remove_character_modifier = savoring_the_end_days
		}
		add_character_modifier = { name = secluded_revelry duration = -1 }
		event_target:bored_courtier_target = {
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_budding_friendship }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			opinion = {
				who = event_target:bored_courtier_target
				modifier = opinion_budding_friendship
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseclusion72 # you've made life in seclusion bearable!
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBseclusion72_TT }

		event_target:bored_courtier_target = {
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_budding_friendship }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			opinion = {
				who = event_target:bored_courtier_target
				modifier = opinion_budding_friendship
			}
		}

		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = seclusion.73 days = 30 random = 15 } }
	}

	# Perhaps enough is enough - CHAIN END.
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCseclusion72
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTCseclusion72_TT }
		clr_character_flag = masque_of_red_death
	}
}

# seclusion.73 Strangers among us
# Hidden. Creates the masked stranger.
# Scope: ROOT is the party host. Fired by seclusion.72.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.73
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_character_flag = masque_of_red_death
	}

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			10 = {
				create_character = {
					female = no
					age = 21
					random_traits = no
					attributes = { intrigue = 15 martial = 10 diplomacy = 9 }
					dynasty = none
					trait = elusive_shadow
					trait = arbitrary
					trait = patient
					religion = ROOT
					culture = ROOT
					race = ROOT
					health = 7
				}
				new_character = {
					# Set Racial Trait
					character_event = { id = aiclean.31 }
					will_not_marry_effect = yes
					add_character_modifier = { name = in_seclusion duration = -1 }
					add_character_modifier = { name = attending_masquerade duration = -1 }
					set_character_flag = immune_bubonic_plague
					save_event_target_as = partying_stranger
				}
			}
			10 = {
				create_character = {
					female = yes
					age = 21
					random_traits = no
					attributes = { intrigue = 15 martial = 10 diplomacy = 9 }
					dynasty = none
					trait = elusive_shadow
					trait = arbitrary
					trait = patient
					religion = ROOT
					culture = ROOT
					race = ROOT
					health = 7
				}
				new_character = {
					# Set Racial Trait
					character_event = { id = aiclean.31 }
					will_not_marry_effect = yes
					add_character_modifier = { name = in_seclusion duration = -1 }
					add_character_modifier = { name = attending_masquerade duration = -1 }
					set_character_flag = immune_bubonic_plague
					save_event_target_as = partying_stranger
				}
			}
		}
		event_target:secluded_party_host = { character_event = { id = seclusion.75 } }
	}
}

# seclusion.75 Who is that?
# A masked stranger the ruler never invited is at the party.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler with masque_of_red_death. Fired by seclusion.73.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.75
	picture = GFX_evt_suspicious_noble
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	trigger = {
		has_character_flag = masque_of_red_death
	}

	hide_new = yes
	hide_from = yes

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseclusion75_A
		trigger = {
			has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
			NOR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseclusion75_B # if you left seclusion
		picture = GFX_evt_prospering_province
		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseclusion75_C # lunatic
		trigger = {
			has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
			OR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 }
		}
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseclusion75 # who is that?
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAseclusion75_TT }

		event_target:partying_stranger = {
			set_character_flag = marking_flag #to make char show up in option
		}

		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = seclusion.76 days = 15 } }

		trigger = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion }
	}

	option = { # isn't there something off about them?
		name = EVTOPTBseclusion75
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBseclusion75_TT }

		event_target:partying_stranger = {
			set_character_flag = marking_flag #to make char show up in option
		}

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = cynical } }
			random = {
				chance = 50
				add_trait_cynical_effect = yes
				remove_trait_zealous_effect = yes
			}
		}

		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = seclusion.76 days = 15 } }

		trigger = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion }
	}

	# It was fun while it lasted. But who's that? -  if you have left seclusion
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCseclusion75
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = seclusion.77 days = 15 } }

		event_target:partying_stranger = {
			set_character_flag = marking_flag #to make char show up in option
		}

		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion } }
	}
}

# seclusion.76 Approaching the stranger
# The ruler decides how to face the masked guest.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by seclusion.75.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.76
	picture = GFX_evt_suspicious_noble
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	only_rulers = yes

	trigger = {
		has_character_flag = masque_of_red_death
	}

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseclusion76_A # regular desc
		trigger = { NOR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseclusion76_B # lunatic desc
		trigger = { OR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 } }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseclusion76 # be polite about it
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAseclusion76_TT }
		event_target:partying_stranger = {
			set_character_flag = optA
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = seclusion.83 days = 10 random = 5 } }
		}
		trigger = { NOR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 } }
	}

	option = { # demand to know who they are
		name = EVTOPTBseclusion76
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBseclusion76_TT }
		event_target:partying_stranger = {
			set_character_flag = optB
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = seclusion.83 days = 10 random = 5 } }
		}
		trigger = { NOR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 } }
	}

	# special option - IMMORTAL - see through them!
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCseclusion76
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTCseclusion76_TT }
		tooltip_info = immortal
		event_target:partying_stranger = {
			set_character_flag = optC
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = seclusion.83 days = 10 random = 5 } }
		}
		trigger = { trait = immortal }
	}
	# special option - INTRIGUE - see through them!
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDseclusion76
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTDseclusion76_TT }
		tooltip_info = intrigue
		event_target:partying_stranger = {
			set_character_flag = optC
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = seclusion.83 days = 10 random = 5 } }
		}
		trigger = {
			intrigue = 15
			NOT = { trait = immortal }
			NOR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 }
		}
	}
	# special option - LUNATIC
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTEseclusion76
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTEseclusion76_TT }
		event_target:partying_stranger = {
			set_character_flag = optC
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = seclusion.83 days = 10 random = 5 } }
		}
		set_character_flag = picked_lunatic_opt
		trigger = { OR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 } }
	}
}

# seclusion.83 Masque ping
# Hidden. Routes the ruler's choice to the right meeting (78-80).
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by seclusion.76.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.83
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = optA }
			event_target:secluded_party_host = { character_event = { id = seclusion.78 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = optB }
			event_target:secluded_party_host = { character_event = { id = seclusion.79 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = optC }
			event_target:secluded_party_host = { character_event = { id = seclusion.80 } }
		}
		clr_character_flag = optA
		clr_character_flag = optB
		clr_character_flag = optC
	}
}

# seclusion.77 The stranger disappears
# The guest is gone, leaving only a stinking mask.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by seclusion.75.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.77
	picture = GFX_evt_prospering_province
	desc = EVTDESCseclusion77
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	only_rulers = yes
	hide_from = yes

	trigger = {
		has_character_flag = masque_of_red_death
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_tooltip = { # Death leaves court and becomes random person
			event_target:partying_stranger = {
				leave_court_effect = yes
				remove_character_modifier = attending_masquerade
				remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion
				remove_character_modifier = will_not_marry
				remove_trait = cannot_marry
				clr_character_flag = marking_flag
				clr_character_flag = immune_bubonic_plague
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseclusion77 # just as well?
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAseclusion77_TT }

		add_character_modifier = { name = lucky_to_be_alive duration = 365 }

		trigger = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion }
	}
}

				custom_tooltip = { text = seclusion78_TT_bad_outcome }

		}
	}
}

		}
		trigger = { trait = immortal NOT = { has_character_flag = picked_lunatic_opt } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBseclusion80 # That's not good
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBseclusion80_TT }

				modifier = { factor = 1.1 intrigue = 18 }

			}
			20 = {
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = slow }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_mental_disorder_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = blinded }
				modifier = { factor = 2 has_severe_disability_trigger = yes }
				custom_tooltip = { text = seclusion78_TT_bad_outcome }
				set_character_flag = bad_outcome_D
				character_event = { id = seclusion.81 }
			}
		}
		trigger = {
			intrigue = 15
			NOT = { trait = immortal }
			NOT = { has_character_flag = picked_lunatic_opt }
		}
	}

	option = {
