# seclusion.82 The herald leaves
# The ruler drives the herald off, tricks it, or strikes a deal.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by seclusion.78-80.
narrative_event = {
	id = seclusion.82
	title = EVTNAMEseclusion82
	picture = GFX_evt_danse_macabre
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_religion

	trigger = {
		has_character_flag = masque_of_red_death
	}

	desc = { text = EVTDESCseclusion82_A trigger = { has_character_flag = good_outcome_A } }
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCseclusion82_B #
		picture = GFX_evt_prospering_province
		trigger = { has_character_flag = good_outcome_B }
	}
	desc = { text = EVTDESCseclusion82_C trigger = { has_character_flag = good_outcome_C } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCseclusion82_D trigger = { has_character_flag = good_outcome_D } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCseclusion82_E trigger = { has_character_flag = good_outcome_E } }

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAseclusion82 # yay
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAseclusion82_TT }

		add_character_modifier = { name = lucky_to_be_alive duration = 1825 }

		hidden_effect = { if = { limit = { has_nickname = no } give_nickname = nick_the_survivor } }

		hidden_tooltip = { # Death leaves court and becomes random person
			event_target:partying_stranger = {
				leave_court_effect = yes
				remove_character_modifier = attending_masquerade
				remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion
				remove_character_modifier = will_not_marry
				remove_trait = cannot_marry
				clr_character_flag = marking_flag
				clr_character_flag = immune_bubonic_plague
			}
		}

		clr_character_flag = good_outcome_A
		clr_character_flag = good_outcome_B

		trigger = {
			OR = { has_character_flag = good_outcome_A has_character_flag = good_outcome_B }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCseclusion82 # yay
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTCseclusion82_TT }

		add_character_modifier = { name = seizing_the_day duration = 1825 }

		hidden_effect = { if = { limit = { has_nickname = no } give_nickname = nick_the_survivor } }

		hidden_tooltip = { # Death leaves court and becomes random person
			event_target:partying_stranger = {
				leave_court_effect = yes
				remove_character_modifier = attending_masquerade
				remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion
				remove_character_modifier = will_not_marry
				remove_trait = cannot_marry
				clr_character_flag = marking_flag
				clr_character_flag = immune_bubonic_plague
			}
		}

		hidden_effect = { give_nickname = nick_the_survivor }

		clr_character_flag = good_outcome_C

		trigger = { has_character_flag = good_outcome_C }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDseclusion82 # yay
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTDseclusion82_TT }

		add_character_modifier = { name = lucky_to_be_alive duration = 1825 }

		hidden_effect = { if = { limit = { has_nickname = no } give_nickname = nick_the_survivor } }

		hidden_tooltip = { # Death leaves court and becomes random person
			event_target:partying_stranger = {
				leave_court_effect = yes
				remove_character_modifier = attending_masquerade
				remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion
				remove_character_modifier = will_not_marry
				remove_trait = cannot_marry
				clr_character_flag = marking_flag
				clr_character_flag = immune_bubonic_plague
			}
		}

		clr_character_flag = good_outcome_D

		trigger = { has_character_flag = good_outcome_D }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTEseclusion82 # yay
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTEseclusion82_TT }

		add_character_modifier = { name = lucky_to_be_alive duration = 1825 }

		hidden_effect = { if = { limit = { has_nickname = no } give_nickname = nick_the_lucky } }

		hidden_tooltip = { # Death leaves court and becomes random person
			event_target:partying_stranger = {
				leave_court_effect = yes
				remove_character_modifier = attending_masquerade
				remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion
				remove_character_modifier = will_not_marry
				remove_trait = cannot_marry
				clr_character_flag = marking_flag
				clr_character_flag = immune_bubonic_plague
			}
		}

		clr_character_flag = good_outcome_E
		clr_character_flag = picked_lunatic_opt

		trigger = { has_character_flag = good_outcome_E }
	}
}

# seclusion.100 The seclusion list
# Hidden. About every nine months rolls one seclusion event (food, tunnels,
# lovers, corpses, nightmares, the masque...) and schedules itself again. When
# the ruler has left seclusion it stops and frees anyone left behind.
# Scope: ROOT is the secluded ruler. Fired by seclusion_list_start_effect and
# itself.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.100

	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes # seclusion_list_loop_effect, then itself

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion }
			random_list = {
				20 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						NOR = {
							host = {
								any_courtier = {
									has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
									has_epidemic_disease_trigger = yes
								}
							}
							has_character_flag = exposed_to_outside
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						host = {
							any_courtier = {
								has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
								developing_epidemic_disease_trigger = yes
							}
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						NAND = {
							location = { has_disease = yes }
							NOR = { num_of_symptoms = 1 is_ill = yes }
						}
					}
					give_epidemic_disease_effect = yes
				}
				80 = { }
			}
			random_list = {
				# Commoners pile corpses outside gates
				20 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						NAND = {
							host = { character = ROOT }
							NOT = { has_character_flag = seclusion_piling_corpses }
							location = {
								OR = {
									disease = nurgles_rot
									disease = creeping_buboes
									disease = typhus
									disease = small_pox
									disease = bubonic_plague
								}
							}
						}
					}
					character_event = { id = seclusion.4 }
				}
				# Courtier has symptoms in seclusion
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						NAND = {
							host = { character = ROOT }
							location = { has_disease = yes }
							any_courtier = {
								prisoner = no
								has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
								num_of_symptoms = 1
							}
						}
					}
					character_event = { id = seclusion.5 }
				}
				# Lovers discovered in seclusion
				20 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						NAND = {
							host = { character = ROOT }
							any_courtier = {
								ai = yes
								at_location = ROOT
								has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
								any_lover = {
									ai = yes
									NOT = { character = ROOT }
									has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
									at_location = ROOT
								}
							}
						}
					}
					character_event = { id = seclusion.6 }
				}
				# Other courtier in seclusion is kind of beautiful
				20 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						NAND = {
							NOT = { has_character_flag = seclusion_lazy_seduction }
							NOT = { trait = celibate }
							can_marry = yes
							is_married = no
							is_consort = no
							has_lover = no
							is_adult = yes
							is_no_females_race = no
							any_realm_character = {
								is_adult = yes
								is_opposite_sex = ROOT
								reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 0 }
								at_location = ROOT
								has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
								is_married = no
								is_consort = no
								has_lover = no
								can_marry = yes
							}
						}
					}
					character_event = { id = seclusion.8 }
				}
				# Nightmares
				25 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						NAND = {
							NOT = { has_character_flag = seclusion_nightmares }
							host = { character = ROOT }
							capital_scope = { has_disease = yes }
						}
					}
					character_event = { id = seclusion.12 }
				}
				# Gain a friend
				20 = {
					trigger = {
						NOT = { has_character_flag = seclusion_gained_friend }
						any_courtier = {
							at_location = ROOT
							has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
							NOT = { is_friend = ROOT }
							NOT = { is_rival = ROOT }
							opinion = { who = ROOT value = 0 }
							OR = {
								AND = {
									age = 6
									NOT = { age = 16 }
									ROOT = { age = 6 NOT = { age = 16 } }
								}
								AND = { age = 16 ROOT = { age = 16 } }
							}
						}
					}
					character_event = { id = seclusion.13 }
				}
				# Courtier has the plague
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						NAND = {
							host = { character = ROOT }
							location = {
								OR = {
									disease = nurgles_rot
									disease = creeping_buboes
									disease = typhus
									disease = small_pox
									disease = bubonic_plague
								}
							}
							any_courtier = {
								at_location = ROOT
								has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
								NOT = { is_friend = ROOT }
								has_epidemic_disease_trigger = yes
							}
						}
					}
					character_event = { id = seclusion.14 }
				}
				# Child has a peasant lover outside of seclusion
				20 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						NAND = {
							NOT = { has_character_flag = seclusion_child_has_lover }
							any_child = {
								age = 16
								NOT = { trait = craven }
								NOT = { trait = celibate }
								at_location = ROOT
								has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
							}
						}
					}
					character_event = { id = seclusion.31 }
				}
				# Food shortage event chain start
				25 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						NAND = {
							host = { character = ROOT }
							NOT = { has_character_flag = seclusion_food_events }
							NOT = { has_character_flag = seclusion_events_on }
							is_adult = yes
						}
					}
					character_event = { id = seclusion.110 }
				}
				# Tunnel steward
				20 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						NAND = {
							age = 16
							host = { character = ROOT }
							job_treasurer = { always = yes ai = yes }

							capital_holding = { holding_type = castle }
							NOT = { has_character_flag = seclusion_tunnel }
							NOT = { has_character_flag = seclusion_events_on }
						}
					}
					character_event = { id = seclusion.51 }
				}
				10 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						OR = {
							# has_game_rule = {
							# name = supernatural_events
							# value = off
							# }
							ai = yes
							war = yes
							NOT = {
								any_realm_province = {
									OR = {
										disease = typhus
										disease = nurgles_rot
										disease = creeping_buboes
										disease = bubonic_plague
									} # the Red Pox or a Nurgle plague
								}
							}
							has_character_flag = masque_of_red_death
							has_character_flag = seclusion_events_on
							has_character_modifier = lucky_to_be_alive
							has_character_modifier = seizing_the_day
							has_character_modifier = savoring_the_end_days
							has_character_modifier = secluded_revelry
							prisoner = yes
							is_adult = no
							NOT = { current_heir = { dynasty = ROOT } }
							NOR = {
								any_courtier = {
									is_adult = yes
									is_incapable = no
									has_vice_trigger = yes
									prisoner = no
									has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
								}
								any_courtier = {
									is_close_relative = yes
									is_adult = yes
									prisoner = no
									is_incapable = no
									is_pious_trigger = no
									has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
								}
							}
						}
					}
					narrative_event = { id = seclusion.71 }
				}
				40 = { }
			}

			seclusion_list_next_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			clr_character_flag = seclusion_list_running
			any_courtier = { # anyone left behind in seclusion
				limit = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion }
				clr_character_flag = seclusion_events_on
				clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb
				remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion
			}
		}
	}
}

# seclusion.98 Imprisoned in seclusion
# Hidden. Being thrown in prison ends seclusion and its modifiers.
# Scope: ROOT is the prisoner. Fired from on_become_imprisoned_any_reason.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.98
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
	}

	immediate = {
		clr_character_flag = seclusion_events_on
		clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb
		remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = { # Still has some left over modifiers...
					has_character_modifier = tiny_food_shortage
					has_character_modifier = minor_food_shortage
					has_character_modifier = normal_food_shortage
					has_character_modifier = starving_food_shortage
				}
			}
			remove_character_modifier = tiny_food_shortage
			remove_character_modifier = minor_food_shortage
			remove_character_modifier = normal_food_shortage
			remove_character_modifier = starving_food_shortage
		}

		if = {
			limit = { is_ruler = yes }
			clr_character_flag = exposed_to_outside
			clr_character_flag = seclusion_piling_corpses
			clr_character_flag = seclusion_lazy_seduction
			clr_character_flag = seclusion_nightmares
			clr_character_flag = seclusion_gained_friend
			clr_character_flag = seclusion_child_has_lover
			clr_character_flag = seclusion_tunnel
			clr_character_flag = seclusion_food_events

			any_courtier = {
				limit = {
					OR = { # Still has some left over modifiers...
						has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
						has_character_modifier = tiny_food_shortage
						has_character_modifier = minor_food_shortage
						has_character_modifier = normal_food_shortage
						has_character_modifier = starving_food_shortage
					}
				}
				remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion
				remove_character_modifier = tiny_food_shortage
				remove_character_modifier = minor_food_shortage
				remove_character_modifier = normal_food_shortage
				remove_character_modifier = starving_food_shortage
			}
		}
	}
}

# seclusion.99 Left behind in seclusion
# Hidden. Ends seclusion for a new title holder whose host is no longer
# secluded.
# Scope: ROOT is the new holder. Fired from on_new_holder.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.99

	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_new_holder
	only_rulers = yes

	trigger = {
		host = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion } }
		has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
		NAND = { is_landed = yes capital_scope = { has_epidemic = yes } }
	}

	immediate = {
		clr_character_flag = seclusion_events_on
		clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb
		remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion
	}
}

# seclusion.102 A councillor in seclusion
# A councillor tells the liege they have shut their gates and cannot serve.
# Scope: ROOT is the liege, FROM the councillor. Fired by the shut_the_gates
# decision.
character_event = {
	id = seclusion.102
	picture = GFX_evt_council

	is_triggered_only = yes
	notification = yes

	desc = EVTDESCseclusion100_NOTE

	#	desc = {
	#		trigger = { NOT = { reverse_opinion = { who = FROM value = 0 } } }
	#		text = EVTDESCseclusion100_A
	#	}
	#	desc = {
	#		trigger = { reverse_opinion = { who = FROM value = 0 } }
	#		text = EVTDESCseclusion100_B
	#	}

	option = {
		name = {
			trigger = { has_unpleasant_personality_trigger = yes }
			text = EVTOPTAseclusion100_A
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { has_unpleasant_personality_trigger = no }
			text = EVTOPTAseclusion100_B
		}
	}
}
